# France Statutory Integrations Research (SIR) - FR-SIR-INDEX-001 v1.0

The complete France statutory-integration research: the SIR index, the full integration-landscape discovery record (5 authorities assessed, 3 with a machine-to-machine channel, 15 in-scope filings, 29 sources), and the complete 7-phase implementation runbooks for NETENT-DSN, URSSAF-DPAE and DGFIP-EDI - portals, channels, authentication, enrolment, transport, payload formats, inbound returns, conformance and run/operate.

# Part 1 - Statutory Integration Register index (FR-SIR-INDEX-001 v1.0)

France Statutory Integration Index — FR-SIR-INDEX-001 v1.0
Country: France (FR)  |  Currency: EUR  |  Year: 2026  |  Verdict: Has Integrations
Source: integration-landscape.json (schema integration-landscape/1.0, generated 2026-08-27). 5 authorities assessed — 3 with a system-to-system integration, 2 portal or manual only, across 3 distinct technical channels.

### Authority index


| Authority | Integration | Channel | Direction(s) | Developer portal | Configuration Runbook location |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NETENT-DSN<br>GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr - Declaration Sociale Nominative deposit point and returns service | Yes | REST API (API DSN, machine-to-machine) | outbound, inbound | https://www.net-entreprises.fr/declaration/api-dsn/ | 03-statutory-integrations/NETENT-DSN/ |
| URSSAF-DPAE<br>URSSAF - Declaration Prealable A l'Embauche deposit point | Yes | REST API (API DPAE, machine-to-machine) | outbound, inbound | https://www.data.gouv.fr/dataservices/api-declaration-prealable-a-lembauche | 03-statutory-integrations/URSSAF-DPAE/ |
| DGFIP-EDI<br>DGFiP - taxe sur les salaires (forms 2501-SD and 2502-SD) via the EDI channel | Yes | EDIFACT file exchange through a DGFiP-accredited partenaire EDI (EDI-Paiement and EDI-TDFC) | outbound | https://www.impots.gouv.fr/partenaires-edi-0 | 03-statutory-integrations/DGFIP-EDI/ |
| CNAM-DIRECT<br>Assurance Maladie / CPAM - flows that do NOT travel in the DSN (work-accident declaration, daily-benefit payment statements) | No | net-entreprises.fr portal only - online form (EFI) or in-browser structured-file upload (EDI mode) outbound; portal download inbound | outbound, inbound | None published — no machine-to-machine developer documentation exists | Not applicable — no runbook (manual / portal-only authority) |
| CDC-SOLTEA<br>Caisse des Depots - SOLTeA platform (allocation of the apprenticeship tax balance) | No | Web portal only (soltea.gouv.fr); an in-browser CSV upload exists for bulk SIRET and habilitation administration, not for the allocation itself | outbound | None published — no machine-to-machine developer documentation exists | Not applicable — no runbook (manual / portal-only authority) |

Per-authority Configuration Runbooks are produced only for the authorities marked Integration = Yes, and each is written to its own folder at 03-statutory-integrations/<AUTH_CODE>/ as named in the last column above. The authorities marked Integration = No are reached through a human-operated web portal with no machine-to-machine channel and no published developer documentation; they need no runbook, and their filings are discharged manually. Excluding them is a statement about the channel, not about the obligation — the filings themselves remain in scope and are specified in the Report Specifications.

# Part 2 - Integration landscape (full discovery record)

| Key | Value |
|---|---|
| `schema` | integration-landscape/1.0 |
| `artifact` | SIR-DISCOVERY |
| `country` | France |
| `code` | FR |
| `currency` | EUR |
| `year` | 2026 |
| `generated` | 2026-08-27 |
| `generated_by` | si-discovery |
| `scope_version` | 1.0 |
| `verdict` | HAS_INTEGRATIONS |
| `verdict_rationale` | France operates a genuine, publicly documented machine-to-machine channel for the flow that carries the overwhelming majority of its payroll statutory data. The Declaration Sociale Nominative - which alone discharges the URSSAF, CNAV, CPAM, AGIRC-ARRCO, France Travail and DGFiP withholding-at-source obligations, plus the annual Agefiph and apprenticeship-tax-balance declarations - is deposited through the API DSN, a REST machine-to-machine interface published by GIP-MDS, and every organisation return (compte rendu metier) including the DGFiP withholding rate and the CARSAT AT/MP net rate is retrievable through the same API. A second independent REST API, the URSSAF API DPAE, carries the pre-hire declaration. A third channel, DGFiP EDI, carries the payroll tax. Three of the five in-scope receiving channels are system-to-system; two are portal-only and are marked as such. The verdict is NOT MANUAL_ONLY. |
| `modelling_note` | France's landscape is channel-concentrated, not authority-concentrated: a single deposit point (net-entreprises.fr, operated by GIP-MDS) serves at least six distinct statutory authorities through one file and one API. Entries below are therefore keyed to the body that OWNS the technical channel, and each entry names every authority it serves in receiving_bodies. URSSAF, CNAV, CPAM, AGIRC-ARRCO, France Travail, CARSAT/CNAM-RP, Agefiph and DGFiP all appear - as channel owners where they own one, and as receiving_bodies where they do not. Modelling one entry per authority would have produced six near-identical DSN runbooks and would have misrepresented the build as six integrations rather than one. |
| `schema_note` | The si-discovery agent definition at .claude/agents/si-discovery.md is NOT installed in this repository - shared/si-runbook was never deployed - so the field set could not be read from it. This file is written to the schema the v2.9 pipeline actually CONSUMES: mercans_pipeline_v2_9.sh lines 1535-1592 read top-level 'verdict' and 'authorities[]', and within each authority 'code' (used verbatim as a directory name) and 'integration' (boolean, strict is-True test); the sir_index prompt at lines 1563-1569 additionally requires channel, direction(s) and dev portal per authority. Those six fields carry exactly those names and types here. All other fields are additive and are ignored by the consumer. Flagged so that a later reader does not mistake this for a schema that the agent definition validated. |

### authorities (5 items)


#### 1. NETENT-DSN

| Key | Value |
|---|---|
| `code` | NETENT-DSN |
| `name` | GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr - Declaration Sociale Nominative deposit point and returns service |
| `integration` | true |
| `channel` | REST API (API DSN, machine-to-machine) |
| `channel_type` | api_rest |
| `dev_portal` | https://www.net-entreprises.fr/declaration/api-dsn/ |
| `authority_role` | Groupement d'interet public mandated to operate the single deposit point for social declarations. Not itself a levying authority; it routes to the bodies listed in receiving_bodies. |
| `authentication` | Declarant credentials held on net-entreprises.fr: SIRET + surname + first name + password (the same triplet used for portal login). A digital certificate from a net-entreprises-approved certification authority is accepted for connection. A concentrateur - a payroll provider filing for many clients - authenticates through net-entreprises.fr, and the same API covers the agricultural regime deposit point on msa.fr. NOTE: no OAuth2 client-credentials flow was found in the public documentation; the API reuses the declarant's portal identity, which is a real operational constraint for a service provider (credential custody per client). |
| `test_environment` | A conformity/test path is referenced on the API DSN page but its enrolment terms were not established from public pages. UNVERIFIED - not a claim of absence. |
| `evidence` | net-entreprises.fr states the API DSN is the interface that lets the declarant's payroll software - or a concentrateur - authenticate directly on the deposit point without navigating the portal, and that it also returns every acknowledgement and compte rendu metier issued by the recipient bodies. The knowledge-base article describes a four-call REST sequence: authenticate, deposit, consult flow status, download returns. NEOReS states its own object is a fully automatable industrial return system consumable by payroll solutions via API. The AT/MP net rate is delivered as CRM 34 and net-entreprises states explicitly that it can be retrieved by API so that payroll is updated with it automatically. |
| `confidence` | high |


##### directions (2 items)

- outbound
- inbound


##### receiving_bodies (9 items)

- URSSAF (recouvrement, general regime contributions)
- CNAV / old-age insurance (identification and career data)
- CPAM / Assurance Maladie (daily benefit entitlement, work-stoppage signals)
- AGIRC-ARRCO institutions (supplementary pension)
- France Travail (end-of-contract signal, unemployment entitlement)
- DGFiP (withholding at source - outbound declaration and inbound rate)
- CARSAT / CNAM-Risques professionnels (AT/MP net rate, via CRM 34)
- Agefiph, through URSSAF (disabled-worker employment return, in the April DSN)
- Organismes complementaires - supplementary health and death-and-disability insurers (fiches de parametrage)

- FR-DSN-001
- FR-DSN-002

- FR-AGIRC-001


##### operations (5 items)

- authentication service (declarant or concentrateur)
- DSN deposit (depot) - monthly declaration and event signals
- flow status consultation (per flow, per period, per concentrateur)
- return download - AEE (accuse d'enregistrement electronique), certificat de conformite, and all organisation comptes rendus metiers
- retrieval of fiches de parametrage for organismes complementaires

##### known_constraints (3 items)

- The AT/MP rate delivered in CRM 34 is the rate calculated by the regional fund WITHOUT reduction cases applied; where an employee is on a reduced rate the employer must derive the applicable rate from the transmitted one. CRM 34 is not a directly postable rate for every employee.
- The DGFiP withholding rate arrives only through this return flow. There is no separate DGFiP rate API. Loss of the return leg is a tax failure, not merely a data gap.
- The compte AT/MP itself - the dematerialised rate notification and its calculation detail - remains a portal service; only the CRM 34 rate value is machine-retrievable.


#### 2. URSSAF-DPAE

| Key | Value |

| `code` | URSSAF-DPAE |
| `name` | URSSAF - Declaration Prealable A l'Embauche deposit point |
| `integration` | true |

| `channel_type` | api_rest |
| `dev_portal` | https://www.data.gouv.fr/dataservices/api-declaration-prealable-a-lembauche |
| `authority_role` | URSSAF is the receiving authority in its own right for the pre-hire declaration. |
| `authentication` | Restricted-access API; access is granted by the producer. Public documentation gives the deposit base URL and a producer contact address but no self-service enrolment page. Treat enrolment as a gated step requiring contact with URSSAF. |
| `test_environment` | Not established from public sources. UNVERIFIED. |
| `evidence` | URSSAF publishes the API DPAE as a machine-to-machine offer letting the DPAE-producing software authenticate directly on the URSSAF deposit point and transmit a very large number of declarations, receiving electronic registration acknowledgements, conformity certificates and the compte rendu metier. data.gouv.fr lists it as a REST API produced by URSSAF with restricted access. URSSAF states the DPAE offer converges on the DSN offer, which eases implementation for editors that already run the API DSN. |
| `confidence` | high |

###### 4. URSSAF - DPAE, realiser la declaration prealable a l'embauche

| Key | Value |

| `public` | true |


##### known_constraints (3 items)

- This is a SEPARATE channel from the DSN in 2026. The technical specification provides for the DPAE to become DSN event signal type 10, but production was deferred and is not expected before 1 January 2027. A build must not fold it into the DSN.
- Two legacy channels are closed and must not be designed for: e-mail transmission ended 1 July 2022, and the CFT flow ended at the close of 2022.
- A portal alternative exists - online form, or manual file upload on net-entreprises.fr - if API enrolment is not obtained.


#### 3. DGFIP-EDI

| Key | Value |
|---|---|
| `code` | DGFIP-EDI |
| `name` | DGFiP - taxe sur les salaires (forms 2501-SD and 2502-SD) via the EDI channel |
| `integration` | true |
| `channel` | EDIFACT file exchange through a DGFiP-accredited partenaire EDI (EDI-Paiement and EDI-TDFC) |
| `channel_type` | edi_via_accredited_intermediary |
| `dev_portal` | https://www.impots.gouv.fr/partenaires-edi-0 |
| `authority_role` | DGFiP is the receiving authority. This entry covers ONLY the payroll tax. DGFiP's withholding-at-source obligation travels inside the DSN and is covered by NETENT-DSN. |
| `authentication` | INDIRECT BY DESIGN. DGFiP accepts EDI files only from a partenaire EDI it has itself accredited; no other party may transmit. No adhesion formality is required of the EMPLOYER - access to the EDI teleprocedure takes effect as soon as an accredited partner files on its account. Mercans must therefore either obtain DGFiP partenaire EDI accreditation or contract an accredited partner. This is a commercial and accreditation dependency, not a technical one, and it is the single largest unknown in this entry. |
| `test_environment` | Partner-side homologation exists as part of DGFiP accreditation; terms are not public. UNVERIFIED. |
| `evidence` | impots.gouv.fr states that provisional payments and annual returns for the taxe sur les salaires must be transmitted and paid by dematerialised means, either from the espace professionnel or through an EDI partner, and that only accredited EDI partners may send files to DGFiP for EDI-TVA, TDFC, EDI-Paiement (covering IS, TS, RCM, CVAE) and the other EDI procedures. Electronic filing is compulsory; the regulatory reference given by DGFiP is Article 369 of Annex III to the General Tax Code, and the general electronic-filing obligation sits at CGI Article 1649 quater B quater. |
| `confidence` | medium |
| `confidence_note` | The channel's existence, its compulsory dematerialisation and the accredited-partner monopoly are firmly sourced. What is NOT established is the 2026 edition of the EDI-Paiement cahier des charges and the practical accreditation path for a foreign payroll provider. Both are runbook-stage questions. |

| `public` | true |


##### known_constraints (3 items)

- CONDITIONAL FILING. The taxe sur les salaires is due only from employers not liable to VAT on at least 90 percent of turnover. For a client outside that population the filing must be SUPPRESSED, not filed nil. An integration that always files is wrong.
- The espace professionnel (EFI) at impots.gouv.fr is the portal fallback and is a fully lawful channel. If partner accreditation is not obtained, this authority degrades to portal-only with no compliance breach.
- Filing rhythm varies by amount - annual below EUR 4,000; quarterly between EUR 4,000 and 10,000; monthly above EUR 10,000, each with a closing 2502-SD - so the integration must be schedule-driven, not fixed-monthly.

| `confidence` | high |

##### known_constraints (2 items)

- The DAT structured-file mode materially reduces keying effort and should be used, but it is a browser upload. Do NOT specify it as an API. Calling it an integration would misstate both the operating model and the run cost.
- BPIJ XML is machine-READABLE but not machine-RETRIEVABLE. A human must download it. This is the reconciliation flow for subrogation, so the manual step recurs every pay period.

##### known_constraints (2 items)

- The allocation is a CLIENT BUSINESS DECISION, not a payroll computation. Even if an API existed, payroll holds no data to send beyond the amount already declared in the DSN. Building an integration here would automate a decision that belongs to the client.
- A default distribution rule applies where the employer makes no allocation, so a missed window is not a compliance breach.

### method

| Key | Value |
|---|---|
| `approach` | Every in-scope filing in FR-compliance/scope_manifest.json was traced to its receiving authority, then each authority's own publication - net-entreprises.fr, urssaf.fr, impots.gouv.fr, soltea.gouv.fr, caissedesdepots.fr, data.gouv.fr - was searched for a system-to-system channel. A channel was accepted as an integration ONLY where the authority itself describes unattended machine-to-machine operation. Browser file upload was classified as portal, not integration. |
| `date_of_research` | 2026-08-27 |
| `no_live_endpoints_touched` | true |
| `no_authentication_attempted` | true |
| `runbooks_written` | false |


### limitations (4 items)

- Three published documents could not be retrieved from this environment: the URSSAF API-DPAE-Fonctionnement PDF (connection reset), the DPAE EDI download site telechargement.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr (connection refused), and the SOLTeA employer user guide (retrieved but image-only, no extractable text). Their URLs are recorded above. These are retrieval failures in this environment, not evidence that the documents are absent or the channels non-existent.
- The API DSN and API DPAE implementation guides were identified and their URLs recorded, but their contents were not parsed. Every field-level, endpoint-level and error-handling claim is therefore deferred to the runbook stage. Nothing in this file asserts a request shape, an endpoint path or a return code.
- No test or homologation environment was confirmed for any of the three integrable channels. Marked UNVERIFIED rather than absent.
- Negative findings - SOLTeA, the DAT, the BPIJ - rest on systematic search of the operators' own publications. That is the strongest negative evidence obtainable without contacting the operators, and it is not proof of absence.

| **P5** | [`P5-inbound-returns-neores.md`](P5-inbound-returns-neores.md) | Return catalogue (~40 codes), acceptance chain, status vocabulary, the 4 returns that reach payroll, retention obligation | BUILD-READY · schema pack gated |
| **P6** | [`P6-test-conformance-cutover.md`](P6-test-conformance-cutover.md) | Environments, pre-flight gate, 12 connectivity + 24 functional tests, cutover, post-cutover watch | BUILD-READY · 1 gated, 1 unverified |
| **P7** | [`P7-run-operate.md`](P7-run-operate.md) | Calendar, job schedule, retry policy by class, alerting (and the do-not-alert set), SLA, archival, change management, monthly runbook | BUILD-READY |

##### Supporting

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| [`INTERLINKAGES.md`](INTERLINKAGES.md) | Cross-references to `URSSAF-DPAE` and `DGFIP-EDI`, and links into the rest of the FR dossier. **Only** file discussing other authorities. |
| [`effort-estimates.md`](effort-estimates.md) | 59 person-days engineering; 12–16 weeks elapsed, gated by enrolment |
| [`../../integration-sources/netent-dsn/_source-manifest.md`](../../integration-sources/netent-dsn/_source-manifest.md) | Every downloaded artefact with SHA-256, source URL, and what was **not** obtainable |

---

##### Read this first

Three things about this integration are counter-intuitive and cause most failures:

1. **The AEE is not acceptance.** Deposit returns HTTP 200 and an AEE (code 10) even when
   the file is structurally wrong. Acceptance is the **certificat de conformité (code 11)**.
   → P5 §5.3, P4 §4.6
2. **The inbound leg is statutorily mandatory.** The DGFiP withholding rate reaches payroll
   *only* as CRM 94. There is no rate API. No return leg = tax failure. → P1 §1.5, P5 §5.5
3. **Only unitary deposit is supported** — one `S20` block per call — and violating it
   fails *asynchronously*, days later. → P4 §4.6, P6 §6.3 assertion 2

---

| NEOReS — Cahier technique | **CT2026.1.0** | 2025-04-08 |
| NEOReS — note différentielle CT2024.1→CT2026.1 | — | — |

### NETENT-DSN / `P1-authority-channel-profile.md`

#### P1 — Authority & Channel Profile
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr — DSN deposit point and returns service**

Status: **BUILD-READY** · Mode: structural · Directions: outbound + inbound
Source basis: API guide v6.8 (2026-07-06), NEODeS CT2026.1.2, NEOReS CT2026.1.0
Scope: `NETENT-DSN` only. URSSAF-DPAE and DGFIP-EDI are cross-referenced in `INTERLINKAGES.md`.

---

##### 1.1 What this authority is

GIP-MDS (Groupement d'Intérêt Public Modernisation des Déclarations Sociales) operates
net-entreprises.fr, the **single deposit point** for French social declarations. It is a
routing and acknowledgement platform, **not a levying authority**. It accepts one monthly
payroll file per establishment, validates it, distributes it to the receiving bodies, and
returns every acknowledgement and business return those bodies produce.

This has a direct architectural consequence: **one outbound integration serves ten
statutory filings.** Building NETENT-DSN correctly discharges the majority of France's
payroll reporting obligation; failing to build the return leg silently breaks tax
withholding (see §1.5).

##### 1.2 Filings carried by this channel

Ten in-scope filings from `scope_manifest.json` ride this one integration:

| Filing ID | Direction | Frequency | Carried how |
|-----------|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| `FR-DSN-001` | outbound | monthly | The monthly DSN itself (NEODeS `S20.G00.05` type 01) |
| `FR-DSN-002` | outbound | per event, ≤5 days | Signalement fin de contrat de travail |
| `FR-DSN-003` | outbound | per event, ≤5 days | Signalement arrêt / reprise de travail |
| `FR-DGFIP-001` | outbound | monthly (quarterly option <11 employees) | Prélèvement à la source declared inside the monthly DSN |
| `FR-DGFIP-002` | **inbound** | monthly | Withholding-rate CRM (code 94) — **the only source of the rate** |
| `FR-URSSAF-002` | outbound | monthly | Aggregate contribution blocks `S21.G00.22/23` |
| `FR-URSSAF-003` | outbound | annual, in the April DSN | OETH / Agefiph disabled-worker return |
| `FR-URSSAF-004` | outbound | annual | Carried in the DSN contribution set |
| `FR-CARSAT-001` | **inbound** | annual + on reclassification | AT/MP net rate delivered as CRM 34 |
| `FR-AGIRC-001` | outbound + inbound | monthly | Supplementary pension data; CRM 71 returns |

**Monthly deadline (from the dossier's own scope determination):** the **5th** of the
following month for employers of 50 or more paying wages in the same month, the **15th**
otherwise.

##### 1.3 Receiving bodies behind the single deposit

URSSAF · CNAV · CPAM / Assurance Maladie · AGIRC-ARRCO institutions · France Travail ·
DGFiP · CARSAT / CNAM-Risques professionnels · Agefiph (through URSSAF) ·
organismes complémentaires (supplementary health and death-and-disability insurers).

Each produces its own return with its own code and its own status semantics. P5 carries
the full catalogue.

##### 1.4 Channel classification

| Property | Value | Source |
|----------|-------|--------|
| Channel type | REST over HTTPS, machine-to-machine | API guide §3 |
| Protocol | HTTP/1.1 | API guide §3 opening line |
| Style | Request/response, plus **hypermedia** for downloads (download URLs are returned by the search services, never constructed) | API guide §10.2 |
| Outbound payload | NEODeS fixed-structure text, **ISO-8859-1**, `Content-Type: text/plain` | API guide §3.2.1 |
| Inbound payload | XML — harmonised DSN "bilans" format or NEOReS, depending on return code | API guide §12 |
| Authentication | Portal credential triplet → base64 bearer token, `Authorization: DSNLogin` | API guide §2.3.1, §3.1 |
| SOAP/WSDL | **None.** REST only. | API guide throughout |
| Availability | 7×24, no recurring maintenance window | API guide §8.4 |
| Two regimes | RG (régime général) and RA (régime agricole, msa.fr) on separate hosts | API guide §10.2 |

**Régime scope:** this build targets **RG only**. `scope_manifest.json` descopes the
agricultural regime (MSA) explicitly. Every RA host in the endpoint tables is recorded in
P3 for completeness but is **out of scope to build**.

##### 1.5 Constraints that shape the design

Three constraints are load-bearing and must reach the solution design, not just the runbook:

1. **There is no OAuth2 and no client-credentials flow.** The API reuses the declarant's
   *portal identity* — SIRET + surname + first name + password — and the password is
   transmitted **in clear inside the request body** (TLS is the only protection). The guide
   states the password must be encrypted if stored in the payroll software. For a service
   provider this is a credential-custody problem per client, not a one-off configuration.
   The **concentrateur** model (P2) exists precisely to avoid holding one credential per
   client and is the correct choice for Mercans.

2. **The DGFiP withholding rate arrives only through the return leg.** There is no separate
   DGFiP rate API. If the inbound integration is not built, or silently stops, payroll
   applies stale or default rates and under- or over-withholds tax. This makes the inbound
   direction **statutorily mandatory**, not a convenience.

3. **CRM 34 is not a directly postable AT/MP rate.** The rate delivered is the one computed
   by the regional fund *without reduction cases applied*. Where an employee is on a reduced
   rate, the employer must derive the applicable rate from the transmitted one. Posting
   CRM 34 straight into payroll is a defect.

Additionally: **the compte AT/MP itself** — the rate notification and its calculation
detail — remains a portal-only service. Only the CRM 34 rate value is machine-retrievable.

##### 1.6 Deposit granularity

Only **unitary deposit** is supported: one DSN flow containing **one `S20` block** per
call. Archives are rejected. Violating this does *not* fail the HTTP call — the service
still returns an AEE, and the error surfaces later in the anomaly report (bilan
d'anomalies). This is a trap: a batch-shaped integration will appear to succeed and fail
asynchronously. See P4 §4.6.

##### 1.7 Confidence

**High.** Every statement above is taken from the authority's own published specification
(API guide v6.8, dated 2026-07-06 — current) or from the dossier's own signed scope
determination. The one item carried as unverified is the **conformance/test enrolment
terms** for the EDITEUR environment, which are behind login (P6).


### NETENT-DSN / `P2-enrolment-access-credentials.md`

#### P2 — Enrolment, Access & Credentials
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Status: **BUILD-READY**, with two `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` items behind declarant login.
Source: API guide v6.8 §2.3–2.4, §3.1; `DSN-Inscription-Concentrateurs v1.5.docx`.

---

##### 2.1 Decide the operating model first

Everything downstream depends on this choice. There are two, and they are not
interchangeable.

| | **Declarant** (direct) | **Concentrateur** (recommended for Mercans) |
|---|---|---|
| Who authenticates | Each client, with their own net-entreprises credentials | Mercans, once, with its own SIRET |
| Credentials held | One password **per client** | One password, plus per-client *identification* (no password) |
| Enrolment | Client registers for DSN; nothing else needed | Charte concentrateur + GIP-MDS approval + registration |
| Deposit identifies the declarant by | The token | An `Authorization` header element (§2.4.2) |
| Port | 443 | **8443** for the concentrateur-scoped search services |
| Return retrieval | The declarant's own returns | Only flows **this same concentrateur deposited** |

The guide is explicit that a payroll provider filing for many clients is a
*concentrateur*, and that a company transmitting its own DSN directly from its software
needs **no special procedure beyond DSN registration**.

> **Design consequence.** As a concentrateur, Mercans never stores a client portal
> password. It identifies the client with a base64 `siret;nom;prenom` triple. That removes
> the credential-custody exposure flagged in P1 §1.5(1) and is the reason this model is
> recommended.

##### 2.2 Concentrateur enrolment procedure

Sequence, from `DSN-Inscription-Concentrateurs v1.5`:

1. **Implement the API** per the implementation guide (this runbook set).
2. **Sign the charte concentrateur.** Published in the dedicated area of net-entreprises.fr.
   `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]] charte concentrateur (signable PDF) — the "espace dédié" requires a
   declarant account; not retrievable anonymously.`
3. **Return the signed charte** as PDF to `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr`, or by post in two
   signed copies to:
   Madame Elisabeth HUMBERT-BOTTIN, Directeur du GIP-MDS, 4-14 rue Ferrus, 75014 Paris.
4. **Await GIP-MDS decision.** The file is reviewed; the applicant is added to the
   concentrateur list in back office *or not*. A decision email is returned; if approved, a
   counter-signed copy of the charte comes back. **This is a human approval gate with no
   published SLA** — treat it as the critical-path lead item (see `effort-estimates.md`).
5. **Register for DSN** on net-entreprises.fr (régime général) with the connection triplet
   `{siret, nom, prénom}` that will be used to authenticate. Required **even though the
   concentrateur is not itself a declarant**.
6. **Submit the environment registration form** naming the triplet to enrol, for the
   **EDITEUR** (test) environment and/or **PRODUCTION**. Flows from that concentrateur are
   then recognised.
   `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]] EDITEUR/PRODUCTION enrolment form — linked from the enrolment
   document to a net-entreprises.fr page behind login.`
7. **Client-side registration.** If Mercans acts as *tiers déclarant*, clients go into its
   portfolio and it self-authorises for DSIJ to receive BPIJ. If not, **each client must
   register for DSN themselves** (electronically accepting the Charte Utilisateur DSN) and
   for DSIJ to obtain BPIJ on their own dashboard.

> Step 7 is a **client-onboarding dependency, not an IT task.** No client can be filed for
> until they have registered and accepted the Charte Utilisateur DSN. Build it into the
> onboarding runbook.

##### 2.3 Service codes

The `service` field in the authentication request selects the declarative service. The
values differ between production and the editor test environment:

| Service | Production | EDITEUR test env |
|---------|-----------|------------------|
| DSN Régime Général | `25` | **`97`** |
| DSN Régime Agricole | `26` | **`94`** |
| **Concentrateur DSN** | `98` | `98` (identical) |

Hard-coding `25` and pointing at the test host is a classic first-day failure. Make the
service code an environment-scoped configuration value, not a constant.

##### 2.4 Authentication request contract

`POST /authentifier/1.0/`, `Content-Type: application/xml`.

**Declarant / concentrateur on net-entreprises.fr:**

```xml
<identifiants>
  <siret>12345678901234</siret>
  <nom>CTR2000</nom>
  <prenom>concentrateur</prenom>
  <motdepasse>azerty42</motdepasse>
  <service>98</service>
</identifiants>
```

Field rules:

| Field | Mand. | Length | Rule |
|-------|-------|--------|------|
| `siret` | O | `[14,14]` | SIRET of the declarant, or of the establishment that signed the charte concentrateur |
| `nom` | O | `[1,64]` | Surname, or concentrateur company name. Letters (incl. French accents) with apostrophe, hyphen or space as internal separators only |
| `prenom` | O | `[1,39]` | Same character class as `nom` |
| `motdepasse` | O | `[8,30]` | **Transmitted in clear.** Must be encrypted at rest in the payroll software. |
| `service` | O | `[1,2]` | See §2.3 |

The exact accepted-character regex is reproduced verbatim in API guide §3.1.1; it permits
the accented Latin set and rejects digits and punctuation other than apostrophe, hyphen
and space.

The MSA variant (`monportail.msa.fr`) takes `identifiant` `[1,30]` + `motdepasse` `[1,30]`
instead. **Out of scope** — RA is descoped.

> **Accent rule (§2.4.2).** When a concentrateur identifies a declarant, the name and first
> name **must not contain accents**. The authentication regex above *does* accept accented
> characters. These two rules disagree in scope and are frequently conflated — strip accents
> for the declarant-identification header, not for your own concentrateur credentials.

##### 2.5 Token handling

- The **response body is the token itself**, base64-encoded, `Content-Type: application/octet-stream`, HTTP 200.
- Expiry is carried in the **HTTP `Expires` response header**. There is no refresh endpoint.
- Presented on every business call as:
  - `Authorization: DSNLogin jeton=<token>` — declarant
  - `Authorization: DSNLogin concentrateur=<token>` — concentrateur
  - `Authorization: DSNLogin concentrateur=<token>,declarant=<base64(siret;nom;prenom)>` — concentrateur acting for a client
- On **401**, the software *may* re-authenticate and retry. Implement this as
  authenticate-on-401-once, not as a pre-emptive refresh loop — the platform treats
  repeated authentication failures as an attack and **temporarily blocks the account**
  (HTTP 403).

Registration is checked **twice** — at authentication *and* on every business call that
needs declarant identity. A client who lapses their DSN registration fails at call time,
not at login.

##### 2.6 Certificates and TLS

A digital certificate from a net-entreprises-approved certification authority is accepted
for connection. TLS 1.0 and 1.1 were withdrawn for net-entreprises.fr services in
October 2023 — **TLS 1.2+ only**.

The guide pins the certification authorities per host family (Certigna / Dhimyotis,
Sectigo–TBS, Entrust, DigiCert, Certigna Wild CA, GlobalSign). The trust store used by the
integration must carry these roots; do not assume the platform default is sufficient,
particularly for the CIBTP and cdc.retraites.fr return hosts. Detail in P3 §3.8.

##### 2.7 Support channels

| Need | Route |
|------|-------|
| Technical, API | `incidentDSN@gip-mds.fr` |
| Operations, charte, enrolment | `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr` |
| Phone | 0806 800 700 |
| Functional / business questions | Knowledge base at `net-entreprises.custhelp.com` |

##### 2.8 Access checklist

- [ ] Operating model decided and signed off (concentrateur recommended)
- [ ] Charte concentrateur obtained, signed, sent to `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr`
- [ ] GIP-MDS approval received (**human gate, no published SLA**)
- [ ] `{siret, nom, prénom}` triplet registered for DSN RG on net-entreprises.fr
- [ ] Triplet enrolled for **EDITEUR** via the registration form
- [ ] Triplet enrolled for **PRODUCTION** via the registration form
- [ ] Service codes configured per environment (97 test / 25 prod; 98 concentrateur)
- [ ] Password stored encrypted; never logged (it is in the request body)
- [ ] TLS 1.2+ enforced; CA roots for all return hosts present in the trust store
- [ ] Client onboarding step added: client DSN registration + Charte Utilisateur DSN acceptance


### NETENT-DSN / `P3-transport-interface-contract.md`

#### P3 — Transport & Interface Contract
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Status: **BUILD-READY**. Every endpoint, header and status code below is transcribed from
API guide v6.8 §2, §3, §8, §9, §10, §11.

> **No endpoint in this document was called.** All values are read from the specification.
> The tables are a build input, not a probe result.

---

##### 3.1 Service inventory

Régime général, declarant and concentrateur. Two service versions exist for most
operations: **`/1.0/` returns XML, `/2.0/` returns JSON**. The request is identical.

| # | Operation | Method | Path | Dir |
|---|-----------|--------|------|-----|
| 1 | Authenticate | POST | `/authentifier/1.0/` | — |
| 2 | Deposit a DSN | POST | `/deposer-dsn/{1.0\|2.0}/` | out |
| 3 | List deposits | GET | `/lister-depots/{1.0\|2.0}/{from}[/{to}]` | in |
| 4 | Search returns by flow | GET | `/lister-retours-flux/{1.0\|2.0}/{idflux}` | in |
| 5 | Search returns by declarant | GET | `/lister-retours-declarant/{1.0\|2.0}/{from}[/{to}]` | in |
| 6 | Search returns by concentrateur | GET | `/lister-retours-concentrateur/{1.0\|2.0}/{from}[/{to}]` | in |
| 7 | Download a return | GET | `/telecharger-retour/1.0/{ref}/{id}` | in |
| 8 | List OC parameter sheets — by declarant | GET | `/lister-fiches-declarant/{1.0\|2.0}/{from}[/{to}]` | in |
| 9 | …by concentrateur | GET | `/lister-fiches-concentrateur/{1.0\|2.0}/{from}[/{to}]` | in |
| 10 | …by SIREN | GET | `/lister-fiches-siren/{1.0\|2.0}/{siren}` | in |
| 11 | Download an OC parameter sheet | GET | `/telecharger-fiche/1.0/{ref}.{xml\|pdf}` | in |
| 12 | List deleted OC sheets | GET | `/lister-fiches-suppr-{declarant\|concentrateur\|siren}/{1.0\|2.0}/…` | in |
| 13 | List BPIJ | GET | `/lister-bpij/1.0/{date}[/{to}]?siret=…[&page=n]` | in |
| 14 | Download a BPIJ | GET | `/telecharger-bpij/1.0/{ref}` | in |
| 15 | Search TPT returns | GET | `/lister-retours-tpt-{flux\|date}/1.0/…` | in |
| 16 | Download a TPT return | GET | `/telecharger-retour-TPT/1.0/…` | in |

Timestamps in path segments are `yyyyMMddHHmmss` (e.g. `20130801130000`); BPIJ dates are
`yyyyMMdd`.

##### 3.2 Endpoint addressing — régime général (IN SCOPE)

| Service | Env | URL |
|---------|-----|-----|
| **Authentication** (RG *and* RA *and* concentrateur) | Test | `https://test-services.net-entreprises.fr/authentifier/1.0/` |
| | Prod | `https://services.net-entreprises.fr/authentifier/1.0/` |
| **Deposit** | Test | `https://depot-edit.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr/deposer-dsn/{1.0\|2.0}/` |
| | Prod | `https://depot.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr/deposer-dsn/{1.0\|2.0}/` |
| **Returns — consultation** | Test | `https://consultation-edit.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr/…` |
| | Prod | `https://consultation.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr/…` |
| **Returns — consultation, concentrateur** | Test | `https://consultation-edit.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr:8443/lister-retours-concentrateur/{1.0\|2.0}/` |
| | Prod | `https://consultation.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr:8443/lister-retours-concentrateur/{1.0\|2.0}/` |
| **Returns — download** | Test/Prod | **Hypermedia.** URL is returned by the search services. |
| **OC parameter sheets — consultation** | Test | `https://test-fpoc.net-entreprises.fr/…` |
| | Prod | `https://fpoc.net-entreprises.fr/…` |
| **OC parameter sheets — download** | Test/Prod | **Hypermedia.** |

Concrete download-host examples appearing in the guide's samples:
`https://telechargement-edit.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` (EDITEUR) and
`https://telechargement.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` (PRODUCTION).

> **Authentication is centralised.** A concentrateur always authenticates on
> `services.net-entreprises.fr`, *regardless* of whether it subsequently deposits to the RG
> or RA deposit point.

> **The `:8443` rule is enforced.** A concentrateur reaching the API on any port other than
> 8443 for the concentrateur-scoped services gets **HTTP 403**.

###### Downstream return hosts (referenced, not primary)

Certain bodies serve their returns from their own hosts. Reachability and CA trust for
these must be validated separately:

| Body | EDITEUR | PRODUCTION |
|------|---------|------------|
| France Travail | `teledeclarants.francetravail.fr/ex016-dsnpebp/V1` | `teledeclarants.francetravail.fr/ex016-dsnpe/V1` |
| PAS CRM (DGFiP) | `edit-pascrm.net-entreprises.fr` | `pascrm.net-entreprises.fr` |
| Net CRM | `edit-netcrm.net-entreprises.fr` | `netcrm.net-entreprises.fr` |
| CDC Retraites | `cr8-prex.hpocdc.fr/Retraite/Public/CR8/Facade/api/v1` | `cr8.cdc.retraites.fr/Retraite/Public/CR8/Facade/api/v1` |
| CIBTP | — (none) | `dsn-crm.cibtp.fr/api/crm/consulter?CleAcces=` |
| Declarassur (OC) | `validation.dac.sintia.fr/internet/net-entreprise/` | `www.declarassur.fr/internet/net-entreprise/` |
| FNMF (OC) | `editeur.concentrateur-fnmf.fr` | `telechargement.concentrateur-fnmf.fr` |
| Net-Prev (OC) | `dsn-homologation.net-prev.fr/` | `dsn.net-prev.fr/` |
| CNAM BPIJ | — (Prod only) | `api-bpij.net-entreprises.ameli.fr/BPIJ-app/` |
| CNAM PJ-IJ | `api-pj-ij-val.net-entreprises.ameli.fr/indemnites-journalieres-pj/1.0/` | `api-pj-ij.net-entreprises.ameli.fr/indemnites-journalieres-pj/1.0/` |
| Retours TPT | — (Prod only) | `retours-tpt.net-entreprises.fr/` |

**CIBTP, BPIJ and TPT have no test environment.** They can only be exercised in
production. Plan acceptance for them accordingly (P6 §6.5).

###### Régime agricole (OUT OF SCOPE — recorded for completeness)

Deposit `depot{,-formation}.dsnra.net-entreprises.fr`; consultation
`consultation{,-formation}.dsnra.net-entreprises.fr`; MSA authentication
`logindsn{,-formation}.dsnra.net-entreprises.fr`. Not to be built — see P1 §1.4.

##### 3.3 Request headers

| Header | Value | Notes |
|--------|-------|-------|
| `Authorization` | `DSNLogin jeton=…` / `concentrateur=…[,declarant=…]` | Not on `/authentifier/` |
| `Content-Type` | `application/xml` (auth) · `text/plain` (deposit) | |
| `Content-Encoding` | `gzip` | Supported and used in the guide's own examples |
| `User-Agent` | `Client-DSN (<Product>/<version>; <vendor>)` | Follow the published shape, e.g. `Client-DSN (DsnBuilder/12.5; Paie.fr)` |
| `Host` | Must match the target host | An inconsistent `Host` is an explicit **403** case |
| `Content-Length` | byte length | |

##### 3.4 Response headers that carry control logic

Three response headers are not informational — they drive the client:

| Header | Meaning | Client obligation |
|--------|---------|-------------------|
| `Expires` | On auth: token expiry. On search: **earliest permitted time of the next call.** | Schedule the next poll from this value. Only **HTTP 200** responses carry it and update the platform's counter. |
| `Accept-Ranges: minutes=0-60` | Min/max width, in minutes, of a permitted time-range search | Clamp the search window to this. `minutes=0-44640` (BPIJ) means 31 days. |
| `Cache-Control: no-cache` | Set on all GET responses | Do not cache; the platform sets this to keep personal data out of caches. |
| `Retry-After` | On **202** for concentrateur PDF parameter-sheet requests | Wait the stated delay before re-requesting. |
| `WWW-Authenticate` | On **401**, disambiguates the cause | Parse it — see §3.6. |

##### 3.5 HTTP status semantics

Codes the API overloads beyond their RFC meaning:

| Code | Meaning in this API |
|------|---------------------|
| **200** | OK. Also the only case that carries `Expires` and advances the polling counter. |
| **202** | Accepted. **Only** for concentrateur requests for OC parameter sheets in PDF. Honour `Retry-After`. |
| **204** | No content. **Only** for OC parameter sheets — the sheet was deleted and replaced. |
| **401** | Token missing, invalid or expired; or credentials wrong; or declarant/concentrateur not registered for DSN (**wait until D+1** after registration). |
| **403** | Account temporarily blocked after repeated failed authentications · attempting to read returns or deposits belonging to a flow you did not deposit · **concentrateur using a port other than 8443** · inconsistent `Host` header. |
| **404** | Unknown declarant (not registered) · unknown flow id · unknown return id — **including after purge** (see §3.7). Also returned during service interruptions. |
| **406** | Client demanded an unsupported `Accept-Encoding`. Supported set must include `gzip`, or send nothing. |
| **415** | Unsupported media type. |
| **422** | Invalid parameters. On **deposit, this is the avis de rejet** — the file was refused. Also currently returned where **429** is intended (see below). |
| **429** | Polling contract breached: called before `Expires`, or search range wider than `Accept-Ranges`. |
| **503** | Service interruption (ideal case; **404 may be returned instead**). |

> **Two traps here.**
> 1. **422 on deposit is a business rejection, not a malformed request.** The response body
>    is the *avis de rejet*. Do not retry it unchanged — it will be rejected identically.
> 2. **404 is ambiguous.** It means "unknown", "purged" *or* "the platform is down". Do not
>    treat a 404 on a known-good flow id as data loss without checking the supervision
>    endpoint (§3.9) first.

For OC parameter sheets, the guide states **422 will be replaced by 429** once the
platform's Apache version permits. Handle both for that service family.

##### 3.6 401 disambiguation via `WWW-Authenticate`

| Realm | Cause | Correct response |
|-------|-------|------------------|
| `Basic realm="Expiration du mot de passe"` | Password expired | **Human action.** Alert; the password must be reset on the portal. Do not retry. |
| `Basic realm="net-entreprises.fr"` / `"msa.fr"` | Unknown account or wrong password | Stop. Do not retry — retries lead to a 403 block. |
| `DSNLogin realm="Jeton manquant ou invalide"` | Token missing/invalid | Re-authenticate once, retry once. |
| `DSNLogin realm="Expiration du jeton"` | Token expired | Re-authenticate once, retry once. |
| `DSNLogin realm="Utilisateur non-inscrit au service"` | Declarant or concentrateur not registered for DSN | **Onboarding defect.** Alert; if registration is same-day, wait until D+1. |

Distinguishing these correctly is the difference between a self-healing integration and
one that locks the account out. Implement the realm parse; do not treat all 401s alike.

##### 3.7 Retention and purge

| Data | Retained | Consequence |
|------|----------|-------------|
| Return **tracking/status** (via consultation) | **13 months** (same history rule as the dashboard) | |
| Returns **available for download** | **3 months** by the SI DSN | After that they are **purged and not archived**. A later request yields 404. |

**This is an archival obligation on Mercans.** GIP-MDS does not keep the returns.
Anything needed for audit — AEE, certificat de conformité, CRM 94 rate history, CRM 34 —
must be pulled and stored on our side inside the 3-month window. An exceptional request to
the individual OPS is possible but is entirely at that body's discretion.

##### 3.8 TLS and certificate authorities

TLS 1.2 minimum (1.0/1.1 withdrawn October 2023). CA roots the trust store must carry, by
host family:

| CA | Covers |
|----|--------|
| Certigna / Dhimyotis | net-entreprises.fr services, `dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` |
| Sectigo (via TBS) | net-entreprises service hosts |
| Entrust | referenced host family |
| DigiCert | `cdc.retraites.fr` |
| Certigna Wild CA | CIBTP |
| GlobalSign | `declarassur` |

The guide publishes root and intermediate download URLs for each. Validate the chain for
**every** host in §3.2, including the downstream return hosts — they are not all under the
same CA as the deposit point.

##### 3.9 Supervision

Platform status, designed to be polled instead of probing the live services:

- EDITEUR: `http://api-dsn.net-entreprises.fr/api-monitor/1.0/editeur`
- PRODUCTION: `http://api-dsn.net-entreprises.fr/api-monitor/1.0/production`
- XML form: `http://api-dsn.net-entreprises.fr/api-monitor/{version}/{plateforme}/xml/`

Refreshed every 5 minutes. Status codes: `OK` (reachable), `KO` (unreachable), `N/A` (not
tested). Service IDs relevant to an RG concentrateur build:

| ID | Service | ID | Service |
|----|---------|----|---------|
| 0 | Declarant — Authentication | 5 | Concentrateur — Authentication |
| 1 | RG Declarant — Deposit | 6 | RG Concentrateur — Deposit |
| 2 | RG Declarant — Search by range | 7 | RG Concentrateur — Search by range |
| 3 | RG Declarant — Search by flow | 8 | RG Concentrateur — Search by flow |
| 4 | RG Declarant — Download return | 9 | RG Concentrateur — Download return |
| 10 | FPOC Declarant — Search sheets | 11 | FPOC Concentrateur — Search sheets |
| 20 | NETCRM — Download | 21 | PASCRM — Download |
| 22 | BPIJ — Search (**Prod only**) | 23 | BPIJ — Download (**Prod only**) |
| 24–26 | Retours TPT — search by range / by idflux / download | | |

Wire this into monitoring **before** go-live. It is the sanctioned way to distinguish
"platform down" from "our integration is broken" — and it resolves the 404 ambiguity in
§3.5.

##### 3.10 Polling contract

- **Deposit and download are not rate-limited.** Search services are.
- Interval is dictated by `Expires` on each 200 response and **may be changed by the
  platform at any time**. Never hard-code an interval.
- After an error there is no `Expires` and the counter does not advance — an immediate
  retry is permitted.
- Search-range width is bounded by `Accept-Ranges` per service.
- OC parameter-sheet searches are **not** technically interval-limited, but the guide asks
  that they be called on demand rather than polled.

Breaching the contract yields 429 (or 422 today for parameter sheets).

##### 3.11 Reference implementation

`GIPMDS-dsn-api-devkit-main.zip` (source manifest A5) is GIP-MDS's own Java client. It
implements exactly this contract — `services/auth`, `services/depot`,
`services/recherche`, `services/telechargement` — and its JUnit suite demonstrates both the
declarant and concentrateur call sequences. Use it to resolve any ambiguity in this
document before raising a question with `incidentDSN@gip-mds.fr`.


### NETENT-DSN / `P4-outbound-payload-neodes.md`

#### P4 — Outbound Payload Specification (NEODeS)
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Status: **BUILD-READY** (structural). One `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` for the CNAM PJ-IJ OpenAPI
contract, which is an adjacent flow, not the DSN itself.
Source: NEODeS Cahier technique **CT2026.1.2** (dated 2025-12-24); API guide v6.8 §3.2.

> **Mode note.** This phase specifies the *structure and transport contract* of the
> outbound payload — blocks, encoding, framing, deposit rules. It does **not** enumerate
> every rubrique and its value list; the CT is 700+ pages and that enumeration belongs to
> the Data Dictionary and Report Spec artefacts (`FR-compliance/04-reports-spec/`,
> `05-data-dictionary/`). The interlock is recorded in `INTERLINKAGES.md`.

---

##### 4.1 Format identity

| Property | Value |
|----------|-------|
| Norm | **NEODeS** — Norme d'Échanges Optimisée des Déclarations Sociales |
| Version | **CT2026.1.2**, published 2025-12-24 |
| Physical format | Flat text, one rubrique per line |
| Line syntax | `<bloc>.<rubrique>,'<valeur>'` — e.g. `S21.G00.30.001,'1581075005001'` |
| Character encoding | **ISO-8859-1** (Latin-1) — *not* UTF-8 |
| MIME type on deposit | `text/plain` |
| Compression | `Content-Encoding: gzip` supported |
| Extension in practice | `.dsn` / `.txt` |

> **Encoding is a real failure mode.** A UTF-8 file with accented names will be accepted at
> HTTP level and fail downstream on identity matching. Enforce ISO-8859-1 at write time and
> assert it in the pre-flight (P6 §6.3).

##### 4.2 Structural model

The file is a strict hierarchy of numbered blocks. Each block carries an ordinal
(`S10`, `S20`, `S21`, `S89`, `S90`) and a group (`G00`) and an id (`.00`, `.05`, `.30`, …).

```
S10.G00.00   Envoi                    ← file header: software, norm version, nature
S10.G00.01   Emetteur                 ← the sender (SIRET, name, address)
S10.G00.02   Contact Emetteur         ← technical contact, email, phone
S20.G00.05   Déclaration              ← exactly ONE per deposit (see §4.6)
S20.G00.07   Contact chez le déclaré
S20.G00.08   Identifiant métier
S21.G00.06   Entreprise
S21.G00.11     Etablissement
S21.G00.30       Individu
S21.G00.40         Contrat
S21.G00.50           Versement individu
S21.G00.51             Rémunération
S21.G00.78             Base assujettie
S21.G00.81             Cotisation individuelle
S90.G00.90   Total                    ← record count + block count
```

##### 4.3 Declaration natures carried by `S20.G00.05`

One integration, six declaration natures — this is where the ten filings of P1 §1.2 are
distinguished:

| Nature | Purpose | Maps to filing |
|--------|---------|----------------|
| DSN Mensuelle | The monthly declaration | `FR-DSN-001`, `FR-DGFIP-001`, `FR-URSSAF-002/003/004`, `FR-AGIRC-001` |
| DSN Signal Fin de Contrat Unique | End-of-contract event | `FR-DSN-002` |
| DSN Signal Arrêt de Travail | Work-stoppage event | `FR-DSN-003` |
| DSN Signal Reprise de Travail | Return-to-work event | `FR-DSN-003` |
| DSN Signal Déclaration Préalable à l'Embauche | Pre-hire signal | **See caution below** |
| DSN Signal Amorçage | Priming/seeding signal | operational |
| DSN Substitution | Replacement of a prior declaration | correction path |

> **DPAE caution.** NEODeS defines a DPAE signal, but `scope_manifest.json` records that in
> 2026 the DPAE remains a **separate filing** to URSSAF, with its move into the DSN deferred
> to 1 January 2027 at the earliest. **Do not route DPAE through NETENT-DSN for this build.**
> It belongs to `URSSAF-DPAE`. Cross-referenced in `INTERLINKAGES.md`.

##### 4.4 Block inventory (CT2026.1.2)

Complete block list extracted from the CT. Blocks marked ● are mandatory in an ordinary
monthly DSN for the in-scope population; ○ are conditional.

**Header / envelope**

| Block | Name | |
|-------|------|---|
| `S10.G00.00` | Envoi | ● |
| `S10.G00.01` | Emetteur | ● |
| `S10.G00.02` | Contact Emetteur | ● |
| `S20.G00.05` | Déclaration | ● |
| `S20.G00.07` | Contact chez le déclaré | ○ |
| `S20.G00.08` | Identifiant de la déclaration | ○ |

**Employer**

> The `S89` family carries items `scope_manifest.json` places **out of scope** for wage
> types (free-share and stock-option gains are taxable on disposal, outside PAS). Present in
> the norm; not built in this phase.

##### 4.5 Minimal well-formed deposit

The guide's own minimal example, verbatim in shape:

```
S10.G00.00.001,'superpaie'
S10.G00.00.002,'GENIELOG'
S10.G00.00.003,'v99'
S10.G00.00.005,'01'
S10.G00.00.006,'PHASE1V1'
S10.G00.00.007,'01'
S10.G00.00.008,'01'
S10.G00.01.001,'537550964'
S10.G00.01.002,'00010'
...
S20.G00.05.001,'01'
S20.G00.05.005,'01032013'
S20.G00.05.006,'01537550964000105375509640001003131112345'
...
S21.G00.06.001,'537550964'
S21.G00.11.001,'00010'
S21.G00.30.001,'1581075005001'
S21.G00.40.001,'01032013'
S21.G00.51.003,'150.00'
S21.G00.71.001,'A120'
S21.G00.85.001,'53755096400010'
S90.G00.90.001,'82'
S90.G00.90.002,'1'
```

Note the conventions the example fixes:
- Decimals use a **period**, two places: `'150.00'`, `'37.50'`.
- Dates are **`ddMMyyyy`** in the payload (contrast with `yyyyMMddHHmmss` in *URL paths* —
  see P3 §3.1; these are different formats in the same integration).
- `S90.G00.90.001` is the **total line count**; `.002` the declaration count.
- Every value is single-quoted, including numerics.

##### 4.6 Deposit rules — the constraint that fails silently

> **Only unitary deposit is supported.** One DSN flow, containing exactly **one `S20`
> block**. Archives are not accepted.
>
> **If you violate this, the service still returns an AEE.** The HTTP call succeeds. The
> error appears only later, in the bilan d'anomalies.

This is the single most dangerous behaviour in the interface. A batch-shaped integration
looks green on deposit and is discovered broken days later, after the filing deadline. The
mitigation is structural, not operational:

1. Assert `count(S20.G00.05) == 1` in the pre-flight, before the HTTP call.
2. Never treat a 200 + AEE as proof of acceptance. Acceptance is the **certificat de
   conformité** (return code 11), not the AEE (code 10). See P5 §5.3.

##### 4.7 Deposit request

```
POST /deposer-dsn/1.0/ HTTP/1.1
Host: depot.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr
Authorization: DSNLogin concentrateur=<token>,declarant=<base64(siret;nom;prenom)>
User-Agent: Client-DSN (<Product>/<version>; Mercans)
Content-Type: text/plain
Content-Encoding: gzip
Content-Length: <bytes>

<NEODeS file, ISO-8859-1, gzipped>
```

- `/1.0/` → XML response · `/2.0/` → JSON response. Request body identical.
- Declarant identity comes from the **token** in declarant mode, from the
  **`declarant=` header element** in concentrateur mode.
- The SI DSN records **which concentrateur deposited each flow**, and enforces later that
  only that same concentrateur may retrieve its returns (P3 §3.5, 403).

##### 4.8 Synchronous response

The deposit response is **synchronous** and is itself the first return:

| Outcome | HTTP | Body |
|---------|------|------|
| Accepted | **200** | **AEE** — Accusé d'Enregistrement Electronique (return code 10) |
| Rejected | **422** | **Avis de rejet** (also code 10 family) |

Both are re-downloadable later through the returns services. A 422 here is a *business*
rejection — the file was refused. Do not retry unchanged.

##### 4.9 Correction model

There is no in-place edit. Corrections are declarative:

| Situation | Mechanism |
|-----------|-----------|
| Replace a whole prior declaration | **DSN Substitution** nature (`S20.G00.05`), within the permitted window |
| Correct a previously declared base | `S21.G00.45` *Données précédemment déclarées* — carries the period concerned |
| Correct withholding at source | `S21.G00.56` *Régularisation de prélèvement à la source* |
| Wrongly declared supplementary-pension base | `S21.G00.83` / `S21.G00.84` |
| Wrongly declared base/AT-MP base | `S21.G00.95` |

> Consistent with `scope_manifest.json`: **a negative gross is never a payslip line.** A
> correction to a base already declared is a DSN regularisation carrying the period
> concerned. The payroll engine must produce the regularisation block, not a negative wage
> type. This is a build constraint on the WTC/payslip side, enforced here.

##### 4.10 Adjacent outbound flow — CNAM PJ-IJ

Deposit of supporting documents for daily-benefit claims is a separate CNAM API
(`/indemnites-journalieres-pj/1.0/`, P3 §3.2) with its own semantics: **400** for technical
control failure, **409** for functional control failure, page size capped at 100
occurrences. Reject codes are enumerated in the CNAM `.xlsx` reference that ships in the
archive.

`[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]] cnam-contrat-api-pj-ij-v1.0.yml — the OpenAPI contract is named in API
guide §1.2 [10] but is not in the public archive and has no published URL. The .xlsx
companion (referential/reject codes) IS present.`

Not required for the ten in-scope filings; scope it separately if PJ-IJ deposit is
contracted.

##### 4.11 Outbound build checklist

- [ ] Writer emits **ISO-8859-1**, asserted at write time
- [ ] Line syntax `bloc.rubrique,'valeur'`; all values quoted
- [ ] Dates `ddMMyyyy` in payload; amounts `0.00` with period separator
- [ ] `S90.G00.90.001/.002` totals computed, not hard-coded
- [ ] **Pre-flight asserts exactly one `S20.G00.05`**
- [ ] Declaration nature correctly selected per filing (§4.3); **DPAE excluded**
- [ ] Mandatory block set (●) present for the in-scope population
- [ ] Correction paths implemented as regularisation blocks, never negative gross
- [ ] gzip enabled; `Content-Type: text/plain`
- [ ] Concentrateur `declarant=` header base64 of `siret;nom;prenom`, **accents stripped**
- [ ] 422 handled as business rejection (no blind retry)
- [ ] AEE stored, but acceptance judged on the certificat de conformité (P5)


### NETENT-DSN / `P5-inbound-returns-neores.md`

#### P5 — Inbound Returns Specification (NEOReS / harmonised DSN)
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Status: **BUILD-READY** (structural). XSD/XSL artefacts are `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` — named in
the spec but distributed behind declarant login.
Source: API guide v6.8 §1.3, §3.4–3.6, §5, §6, §8.2, §12; NEOReS CT2026.1.0; NEOReS note
différentielle CT2024.1→CT2026.1.

> **The inbound leg is statutorily mandatory, not optional.** The DGFiP withholding rate
> reaches payroll *only* here (P1 §1.5(2)). A missing return leg is a tax failure.

---

##### 5.1 Two return formats coexist

| Format | Used by | Current version |
|--------|---------|-----------------|
| **Harmonised DSN "bilans"** format | Most returns (AEE, BIS, CRM AT, CRM DGFiP, …) | `v01r06` → `v02r03` depending on return |
| **NEOReS** | The newer CRMs (118, 119, 120, 121, 130) | **CT2026.1.0** (2025-04-08); 2026.1.1 and 2027.1.0 schemas announced |
| Body-specific | AGIRC-ARRCO (`Schéma XSD RC V07`), OC (`CRM-DSN-OC-P3-V1.1.4`), BPIJ (`ROOT_BPIJ_V02_04`) | per body |

**The harmonised DSN format is backward compatible.** Integrating the latest version
guarantees compatibility with every return that respects the harmonised format. Build
against the newest, not against each minimum.

NEOReS describes itself as a fully automatable industrial return system intended for direct
consumption by payroll solutions — it is the strategic direction; the harmonised format is
the legacy that is still load-bearing.

##### 5.2 Retrieval sequence

Returns are **pull**, never push. There is no callback and no webhook.

```
1. authenticate                    POST /authentifier/1.0/
2. search                          GET  /lister-retours-flux/{v}/{idflux}
                                or GET  /lister-retours-declarant/{v}/{from}[/{to}]
                                or GET  /lister-retours-concentrateur/{v}/{from}[/{to}]   (port 8443)
3. read the <url> from each result (HYPERMEDIA — do not construct it)
4. download                        GET  <that url>
```

Search responses carry a `<url>` (XML) or `"url"` (JSON) per return, e.g.
`https://telechargement.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr/telecharger-retour/1.0/<ref>/<id>`.
**Always follow the returned URL.** Constructing download URLs is unsupported and will
break when the platform re-hosts a return family.

Search strategy:
- **By flow** (`lister-retours-flux`) — deterministic, tied to one deposit. Use for
  tracking a specific filing to completion.
- **By declarant / concentrateur + time range** — sweeps everything. Use for the scheduled
  harvest. Subject to the `Accept-Ranges` window (P3 §3.4) and the `Expires` interval.

##### 5.3 The acceptance chain — AEE is not acceptance

The single most important operational distinction in this integration:

| Code | Return | What it actually means |
|------|--------|------------------------|
| **10** | **AEE** — Accusé d'Enregistrement Electronique, or **Avis de rejet** | The file was *received*. Nothing more. Issued even when the unitary-deposit rule is broken (P4 §4.6). |
| **11** | **Certificat de conformité**, or **Bilan d'anomalies** | The file was *validated*. **This is acceptance.** |
| **20** | **BIS** — Bilan d'Identification des Salariés | Employees were matched against the national identity referential. |
| **21** | Contrôles inter-déclarations / AT-RT | Cross-declaration consistency checks. |

From the guide's own status matrices:

- AEE `OK` + Certificat de conformité `OK` → clean.
- AEE `OK` + **Bilan d'anomalies `KO`** → **the filing failed.** The deposit succeeded; the
  declaration did not.
- Certificat `OK` + BIS `ANO` → accepted but with identity anomalies to resolve.

> **Do not close a filing on the AEE.** Close it on code 11 `OK`. Treating a 200/AEE as
> "filed" is how a French payroll misses a deadline while its dashboard shows green.

From API guide §1.3 and Annexe 1. Column *Min. format* is the minimum version to integrate;
the harmonised format is backward compatible, so implementing the highest listed suffices.
