> **AR semantics.** Distribution to the OC is *always* OK — errors are corrected inside the
> SI DSN and re-sent. Likewise CNAM/MSA acceptance is always OK. Do not build alerting on
> these ARs; they carry no failure signal.

##### 5.5 Returns that must reach payroll

Most returns are audit evidence. **Four change payroll behaviour** and need a write path
into the engine, not just a file store:

| Code | Delivers | Consumer | Caution |
|------|----------|----------|---------|
| **94** | **PAS withholding rate per employee** | Tax withholding — `FR-DGFIP-002` | **Sole source of the rate.** No fallback API. |
| **34** | AT/MP net rate | Employer contribution — `FR-CARSAT-001` | **Not directly postable** — see below |
| **51** | OC business return | Supplementary health/prévoyance | Per-insurer format |
| **Fiches de paramétrage** | OC contract parameters | Prévoyance contribution setup | Drives configuration, not a period result |

> **CRM 34 must not be posted verbatim.** The rate delivered is the one computed by the
> regional fund **without reduction cases applied**. Where an employee is on a reduced rate,
> the applicable rate must be *derived* from the transmitted one. A straight post is a
> defect. (Landscape `known_constraints`; carried forward from P1 §1.5(3).)

> **The compte AT/MP remains portal-only.** The dematerialised rate notification and its
> calculation detail are not machine-retrievable — only the CRM 34 value is. Any process
> needing the calculation basis has a **manual step**.

##### 5.6 Status vocabulary

Returns carry a status, and the per-return matrices in §5 of the guide define which are
possible for each code:

| Status | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `OK` | Processed successfully |
| `KO` | Failed |
| `ANO` | Processed with anomalies |
| `EN COURS` | In progress — **note: the literal value contains a space** |
| `non renseigné` | Not populated for this return type |

> The guide records a correction for a typo on `EN COURS`. Match on the exact string
> including the space; do not normalise it away and do not match on a prefix.

Notable per-code behaviour:
- **CRM 62** (URSSAF): status `KO` was **replaced by `ANO`**. Logic keyed on `KO` for this
  code is stale.
- **CRM 93** is **not generated when processing is OK** — absence is success, not a missing
  return. Do not alert on it.
- **CRM 116** (CIBTP): no CRM link when the status is `OK`.
- OPS may **overwrite** a return. The same return id can change. Re-fetch rather than
  assuming immutability.

##### 5.7 Retention — an obligation on us

| Data | Held by SI DSN |
|------|----------------|
| Return tracking / status | **13 months** |
| Returns downloadable | **3 months**, then **purged and not archived** |

After 3 months a download yields **404**. Exceptional recovery must be requested from the
individual OPS and depends entirely on that body's own retention practice.

**Therefore:** the harvest job must pull and durably store every return within the window,
and the archive is Mercans-side. This is the audit trail for ten statutory filings. Treat
storage as part of the integration, not as an afterthought. See P7 §7.6.

##### 5.8 Schema artefacts

`[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` — the following are named in API guide §1.2 by **file name only, with
no public URL**. They are distributed through the net-entreprises documentation area, which
requires a declarant account. They were not retrievable without authenticating, which is
outside the acquisition rules for this run:

| Artefact | Needed for |
|----------|-----------|
| `NEOReS_Master_Message_2026.1.1.xsd`, `NEOReS_datatypes_2026.1.1.xsd` | Parsing CRM 118–121, 130 |
| `NEOReS_Master_Message_2027.1.0.xsd`, `NEOReS_datatypes_2027.1.0.xsd` | Forward compatibility |
| `dsn_bilans_v02r03.xsd` (+ `v01r06`…`v02r02`) and matching `.xsl` | Parsing the harmonised format |
| `DSN.xsl`, `NEORES.xsl`, `transcodification_XSL.xml` | Human-readable rendering |
| `Schéma XSD RC V07.xsd`, `accuseReceptionDSNDTO.xsd`, `notificationCcmdDSNDTO.xsd` | AGIRC-ARRCO CRM 71 |
| `ROOT_BPIJ_V02_04.xsd` | BPIJ |
| `NEORES-2026-détails.xlsx`, `NEORES Table des types de paramètres RO.B008.R001` | NEOReS parameter typing |

**What we do have**, and what it is sufficient for: the NEOReS CT2026.1.0 specification PDF
and the CT2024.1→CT2026.1 differential note (source manifest A2, A3), plus
`DSN-NormalisationBilans-Spécifications du schéma-VT2.2.docx` (the harmonised-format schema
specification) and the current OC CRM and fiche-de-paramétrage format PDFs, all inside
archive A4. **Structural design can proceed on these.** The XSDs are needed for *parser
validation*, not for design — they are a build-time dependency, not a blocker to this
phase.

**Action:** request the schema pack from `incidentDSN@gip-mds.fr`, or download it from the
documentation area once a declarant account exists (P2 §2.2 step 5). Sequenced in
`effort-estimates.md`.

##### 5.9 Inbound build checklist

- [ ] Search-then-follow-hypermedia implemented; **no constructed download URLs**
- [ ] `Expires` honoured for poll scheduling; `Accept-Ranges` clamps the search window
- [ ] Concentrateur searches use **port 8443**
- [ ] Acceptance judged on **code 11**, never on the AEE
- [ ] Status match on exact strings incl. `EN COURS` (with the space)
- [ ] CRM 62 `ANO` handled (not `KO`); CRM 93 absence treated as success
- [ ] Returns re-fetched rather than assumed immutable (OPS may overwrite)
- [ ] **CRM 94 → payroll tax rate**, with alerting when it fails to arrive
- [ ] **CRM 34 → derived**, not posted verbatim
- [ ] All returns archived Mercans-side **inside 3 months**
- [ ] Schema pack obtained and parsers validated against the real XSDs


### NETENT-DSN / `P6-test-conformance-cutover.md`

#### P6 — Test, Conformance & Cutover
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Status: **BUILD-READY**, with one `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` (EDITEUR enrolment form) and one
declared **UNVERIFIED** item (formal conformance terms).
Source: API guide v6.8 §2.4.1, §3.1.1, §10.2, §11; `DSN-Inscription-Concentrateurs v1.5`.

---

##### 6.1 Environments

| Environment | Name in the spec | Purpose |
|-------------|------------------|---------|
| **EDITEUR** | "Environnement Editeur", also "Test" | Software-vendor test platform. Full RG deposit + returns. |
| **PRODUCTION** | "Environnement PRODUCTION" | Live filing. |

There are exactly two. There is no separate staging or pre-production tier.

Environment differences that must be configuration, never constants:

| Item | EDITEUR | PRODUCTION |
|------|---------|------------|
| Service code, DSN RG | **`97`** | `25` |
| Service code, concentrateur | `98` | `98` |
| Auth host | `test-services.net-entreprises.fr` | `services.net-entreprises.fr` |
| Deposit host | `depot-edit.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` | `depot.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` |
| Consultation host | `consultation-edit.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` | `consultation.dsnrg.net-entreprises.fr` |
| OC sheets host | `test-fpoc.net-entreprises.fr` | `fpoc.net-entreprises.fr` |
| Supervision | `…/api-monitor/1.0/editeur` | `…/api-monitor/1.0/production` |

Additionally, the return-list table marks each return **Type Test / Type Réel** per
environment — some returns exist in EDITEUR only as synthetic types.

###### Services with no test environment

**CIBTP · BPIJ · Retours TPT** are listed **Production only** (P3 §3.2, and supervision IDs
22–26 are annotated "uniquement en Production").

> These three cannot be exercised before go-live. Either descope them from the acceptance
> gate and accept first-run risk in production with heightened monitoring, or contract a
> controlled production pilot for them. **Decide this explicitly** — do not let it be
> discovered at cutover. BPIJ additionally depends on DSIJ authorisation (P2 §2.2 step 7).

##### 6.2 Getting access to EDITEUR

Enrolment is not self-service. Per `DSN-Inscription-Concentrateurs v1.5` step 6, the
connection triplet `{siret, nom, prénom}` must be submitted on a registration form
nominating **EDITEUR** and/or **PRODUCTION**; flows from that concentrateur are then
recognised.

`[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]] EDITEUR/PRODUCTION registration form — the enrolment document links it
to a net-entreprises.fr page reachable only when logged in. Not retrievable anonymously.`

**UNVERIFIED — not a claim of absence:** the landscape records that a conformity/test path
is referenced on the API DSN page but its **enrolment terms were not established from
public pages**. Whether GIP-MDS operates a *formal conformance/certification* gate for
concentrateurs — beyond charte approval plus environment registration — could not be
confirmed without an account. Nothing found states that such a gate exists; nothing found
excludes it. **Resolve with `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr` at the same time as the charte
submission** (P2 §2.2 step 3) so the answer arrives before it is on the critical path.

##### 6.3 Pre-flight gate (before any HTTP call)

These assertions run against the generated file, offline. Each catches a failure the API
will *not* reliably report:

| # | Assertion | Catches |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 1 | Encoding is **ISO-8859-1** | Accents corrupted downstream; identity matching fails |
| 2 | **Exactly one `S20.G00.05`** | The unitary-deposit trap — API returns an AEE anyway (P4 §4.6) |
| 3 | `S90.G00.90.001` equals actual line count; `.002` equals declaration count | Silent structural corruption |
| 4 | Line syntax `bloc.rubrique,'valeur'` on every line; all values quoted | Parse rejection |
| 5 | Dates `ddMMyyyy`; amounts `n.nn` with period | Format rejection |
| 6 | Mandatory block set (P4 §4.4 ●) present | Bilan d'anomalies KO |
| 7 | Declaration nature matches the intended filing; **nature is not DPAE** | Wrong-channel filing (P4 §4.3) |
| 8 | No negative gross; corrections use regularisation blocks | Scope-manifest rule violation |
| 9 | `declarant=` header value is base64 of `siret;nom;prenom`, **accents stripped** | Declarant identification failure (P2 §2.4) |

Assertion 2 is the one that matters most: it is the only defence against a deposit that
returns 200 and fails days later.

##### 6.4 Connectivity and contract tests (EDITEUR)

| # | Test | Expected |
|---|------|----------|
| C1 | TLS handshake to each host in P3 §3.2, incl. downstream return hosts | TLS 1.2+, chain validates against the pinned CAs |
| C2 | Authenticate, service `97` | 200; body is the base64 token; `Expires` present |
| C3 | Authenticate, concentrateur `98` | 200 + token |
| C4 | Authenticate with a wrong password **once** | 401, `WWW-Authenticate: Basic realm="net-entreprises.fr"`. **Do not repeat** — repeats trigger the 403 block. |
| C5 | Business call with no token | 401, `DSNLogin realm="Jeton manquant ou invalide"` |
| C6 | Business call with an expired token | 401, `DSNLogin realm="Expiration du jeton"` |
| C7 | Concentrateur search on port 443 instead of 8443 | **403** — proves the port rule is enforced and handled |
| C8 | Mismatched `Host` header | 403 |
| C9 | Unsupported `Accept-Encoding` | 406 |
| C10 | Search with a range wider than `Accept-Ranges` | 429 (422 for OC sheets today) |
| C11 | Two searches back-to-back, ignoring `Expires` | 429 |
| C12 | Search after an error response | Permitted immediately — no `Expires`, counter not advanced |

##### 6.5 Functional test cases

| # | Case | Asserts |
|---|------|---------|
| F1 | Deposit a valid monthly DSN, one `S20` | 200 + AEE (code 10) |
| F2 | Retrieve **code 11 certificat de conformité** for F1 | **Acceptance path** — the real success criterion |
| F3 | Deposit a deliberately invalid file | **422** + avis de rejet; no blind retry fires |
| F4 | Deposit a file with **two `S20` blocks** | Confirms the trap: **200 + AEE**, then a bilan d'anomalies KO. Proves detection works. |
| F5 | Retrieve BIS (code 20) | Identity matching; `ANO` path exercised |
| F6 | Search returns by flow id | Deterministic tracking |
| F7 | Search returns by concentrateur + range, port 8443 | Bulk harvest path |
| F8 | Download via the **hypermedia** URL from the search result | No constructed URLs anywhere |
| F9 | Attempt to download a return for a flow deposited by **another** concentrateur | **403** — security boundary holds |
| F10 | Retrieve **CRM 94** (DGFiP rate) and write it to payroll | The mandatory inbound path |
| F11 | Retrieve **CRM 34** and confirm the **derivation** step runs, not a direct post | The reduced-rate defect guard (P5 §5.5) |
| F12 | Retrieve CRM 118/119/120/121 | **NEOReS** parser, distinct from the harmonised parser |
| F13 | CRM 62 returning `ANO` | Not mis-handled as `KO` |
| F14 | Monthly DSN where CRM 93 never arrives | Treated as **success**, not a missing return |
| F15 | Return re-issued/overwritten by an OPS | Re-fetch, not cached-immutable |
| F16 | Event signal: fin de contrat (`FR-DSN-002`) | Event nature routing |
| F17 | Event signal: arrêt / reprise (`FR-DSN-003`) | Event nature routing |
| F18 | Substitution declaration replacing a prior filing | Correction path |
| F19 | `S21.G00.56` PAS regularisation | Correction path, no negative gross |
| F20 | April DSN carrying `S21.G00.13` OETH | `FR-URSSAF-003` annual path |
| F21 | Status `EN COURS` (with the space) | Exact-string matching |
| F22 | OC parameter-sheet search + PDF request as concentrateur | **202** + `Retry-After` honoured |
| F23 | OC sheet deleted and replaced | **204** handled |
| F24 | Download a return older than 3 months | **404** — purge behaviour understood, archive proves its worth |

F4, F11 and F14 are the three cases most likely to be skipped and most likely to cause a
production incident. Keep them in the gate.

##### 6.6 Cutover

**Preconditions**

- [ ] Charte concentrateur counter-signed by GIP-MDS (P2 §2.2 step 4)
- [ ] Conformance question of §6.2 answered by `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr`
- [ ] Triplet registered for **PRODUCTION**
- [ ] Schema pack obtained; parsers validated against real XSDs (P5 §5.8)
- [ ] Pre-flight gate (§6.3) green on real client data
- [ ] EDITEUR suite (§6.4, §6.5) green, F4/F11/F14 included
- [ ] Client-side DSN registration + Charte Utilisateur DSN accepted, **per client**
- [ ] Supervision endpoints wired into monitoring (P3 §3.9)
- [ ] Return archive storage live, with retention ≥ statutory audit period
- [ ] CIBTP / BPIJ / TPT production-only risk explicitly accepted or descoped (§6.1)

**Sequencing.** Cut over **immediately after a filing deadline**, never before one. The
monthly deadline is the **5th** (employers of 50+ paying in the same month) or the **15th**
otherwise. A cutover in the days before a deadline leaves no room to fall back.

**Parallel run.** Run one full cycle in parallel with the incumbent channel: deposit,
harvest returns, and reconcile **CRM 94 rates** against what the incumbent applied. The
rate is the highest-consequence field in the integration; reconcile it explicitly rather
than assuming.

**Rollback.** The fallback is portal deposit on net-entreprises.fr — the same declarations
can be filed manually. Rollback is therefore always available but is **per-client manual
work**; size it before cutover rather than during an incident.

**D+1 rule.** Registration is not effective immediately. A newly registered declarant or
concentrateur yields 401/404 until **D+1**. Do not schedule a go-live on the same day as a
registration.

##### 6.7 Post-cutover watch

For the first two filing cycles, verify daily:

1. Every deposit reached a **code 11 `OK`**, not merely an AEE.
2. **CRM 94** arrived for every client. Absence is a tax incident, not a data gap.
3. **CRM 34** derivations produced sane rates.
4. No return aged past 60 days un-archived (early warning on the 3-month purge).
5. Supervision shows the platform `OK` when our calls fail — i.e. the failure is ours.


### NETENT-DSN / `P7-run-operate.md`

#### P7 — Run & Operate
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Status: **BUILD-READY**.
Source: API guide v6.8 §5, §8.2–8.4, §9, §11; `scope_manifest.json` (deadlines);
`DSN-Inscription-Concentrateurs v1.5` (support routes).

---

##### 7.1 Operating calendar

| Flow | Cadence | Deadline |
|------|---------|----------|
| Monthly DSN (`FR-DSN-001` + 5 filings riding it) | monthly | **5th** of the following month — employers of 50+ paying wages in the same month; **15th** otherwise |
| Fin de contrat (`FR-DSN-002`) | per event | within **5 days** |
| Arrêt / reprise de travail (`FR-DSN-003`) | per event | within **5 days** |
| PAS (`FR-DGFIP-001`) | monthly, inside the DSN | with the DSN; quarterly option below 11 employees |
| OETH / Agefiph (`FR-URSSAF-003`) | annual | **in the April DSN** |
| AT/MP rate (`FR-CARSAT-001`) | inbound, annual + on reclassification | on receipt |
| PAS rate (`FR-DGFIP-002`) | **inbound, monthly** | **before the next payroll run** |

> The two event signals have a 5-day clock that runs from the *event*, not from the payroll
> cycle. They cannot be batched into the monthly run. Scheduling must be event-driven for
> these, calendar-driven for the rest.

##### 7.2 Job schedule

| Job | When | Notes |
|-----|------|-------|
| **Deposit — monthly** | From payroll close, with margin before the 5th/15th | Never on the deadline day itself |
| **Deposit — event signals** | Triggered on the event, ≤5 days | Independent of the monthly cycle |
| **Harvest — flow-scoped** | After each deposit, until code 11 resolves | Deterministic close-out per filing |
| **Harvest — sweep** | Continuous, interval dictated by `Expires` | Concentrateur-scoped, port 8443 |
| **Rate application** | On CRM 94 arrival, **before the next payroll run** | Blocking dependency |
| **Archive** | On every download | Well inside the 3-month purge |
| **Supervision poll** | Aligned to the platform's own 5-minute refresh | Distinguishes their outage from our defect |

**Never hard-code a polling interval.** It comes from `Expires` on each 200 response and
the platform may change it at any time (P3 §3.10).

##### 7.3 Error handling

Retry policy by class — the distinction matters, because the wrong retry locks out an
account:

| Class | Codes | Action |
|-------|-------|--------|
| **Retryable, immediate** | any error (no `Expires` issued, counter not advanced) | One immediate retry is permitted |
| **Retryable, after re-auth** | 401 `Jeton manquant/invalide`, 401 `Expiration du jeton` | Re-authenticate **once**, retry **once** |
| **Retryable, delayed** | 429; 202 with `Retry-After` | Honour the stated delay |
| **NEVER retry** | 401 `Basic realm="net-entreprises.fr"` (bad credentials) | Repeats cause a **403 account block**. Alert a human. |
| **NEVER retry** | 401 `Basic realm="Expiration du mot de passe"` | Password expired — portal reset required |
| **NEVER retry unchanged** | **422 on deposit** = avis de rejet | Business rejection. Fix the file. |
| **Investigate** | 403 | Wrong port (8443), wrong `Host`, or reaching another party's flow |
| **Check supervision first** | 404, 503 | 404 is ambiguous: unknown / purged / **platform down** |

> The single worst failure mode is a naive retry loop on a credential error. It converts a
> configuration mistake into a **blocked account** and takes every client on that
> concentrateur credential offline. Implement the `WWW-Authenticate` realm parse (P3 §3.6)
> before go-live, not after the first incident.

##### 7.4 Alerting

**P1 — statutory consequence**

| Trigger | Why |
|---------|-----|
| **CRM 94 not received** for a client in a cycle | Withholding proceeds on a stale rate. Tax failure. |
| Monthly DSN with **no code 11 `OK`** approaching the deadline | The filing is not accepted, regardless of the AEE |
| **Bilan d'anomalies `KO`** (code 11) | Declaration rejected on validation |
| Event signal approaching its **5-day** limit unsent | Statutory lateness |
| **403 account block** | All clients on that credential are down |

**P2 — data integrity**

| Trigger | Why |
|---------|-----|
| BIS (code 20) `ANO` | Employee identity unmatched nationally |
| CRM 62 `ANO` | URSSAF anomaly (note: `ANO`, not `KO`) |
| CRM 34 derivation produced an out-of-range rate | Reduced-rate handling wrong |
| Return aged >60 days un-archived | Approaching the 3-month purge |
| Deposit returned 200 + AEE but pre-flight assertion 2 failed | Unitary-deposit trap |

**Explicitly do NOT alert on**

- **CRM 93 absent** — it is *not generated* when processing is OK. Absence is success.
- **AR codes 30, 40, 50, 60** — distribution/acceptance ARs are always OK by design.
- **CRM 116 with no CRM link** when status is `OK` — expected.

Mis-alerting on these three trains operators to ignore the channel, which is how a real
P1 gets missed.

##### 7.5 Service level

- **7×24, no recurring maintenance windows.** Interruptions do occur and surface as
  **503**, or — less helpfully — **404**.
- **Deposit and download are not rate-limited.** Only search is.
- No published availability percentage and no published incident SLA. The supervision
  endpoints (P3 §3.9) are the sanctioned status source.

Because there is no committed SLA, **schedule margin is the only real protection**. Filing
on the deadline day with no buffer has no contractual remedy if the platform is down.

##### 7.6 Archival — a Mercans obligation

GIP-MDS keeps downloadable returns for **3 months** and then **purges without archiving**.
Tracking data persists 13 months; the returns themselves do not.

Must be archived on our side, per client per period:

- The **deposited NEODeS file** (what we filed)
- **AEE** (code 10) — proof of receipt
- **Certificat de conformité** (code 11) — proof of acceptance
- **CRM 94** rate history — the basis for every PAS deduction
- **CRM 34** as received, **and** the derived rate with its derivation
- **BIS** (code 20) and any anomaly reports
- All body CRMs relevant to the ten in-scope filings

Retention must meet the French payroll audit period, which is **longer than three months**
by a wide margin. This archive is the only evidence that a filing was made and accepted.

##### 7.7 Change management

The specifications version annually and mid-year:

| Artefact | Current | Cadence |
|----------|---------|---------|
| NEODeS cahier technique | **CT2026.1.2** (2025-12-24) | Annual major + point releases |
| NEOReS cahier technique | **CT2026.1.0** (2025-04-08) | 2026.1.1 and **2027.1.0** already announced |
| API implementation guide | **v6.8** (2026-07-06) | Roughly 2–4 revisions/year |
| OC CRM format | `V1.1.4` (2025-06-17) | Ad hoc |
| OC fiche de paramétrage | `R1.4.0` | Ad hoc |

**Standing actions**

1. Re-download the API guide and both CTs **quarterly**; diff against the copies in
   `integration-sources/netent-dsn/` (SHA-256 in `_source-manifest.md` makes this a
   one-command check).
2. Track the **NEOReS 2027.1.0** schemas — already published as forthcoming. The migration
   is known, not hypothetical.
3. The harmonised DSN format is **backward compatible**; integrate the newest and older
   returns keep parsing. NEOReS makes no such promise — check each release.
4. Watch the **DPAE-into-DSN** move, deferred to 1 January 2027 at the earliest. When it
   lands, `URSSAF-DPAE` collapses into this channel (see `INTERLINKAGES.md`).
5. Subscribe to the GIP-MDS change notices; the guide's own revision table shows behaviour
   changes shipped in point releases (e.g. CRM 62 `KO`→`ANO`, TLS withdrawal).

##### 7.8 Support and escalation

| Level | Route | Use for |
|-------|-------|---------|
| L1 | Knowledge base — `net-entreprises.custhelp.com` | Functional/business questions |
| L2 | `incidentDSN@gip-mds.fr` | API technical incidents, schema requests |
| L2 | `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr` | Charte, enrolment, environment registration |
| L3 | **0806 800 700** | Urgent, deadline-threatening |
| Postal | GIP-MDS, 4-14 rue Ferrus, 75014 Paris | Charte originals |

Before escalating: check supervision (P3 §3.9). If the platform reports `KO`, it is theirs;
if `OK`, it is ours.

##### 7.9 Runbook — monthly cycle

1. Payroll closes. Generate NEODeS per client.
2. **Pre-flight gate** (P6 §6.3). Assertion 2 — one `S20` — is non-negotiable.
3. Authenticate as concentrateur (service `98`).
4. Deposit per client with `declarant=` identification. Expect 200 + AEE; 422 = rejection.
5. Store the AEE. **Do not mark the filing complete.**
6. Harvest by flow id until **code 11** resolves.
   - `OK` → filed. Archive the certificat. **Now** mark complete.
   - `KO` → bilan d'anomalies. Correct and re-file, deadline permitting.
7. Sweep for body returns (concentrateur-scoped, port 8443, honouring `Expires`).
8. Route **CRM 94** into payroll before the next run. **Alert if absent.**
9. Route **CRM 34** through derivation, never verbatim.
10. Archive everything (§7.6).
11. Reconcile: every client has a code 11 `OK` and a CRM 94. Escalate exceptions before the
    next deadline, not after.


### NETENT-DSN / `INTERLINKAGES.md`

#### INTERLINKAGES
**FR · NETENT-DSN ↔ URSSAF-DPAE, DGFIP-EDI, and the FR dossier artefacts**

This file is the **only** place in the NETENT-DSN runbook set where other authorities are
discussed. P1–P7 are scoped to `NETENT-DSN` alone. Nothing here is a build instruction for
another authority — each has (or needs) its own runbook.

---

##### 1. Authority map

France's payroll reporting is **not** one channel. It is one dominant channel plus two
separate ones that cannot be folded into it.

| Authority | Channel | Filings | Runbook |
|-----------|---------|---------|---------|
| **`NETENT-DSN`** | REST API, net-entreprises.fr | 10 (see P1 §1.2) | **This set** |
| `URSSAF-DPAE` | REST API, `depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` | 1 — `FR-URSSAF-001` | Separate |
| `DGFIP-EDI` | EDIFACT via accredited partner | 1 — `FR-DGFIP-003` | Separate |

---

##### 2. NETENT-DSN ↔ URSSAF-DPAE

###### 2.1 The boundary, and why it is easy to get wrong

The NEODeS norm **defines a DPAE signal** — `S20.G00.05` carries a
*DSN Signal Déclaration Préalable à l'Embauche* nature (P4 §4.3). It is in the 2026 spec.
It is tempting to use.

> **Do not.** In 2026 the DPAE remains a **separate filing to URSSAF on its own channel**.
> The technical provision for it to become a DSN event signal exists, but production was
> deferred and is **not expected before 1 January 2027**.

`scope_manifest.json` records this independently: the DPAE "remains a SEPARATE filing"
whose move into the DSN "is deferred to 1 January 2027 at the earliest."

This is the one place where the NEODeS specification and the current legal reality diverge,
and where a build that follows the spec literally will file into the wrong channel. P4 §4.3
and the P6 §6.3 pre-flight (assertion 7) both guard it.

###### 2.2 What connects them operationally

| Link | Detail |
|------|--------|
| **Same event, two filings** | A hire triggers a DPAE (before work starts, ≤8 days prior) *and* subsequently appears in the monthly DSN. Both must fire. |
| **Ordering** | DPAE first — it is a *pre*-hire declaration. The employee then appears in the next monthly DSN with a `S21.G00.40` contract block. |
| **Shared identifiers** | SIRET and the employee's NIR are common to both. An identity defect surfaces in the DSN as a **BIS (code 20) `ANO`** (P5 §5.4), often originating upstream at the DPAE. |
| **Shared portal, different services** | net-entreprises.fr offers a **manual** DPAE upload as a portal alternative. Same portal, different channel — not the DSN API. |
| **Independent enrolment** | The DPAE API is restricted-access, granted by the producer (`api-dpae@acoss.fr`). A concentrateur charte for DSN grants **nothing** for DPAE. |

###### 2.3 Watch item

When the DPAE folds into the DSN (2027 at the earliest), `URSSAF-DPAE` collapses into this
channel and `FR-URSSAF-001` becomes an eleventh filing on NETENT-DSN. This is tracked as a
standing action in P7 §7.7(4). It will be a **channel migration**, not a format change.

---

##### 3. NETENT-DSN ↔ DGFIP-EDI

###### 3.1 Two completely different DGFiP obligations

The most common error in French payroll integration design is treating "DGFiP" as one
integration. It is two, and they share nothing:

| | **Withholding at source (PAS)** | **Taxe sur les salaires** |
|---|---|---|
| Filings | `FR-DGFIP-001` (out), `FR-DGFIP-002` (in) | `FR-DGFIP-003` |
| Channel | **Inside the DSN** — `NETENT-DSN` | **EDIFACT** via accredited partner — `DGFIP-EDI` |
| Forms | none (DSN blocks) | 2501-SD, 2502-SD |
| Direction | both | outbound only |
| Runbook | **This set** | Separate |

> `NETENT-DSN` carries **all** of the PAS obligation — the declaration outbound in the
> monthly DSN, and the **rate inbound as CRM 94**. `DGFIP-EDI` carries **none** of it. The
> landscape states this explicitly: DGFiP's withholding obligation "travels inside the DSN
> and is covered by NETENT-DSN."

###### 3.2 What connects them

| Link | Detail |
|------|--------|
| **Common base** | Both draw on the same payroll result, but on different aggregates: PAS on net taxable per employee; taxe sur les salaires on total remuneration at employer level. |
| **No data flows between them** | No return from one feeds the other. They are independent. |
| **Conditional vs unconditional** | The monthly DSN is **always** due. The taxe sur les salaires is due **only** from employers not liable to VAT on ≥90% of turnover — for anyone else the filing must be **suppressed, not filed nil**. |
| **Different rhythms** | DSN is monthly. Taxe sur les salaires is annual (<€4 000), quarterly (€4 000–10 000) or monthly (>€10 000), each with a closing 2502-SD. |
| **Different access model** | DSN: direct API, concentrateur charte. DGFIP-EDI: **DGFiP accepts files only from a partner it has accredited.** Mercans must gain accreditation or contract an accredited partner — a commercial dependency, not a technical one. |

###### 3.3 Relative risk

`NETENT-DSN` is high-volume, high-consequence, **and fully self-serviceable** — enrolment
is a charte and a form. `DGFIP-EDI` is low-volume, conditional, and **gated behind
third-party accreditation** with unverified terms. Its portal fallback (espace
professionnel / EFI) is a fully lawful channel, so it degrades to manual with no compliance
breach.

That asymmetry should drive sequencing: **build NETENT-DSN first**; it discharges ten of
the twelve integrable filings. `DGFIP-EDI` can run manually indefinitely without breach.
`URSSAF-DPAE` cannot — the DPAE is statutory and pre-hire — but it too has a lawful portal
fallback.

---

##### 4. Shared risks across all three

| Risk | NETENT-DSN | URSSAF-DPAE | DGFIP-EDI |
|------|-----------|-------------|-----------|
| Enrolment is a **human approval gate** with no published SLA | Yes (charte) | Yes (producer grants access) | Yes (accreditation) |
| Test environment terms **unverified** | Yes (P6 §6.2) | Yes | Yes |
| Lawful **portal fallback** exists | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Identity data (SIRET, NIR) shared | Yes | Yes | SIRET only |

**A defect in employee identity data propagates to all three.** Fix it once, upstream, in
the employee master — not per channel.

---

##### 5. Links into the rest of the FR dossier

| Artefact | Relationship to this runbook set |
|----------|----------------------------------|
| `FR-compliance/scope_manifest.json` | Source of the filing inventory, deadlines and the DPAE-deferral fact. P1 §1.2 and P7 §7.1 are derived from it. |
| `FR-compliance/03-statutory-integrations/integration-landscape.json` | The discovery output this set builds on. Channel, formats, constraints. |
| `FR-compliance/03-statutory-integrations/FR-SIR-INDEX-001-v1.0.docx` | One-row-per-authority index pointing here. |
| `FR-compliance/04-reports-spec/` | **Field-level** NEODeS rubrique specification. P4 is deliberately structural (§4 preamble); the rubrique enumeration belongs there. |
| `FR-compliance/05-data-dictionary/` | Payroll-field → NEODeS-block mapping. |
| `FR-compliance/02-wtc/` | Wage types feeding `S21.G00.51/52/54/78/81`. **P4 §4.9's "no negative gross" rule constrains the WTC**: corrections are regularisation blocks, not negative wage types. |
| `FR-compliance/01-ccg/` | Contribution and withholding rules whose *results* this channel transmits. |
| `FR-compliance/integration-sources/netent-dsn/` | The downloaded artefacts and `_source-manifest.md`. |

###### Cross-artefact obligations this runbook creates

1. **Report Spec / DD** must enumerate rubriques for every block marked ● in P4 §4.4.
2. **WTC** must not permit a negative gross wage type for a prior-period correction (P4 §4.9).
3. **CCG** must document that the PAS rate is **received**, not computed — its only source
   is CRM 94 (P5 §5.5).
4. **CCG / WTC** must document that the AT/MP rate needs **derivation** from CRM 34 where a
   reduction case applies (P5 §5.5).


### NETENT-DSN / `effort-estimates.md`

#### Effort Estimates
**FR · NETENT-DSN · GIP-MDS / net-entreprises.fr**

Basis: the specification set actually in hand (source manifest A1–A5). Estimates are for a
**concentrateur** implementation (P2 §2.1) serving multiple clients, régime général only.

**Unit:** person-days of a developer already fluent in REST + XML integration but **new to
DSN**. They exclude payroll-engine work — generating correct NEODeS *content* is
Report-Spec/DD/WTC scope, not integration scope (see `INTERLINKAGES.md` §5).

> **Confidence.** Build effort is estimated with reasonable confidence: the interface is
> fully specified and we hold the spec. **Lead times are not.** Three items are gated by
> third-party human decisions with no published SLA, and those dominate the schedule.

---

##### 1. Critical path is enrolment, not code

| Gate | Owner | Published SLA | Est. elapsed |
|------|-------|---------------|--------------|
| Charte concentrateur → GIP-MDS review → decision | GIP-MDS | **None** | **Unknown — 2–8 weeks assumed** |
| DSN registration effective | net-entreprises | D+1 stated | 1 day |
| EDITEUR environment registration | GIP-MDS | **None** | **Unknown — 1–3 weeks assumed** |
| Schema pack (XSD/XSL) acquisition | GIP-MDS / portal | **None** | Days, once an account exists |
| Client DSN registration + Charte Utilisateur | **Each client** | n/a | Per-client, onboarding-dependent |

> The 2–8 week and 1–3 week figures are **assumptions, not sourced facts.** Nothing in the
> public documentation states a turnaround. They are flagged as the largest schedule risk in
> this build. **Submit the charte on day 1**, in parallel with development, and resolve the
> conformance question of P6 §6.2 in the same email.

Development can proceed against the specification without EDITEUR access. **Only testing is
blocked.** Sequence accordingly.

---

##### 2. Build effort by phase

| Phase | Work | Days |
|-------|------|-----:|
| **P1** | Understand channel, filings, constraints (this runbook substitutes for most discovery) | 1 |
| **P2** | Credential store, encrypted-at-rest password, concentrateur `declarant=` header, token lifecycle, service-code config | 4 |
| **P3** | HTTP client: endpoint config per env, TLS + CA trust store, header handling, `Expires`/`Accept-Ranges` polling governor, **`WWW-Authenticate` realm parse**, retry policy by class, supervision poll | 8 |
| **P4** | NEODeS writer: ISO-8859-1, line syntax, block assembly, totals, **pre-flight gate (9 assertions)**, deposit call, 422 handling | 8 |
| **P5** | Returns: search-then-hypermedia-download, harmonised-format parser, **NEOReS parser (second format)**, code-11 acceptance logic, status vocabulary incl. `EN COURS`, **CRM 94 → payroll**, **CRM 34 derivation**, archive writer | 14 |
| **P6** | Test harness: 12 connectivity + 24 functional cases, EDITEUR runs, cutover rehearsal | 8 |
| **P7** | Scheduling (calendar + event-driven), alerting incl. the do-not-alert set, archival retention, runbook, change-watch | 6 |
| | **Build subtotal** | **49** |

###### Contingency

| Item | Days |
|------|-----:|
| Schema-pack integration once XSDs arrive (parser validation, possible rework) | 3 |
| Downstream return-host CA/reachability issues (10 distinct hosts, P3 §3.2) | 3 |
| First-contact defects in EDITEUR (spec-vs-behaviour divergence) | 4 |
| **Contingency subtotal** | **10** |

###### Total

| | Days |
|---|-----:|
| Build | 49 |
| Contingency | 10 |
| **Total engineering** | **59 person-days** |

Elapsed, assuming one developer and parallel enrolment: **12–16 weeks**, dominated by the
charte gate — **not** by the 59 days of work.

---

##### 3. Where the effort concentrates

**P5 is the largest phase (14 d), and that is correct.** The inbound leg is usually
underestimated because it looks like "just downloading files". It is not:

- **Two parser families** — harmonised DSN *and* NEOReS — plus body-specific formats
  (AGIRC-ARRCO XSD, OC CRM, BPIJ).
- **~40 return codes**, each with its own status vocabulary and its own quirks (CRM 62
  `ANO` not `KO`; CRM 93 absent on success; CRM 116 link-less when OK).
- **Two returns write into payroll**, one of which (CRM 34) needs a derivation rather than a
  post.
- **An archive obligation** created by the 3-month purge.

**P3 (8 d) is larger than a typical REST client** because the polling governor, the realm
parse and the retry-class policy are all mandatory correctness features, not niceties. The
account-lockout failure mode (P7 §7.3) makes the realm parse a go-live blocker.

**P4 (8 d) is modest** only because content generation is out of scope here. The pre-flight
gate is the valuable part; assertion 2 alone prevents the worst failure mode in the
interface.

---

##### 4. Cost of getting it wrong

Sizing the downside, to justify the test effort in P6:

| Defect | Consequence |
|--------|-------------|
| Return leg not built / silently stops | **CRM 94 never arrives.** PAS applied on stale rates for every client, every month. Tax under/over-withholding at scale, discovered late. |
| Filing closed on the AEE instead of code 11 | Rejected declarations look filed. Deadline missed with no warning. |
| Batch deposit (>1 `S20`) | Returns 200 + AEE. Fails asynchronously, after the deadline. |
| Naive retry on a credential 401 | **403 account block** — every client on that concentrateur credential goes down at once. |
| CRM 34 posted verbatim | Wrong AT/MP contribution for every reduced-rate employee. |
| Returns not archived within 3 months | Purged and unrecoverable. **No evidence a filing was accepted.** |

Each of these is covered by a specific test in P6 §6.5 (F2, F4, F10, F11, F24) and by a
specific alert in P7 §7.4. The test effort is proportionate to the exposure.

---

##### 5. Assumptions

1. **Concentrateur** model (P2 §2.1). Declarant-per-client would add per-client credential
   custody and roughly **+5 days**, plus ongoing operational load.
2. **Régime général only.** MSA/RA is descoped in `scope_manifest.json`. Adding RA is
   roughly **+6 days** (second host set, second auth portal, second service code).
3. **The ten in-scope filings of P1 §1.2.** CNAM PJ-IJ deposit (P4 §4.10) is excluded; it is
   a separate API with its own semantics — roughly **+5 days** if contracted.
4. **CIBTP / BPIJ / TPT** are either descoped or accepted as production-first (P6 §6.1). If
   they must be proven pre-production, that is not a cost — it is **not possible**, since no
   test environment exists.
5. NEODeS *content* correctness is delivered by the Report Spec, DD and WTC artefacts.
6. Archive storage exists as a platform capability; only the writer is costed here.

---

##### 6. Recommended sequence

1. **Day 1 — submit the charte concentrateur** and ask `exploitationDSN@gip-mds.fr` the
   conformance question (P6 §6.2). Everything else is downstream of this.
2. Register the triplet for DSN RG; request EDITEUR enrolment; request the schema pack.
3. Build P2 → P3 → P4 against the specification, offline.
4. Build P5 structurally; validate parsers when the XSDs arrive.
5. On EDITEUR access: run P6 §6.4 then §6.5. Keep F4, F11, F14.
6. Parallel-run one full cycle, reconciling **CRM 94** against the incumbent.
7. Cut over **immediately after** a filing deadline (P6 §6.6).

| **P2** | [`P2-enrolment-access-credentials.md`](P2-enrolment-access-credentials.md) | Declarant vs concentrateur, the email enrolment procedure, service codes 25/98, auth contract, the never-expiring token, TLS, support routing | BUILD-READY · 1 unverified |
| **P3** | [`P3-transport-interface-contract.md`](P3-transport-interface-contract.md) | 4 operations, endpoint table, the port-8443 rule, headers, HTTP semantics, 401 realms, retention, polling — every row tagged **[DPAE]** / **[DSN→]** / **[UNVERIFIED]** | BUILD-READY · 2 unverified |
| **P4** | [`P4-outbound-payload-format120.md`](P4-outbound-payload-format120.md) | Format 120 vs XML, the **complete 989-character record layout**, R.1221-1 mapping, reference tables, the 4-layer validation contract with every error code, deposit rules, the absent correction model, test mode | BUILD-READY · 2 gated |
| **P5** | [`P5-inbound-returns.md`](P5-inbound-returns.md) | The 4 return codes, retrieval sequence, the 4-gate acceptance chain, full error catalogue, the BIS, the 4 return scenarios, per-declaration reconciliation, statutory retention | BUILD-READY · 2 gated |
| **P6** | [`P6-test-conformance-cutover.md`](P6-test-conformance-cutover.md) | The single environment, 15-assertion pre-flight gate, 8 connectivity + 15 `TST` + 6 `RÉEL` tests, cutover with a lawful fallback, post-cutover watch | BUILD-READY · 1 unverified |
| **P7** | [`P7-run-operate.md`](P7-run-operate.md) | Event-driven schedule, 4 jobs, flow lifecycle, retry classes, alerting **and the do-not-alert set**, SLA, statutory archival, change management, per-hire runbook | BUILD-READY · SLA unverified |

##### Supporting

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| [`INTERLINKAGES.md`](INTERLINKAGES.md) | Cross-references to `NETENT-DSN` and `DGFIP-EDI`, and links into the rest of the FR dossier. **Only** file discussing other authorities. |
| [`effort-estimates.md`](effort-estimates.md) | 50 person-days standalone; **37.5 after NETENT-DSN**. 8–12 weeks elapsed, gated by enrolment. |
| [`../../integration-sources/urssaf-dpae/_source-manifest.md`](../../integration-sources/urssaf-dpae/_source-manifest.md) | Every downloaded artefact with SHA-256, source URL, and what was **not** obtainable |

---

##### Read this first

Four things about this integration are counter-intuitive and cause most failures:

1. **HTTP 200 is not a filing.** Deposit returns an AEE that attests only that the *flow* was
   received and parsed. The hire is declared when the **AR (code 41)** arrives carrying a
   populated **`R_DOSSIER`** — three asynchronous gates later. → P5 §5.3, P4 §4.9
2. **There is no correction model and no test environment.** A registered DPAE cannot be
   amended through this channel — re-sending earns code `98`. And testing happens on
   **production**, distinguished only by `C_VERSION = TST`. Quality is enforced in the
   pre-flight gate or not at all. → P4 §4.8, P6 §6.1, P6 §6.3
3. **Reconcile per declaration, never per file.** One deposit yields one AEE, a bilan
   d'anomalies covering some declarations, a certificat de conformité covering others, one
   AR, and conditionally a BIS. Nothing in that set is keyed to the file. → P5 §5.5
4. **The deadline cannot be caught up.** A DPAE not filed before the employee starts work is
   a breach no later action repairs (L.1221-10, R.1221-4). There is no monthly cycle to
   announce a silent failure — the `R_DOSSIER` alarm is the only signal. → P7 §7.1, §7.4

---

##### Source basis

| Artefact | Reference | Date |
|----------|-----------|------|
| API DPAE — Guide d'implémentation de l'API | **NAT/5492**, v1.1 | Oct 2021 |
| L'API DPAE régime général — principes de fonctionnement | **NAT/6804** | Sept 2025 |
| API DPAE — Questions/Réponses | **NAT/5493** | Oct 2023 |
| DPAE Format 120 — dessin d'enregistrement (CFT) | **v5.4** | 30/09/2014 |
| DPAE Format 120 — dessin d'enregistrement (EMAIL) | **v5.3** | 13/06/2013 |
| Services de prévention et de santé au travail | open data `sst_dpae`, 409 rows | live |

Plus the URSSAF SST lists, the Cerfa 14738*01 paper form, and two URSSAF documentation pages.
All downloaded, hashed and listed in the source manifest.

**Required cross-reference:** the *DSN — Guide d'implémentation de l'API* **v6.8**
(2026-07-06), already held under `integration-sources/netent-dsn/`. NAT/5492 names it as
reference document [2] and delegates the entire machine-mode interface to it. P3 tags every
row taken from it as **[DSN→]** and flags the version-drift risk.

Legal instruments cited by article: Code du travail **L.1221-10**, **R.1221-1 to R.1221-9**,
**R.1221-12**, **R.1221-16 to R.1221-18**, **D.1221-18**, **D.1221-19**.

##### Acquisition compliance

- No login, account creation, certificate use, or CAPTCHA interaction.
- **No live service endpoint was called.** Every `depot.`, `consultation.` and
  `mon.urssaf.fr/authentifier_dpae` address in P2/P3 was read out of the specification PDFs.
  One host later identified as a service host (`telechargement.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr`) was
  probed once before the endpoint table was read; **the TCP connection never established**
  and no HTTP request was transmitted. Disclosed in full in the source manifest.
- Downloads limited to the public `www.urssaf.fr` documentation tree, the public
  `www2.due.urssaf.fr/static_declarant/` specification tree, and `open.urssaf.fr`.

##### Open items

| Ref | Item | Type | Why open |
|-----|------|------|----------|
| P4 §4.5 | `C_URSSAF` code table (Annexe II.1) | `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` | Host `fichierdirect.declaration.urssaf.fr` would not accept a connection from this environment |
| P5 §5.6 | BIS (code 20) format, "Doc v1.3" | `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` | Named by version only in NAT/6804; no public URL |
| P5 §5.6 | `dsn_bilans_v01r06.xsd` | `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` | net-entreprises documentation area, behind declarant login. Same artefact family already gated in NETENT-DSN P5 §5.8. |
| P4 §4.1 | `DUE_Upload.xsd` | `[[NEEDS-ARTEFACT]]` | Instance shown in NAT/5492 with six namespaces; no schema URL published |
| P4 §4.3 | `C_APPLICATION` value for the API channel | **UNVERIFIED** | v5.3 requires `"DUE "`, v5.4 requires `"CFT"`; no API artefact names a value |
| P3 §3.8 | TLS version and CA chain for the DPAE hosts | **UNVERIFIED** | Not published; the DSN CA annex covers other hosts |
| P3 §3.1 | Per-declarant returns-search endpoint | **UNVERIFIED** | Capability confirmed in the FAQ; no URL given |
| P3 §3.6 | DPAE `WWW-Authenticate` realm wording | **UNVERIFIED** | Realm literals are DSN-side |
| P5 §5.6 | AR (code 41) exact API-channel layout | **UNVERIFIED** | Derivable from format 120; byte-identity to the CFT-era AR unconfirmed |
| P2 §2.2 | Abonné-registration wording in the FAQ | **UNVERIFIED** | Apparent typographical negation in the source |
| P6 §6.2 | Whether a conformance gate exists | **UNVERIFIED** | Not established from public pages. **Not a claim of absence.** |
| P7 §7.5 | SLA, availability, rate limits | **UNVERIFIED** | data.gouv.fr: *"Non communiqué"* |

**Only two block go-live** (the `C_URSSAF` table; the `C_APPLICATION` value). Nine of the
twelve can be closed by one well-composed enrolment email; two more close on the first test
run. Resolution path and sequencing: `effort-estimates.md` §5 and §7.

##### Scope boundary

Scoped to `URSSAF-DPAE` **only**. `NETENT-DSN` and `DGFIP-EDI` appear in `INTERLINKAGES.md`
as cross-references and nowhere else. Régime agricole (MSA) is out of scope — the MSA DPAE
protocol document present in the dossier describes a different deposit point and is not
referenced by P1–P7.

##### Correction recorded against the dossier

`scope_manifest.json` describes `FR-URSSAF-001` as filed *"via net-entreprises.fr"* in format
*"XML"*. Both need refining: the API deposit point is **URSSAF's own host**
`depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` (net-entreprises' NetDPAE is a *portal* alternative), and the
payload is **TXT format 120 or XML**, with the flat variant recommended. Filing, authority,
frequency and direction are unchanged. Recorded at P1 §1.7 and INTERLINKAGES §5.


### URSSAF-DPAE / `P1-authority-channel-profile.md`

#### P1 — Authority & Channel Profile
**FR · URSSAF-DPAE · URSSAF — Déclaration Préalable À l'Embauche deposit point**

Status: **BUILD-READY** · Mode: structural · Directions: outbound + inbound
Source basis: API DPAE guide d'implémentation NAT/5492 v1.1, principes de fonctionnement
NAT/6804 (Sept 2025), FAQ NAT/5493 (Oct 2023), format 120 dessin d'enregistrement v5.4
Scope: `URSSAF-DPAE` only. NETENT-DSN and DGFIP-EDI are cross-referenced in `INTERLINKAGES.md`.

---

##### 1.1 What this authority is

URSSAF (Unions de Recouvrement des cotisations de Sécurité Sociale et d'Allocations
Familiales) is the **receiving authority in its own right** for the pre-hire declaration. It
is not a routing platform acting for others — unlike GIP-MDS on the DSN channel, URSSAF
levies, receives and adjudicates here.

That said, the declaration it receives is **multi-purpose by law**. Code du travail R.1221-2
makes one DPAE discharge five separate administrative acts at once:

1. employer registration (*demande d'immatriculation de l'employeur*),
2. employee registration with the sickness fund where the employee has no NIR,
3. affiliation to unemployment insurance,
4. enrolment with the occupational health service (SPST),
5. the request for the pre-employment medical examination.

Architecturally this matters: **the payload is small but its downstream fan-out is wide.**
A malformed `C_MT_DCL` does not merely fail a field check — it fails to enrol the new hire
with an occupational health service. A missing NIR triggers a real CPAM registration
request. The declaration is an instruction to four bodies, not a form.

##### 1.2 Filings carried by this channel

**One** in-scope filing from `scope_manifest.json` rides this integration:

| Filing ID | Direction | Frequency | Carried how |
|-----------|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| `FR-URSSAF-001` | outbound | per hire | Format-120 flat record (990 bytes) or `FR_DUE_Upload` XML, deposited on the DPAE deposit service |
| *(same filing)* | **inbound** | per deposit, then J+1 | AEE / avis de rejet, certificat de conformité or bilan d'anomalies, the AR (retour 41), and conditionally the BIS (retour 20) |

One filing, but **both directions are load-bearing** — see §1.5(2).

###### Statutory deadline

| Rule | Source |
|------|--------|
| Filed **at the earliest 8 days** before the foreseeable hire date | Code du travail **R.1221-4** |
| Filed **before the employee begins work** — there is no grace period after | Code du travail **L.1221-10** |
| Electronic filing **mandatory** for employers who filed **> 50** DPAE in the preceding calendar year | Code du travail **D.1221-18** |
| Penalty for breaching the electronic obligation: **0.5 % of the monthly social security ceiling per employee** | Code du travail **D.1221-19** |

`scope_manifest.json` records the same window independently: *"Due within the 8 days
preceding the start and in any event before the employee begins work."*

For a payroll service provider the D.1221-18 threshold is not a corner case — **any client
with meaningful hiring volume is legally obliged to file electronically**, which is what
makes this integration a compliance requirement rather than an efficiency project.

##### 1.3 Receiving bodies behind the single deposit

URSSAF is the addressee, but under R.1221-2 and R.1221-17/18 the data reaches:

| Body | What it receives | Visible to us how |
|------|------------------|-------------------|
| **URSSAF** | The declaration itself; employer registration where new | AR (retour 41) |
| **CPAM** | Registration request for an employee with no NIR (`IMMA` formality) | Formality flag in the AR, Catégorie VI |
| **CNAV / SNGI** | NIR verification against the national identity register (R.1221-18) | **BIS (retour 20)** where identity diverges |
| **SPST** (occupational health service) | Enrolment and medical-examination request (`MT` formality) | Formality flag in the AR |
| **France Travail** | Identity, hire date, contract nature and duration, trial period (R.1221-17) | Not returned to the employer |
| **CRAM** | DADS pre-establishment data (`CERTIF` formality) | Formality flag in the AR |

The four formalities `CERTIF`, `IMMA`, `MT`, `PMF5` are positioned **by the system**, not by
the declarant (A4, Catégorie VI). Since format 120 the employer **can no longer steer them**
— they are derived from the declared data (A10). Omitting the NIR *is* the way to request a
CPAM registration; it is not an error.

##### 1.4 Channel classification

| Property | Value | Source |
|----------|-------|--------|
| Channel type | REST over HTTPS, machine-to-machine | A1 §2, A3 |
| Protocol | HTTP/1.1 | A1 §2.1.1 request example |
| Style | Request/response, plus **hypermedia** for downloads — the `<url>` element added at A1 v1.1 means download URLs are *returned*, not constructed | A1 §"API DPAE", A3 step 4 |
| Outbound payload | Format 120 fixed-length text (990 bytes/record) **or** `FR_DUE_Upload` XML, `Content-Type: text/plain`, `Content-Encoding: gzip` | A1 §2.1.1, A2 |
| XML encoding | **ISO-8859-1** (declared in A1's instance) | A1 §2.1.1 |
| Inbound payload | Returns **10** and **11** in DSN format; returns **20** and **41** in DPAE-specific formats | A3 |
| Authentication | Urssaf-en-ligne portal credential quadruplet → token, `Authorization: DSNLogin` | A3, A2, DSN guide §2.3 |
| SOAP/WSDL | **None.** REST only. | A1, A3 throughout |
| Availability | Not published. data.gouv.fr: *"Non communiqué"* | **UNVERIFIED** |
| Separate test environment | **None.** Testing is done on production with a test indicator. | A3 |
| Régime scope | **Régime général only.** The régime agricole DPAE is an MSA channel with its own protocol. | A1/A2 titles ("régime général") |

**Régime scope:** this build targets **RG only**, consistent with the FR dossier descoping
MSA. The MSA DPAE protocol document present in the dossier
(`00-official-sources/pdfs/dpae_protocole_msa_v7.pdf`) is **not** in scope for this runbook
and describes a different deposit point.

##### 1.5 Constraints that shape the design

Four constraints are load-bearing and must reach the solution design, not just the runbook:

1. **Enrolment differs by operating model, and the choice is not reversible cheaply.**
   A declarant or tiers déclarant needs **no DPAE-specific registration at all** — only an
   Urssaf-en-ligne subscriber whose credentials feed the auth flow (A3). A **concentrateur
   must register separately by email**, *even if it is already registered for the API DSN*
   (A3, verbatim). These are different service codes (25 vs 98), different token semantics,
   and different URL forms (port 8443). P2 §2.1 forces this decision first.

2. **The acknowledgement is not the acceptance, and neither is the AR the whole story.**
   Deposit returns an **AEE** (retour 10) synchronously. That only means the pre-controls
   passed. Norm controls then produce a **certificat de conformité or a bilan d'anomalies**
   (retour 11). Business controls then produce the **AR / certificat de conformité des DPAE**
   (retour 41), which is the document carrying `R_DOSSIER` — and *only* a populated
   `R_DOSSIER` proves the DPAE was registered (A2, A4). A build that treats HTTP 200 as
   "declared" will report success for hires that were never registered. **This is the single
   most dangerous property of the channel.** See P5 §5.3.

3. **Legal proof lives on the inbound leg.** R.1221-7 makes the acknowledgement the *proof*
   of declaration, contestable for two working days and issued within five. R.1221-9
   obliges the employer to hand the employee a copy of the declaration or the
   acknowledgement. R.1221-12 lets a labour inspector demand it. The return leg is therefore
   **evidentiary, not operational** — it must be captured and archived, not just parsed.
   See P7 §7.6.

4. **Failure is per-declaration, not per-file — in both directions.** A file may carry many
   hires; *"Seule la DPAE en anomalie est rejetée"* (A3). Conversely a file that fails
   pre-controls is rejected whole. A build that reconciles at file granularity will
   silently drop individual hires. Reconciliation must be **per declaration**, keyed on the
   employee and the hire datetime. See P5 §5.5.

Additionally: **there is no "annule et remplace".** A2 states the cancel-and-replace
function is *not necessary* for DPAE and that cinematic controls do not apply. A duplicate
is simply rejected with code 98. Correction of an already-registered DPAE is **not a
channel operation** — see P4 §4.8.

##### 1.6 Deposit granularity

Unlike the DSN, **multi-declaration deposit is supported and expected.** A3: the deposited
file *"peut contenir plusieurs embauches pour un même établissement"*, up to **100 MB** per
deposit. A2 recommends ordering the DPAE of one employer consecutively within a
multi-employer file.

**Archives are not accepted on the API leg.** A1 §2.1.1 is explicit: *"Les archives ne sont
pas acceptées."* A2 describes archive handling (gz/tar/zip/gzip, one nesting level) at
length — but that is the **file-upload** path on the portal, not the API. Reading A2's
archive rules as API behaviour is an easy and expensive mistake. See P4 §4.7.

##### 1.7 Confidence

**High for the interface and the payload; medium for the return payload layouts.**

Every statement above is taken from URSSAF's own published documentation or from the Code du
travail. The items carried as unverified or gated are:

- the **layouts of returns 20 and 41** — described but not specified in any obtained artefact
  (P5 §5.6),
- the **XML schema** `DUE_Upload.xsd` — no public URL (P4 §4.4),
- the **`C_URSSAF` code table** (Annexe II.1) — its host is unreachable from here (P4 §4.5),
- **SLA, rate limits and TLS certificate authorities** — not published (P3 §3.8, P7 §7.5).

None blocks structural design. All are enumerated with a resolution path in
`effort-estimates.md` §6.

###### One correction to the dossier's own scope record

`scope_manifest.json` records `FR-URSSAF-001` as going *"to URSSAF, via net-entreprises.fr"*
in format **XML**. Both halves need refining, and the runbook proceeds on the corrected
reading:

| Manifest says | Reality per A1/A2/A3 | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| via `net-entreprises.fr` | The **API** deposit point is URSSAF's own host `depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr`, and authentication is against `mon.urssaf.fr`. net-entreprises' *NetDPAE* is a **portal** alternative on the same declaration, not the API path. | Endpoint tables in P3 are URSSAF hosts. A build pointed at net-entreprises would not reach this API at all. |
| format `XML` | **TXT (format 120) or XML.** A2: *"Format 120 pour la DPAE et l'AR (format TXT ou XML)"*. | P4 recommends **format 120 flat** as the primary build target — see P4 §4.1 for why. |

This is a documentation refinement, not a scope change: the filing, authority, frequency and
direction are unchanged.


### URSSAF-DPAE / `P2-enrolment-access-credentials.md`

#### P2 — Enrolment, Access & Credentials
**FR · URSSAF-DPAE**

Status: **BUILD-READY** · 1 unverified item, 0 gated artefacts
Source basis: A3 (FAQ NAT/5493), A1 (guide NAT/5492), A2 (fonctionnement NAT/6804),
DSN API guide v6.8 for the auth request shape it defers to

---

##### 2.1 Decide the operating model first

Everything downstream — enrolment, credentials, URL form, token semantics, error handling —
forks on one question, and the fork is visible in a single sentence of A3:

> *"Un concentrateur doit s'inscrire à l'API DPAE même s'il est déjà inscrit à l'API DSN."*

| | **Déclarant / tiers déclarant** | **Concentrateur** |
|---|---|---|
| Who this is | An employer, or an agent filing under each client's own portal identity | A software provider filing on behalf of many declarants under **its own** identity |
| DPAE-specific registration | **None required.** A3: *"L'API DPAE ne nécessite pas d'inscription spécifique."* | **Required**, by email, even if already a DSN concentrateur |
| What must pre-exist | At least one subscriber (*abonné*) registered to an Urssaf en ligne online service | A concentrateur SIRET **and** an abonné registered on Urssaf en ligne with the same identifiers |
| Service code | **25** | **98** |
| SIRET used to obtain the token | The declarant's | The **concentrateur's** |
| SIRET used on business calls | (implicit in the token) | The **declarant's**, passed per call |
| Port | default (443) | **8443** on deposit, consultation and download — *not* on authentication |
| Credential custody | One password **per client**, held by us | **One password total** |

**Recommendation for Mercans: concentrateur.**

The reasoning is the same as on the DSN channel but sharper here. Under the declarant model
we would hold, rotate and recover an Urssaf-en-ligne password *for every client*, and A2 is
explicit that this password is the client's ordinary portal password — the same one that
opens their contribution account. That is a credential-custody liability out of proportion
to a pre-hire declaration. Under the concentrateur model we hold one.

The countervailing cost is honest and should be stated: the concentrateur registration is a
**human email approval with no published SLA** (§2.2), and it is a *second* approval even
where the DSN concentrateur charte is already signed. Budget it as a serial dependency, not
a parallel one — see `effort-estimates.md` §1.

> **Do not assume DSN enrolment carries over.** It does not, in either direction. A3 says so
> in as many words. This is the most likely planning error on this integration.

##### 2.2 Concentrateur enrolment procedure

The whole procedure, verbatim from A3:

| Step | Action |
|---|---|
| 1 | Ensure an *abonné* is registered on the Urssaf en ligne online service, **with the same identifiers** that will be declared below. A3: *"Cet abonné concentrateur ne doit être inscrit sur le service en ligne de l'Urssaf avec les mêmes identifiants."* |
| 2 | Send an email to **`api-dpae@acoss.fr`** with the subject line **`Inscription Concentrateur`** |
| 3 | The body must carry exactly four items: **Siret du concentrateur**, **Nom de l'abonné**, **Prénom de l'abonné**, **Adresse courriel de l'abonné** |
| 4 | Await confirmation. **No response time is published.** |

> **On step 1's wording.** A3's sentence contains what reads as a typographical negation
> (*"ne doit être inscrit … avec les mêmes identifiants"*). Read against the rest of the
> document — which requires the auth flow to carry a registered abonné's credentials, and
> which states the concentrateur SIRET is used to obtain the token — the only coherent
> reading is **must be registered with those same identifiers**. The runbook proceeds on
> that reading and flags it: confirm in the enrolment email rather than infer.
> **UNVERIFIED — wording ambiguity in the source.**

There is **no form, no charte, no signature, and no portal page** for this. That is a genuine
simplification relative to the DSN concentrateur route, not a gap in our research: A3 is the
authority's own published answer to exactly this question, and it describes an email.

###### Déclarant / tiers déclarant enrolment

There is nothing to do beyond having an Urssaf en ligne account. A3: *"L'API DPAE ne
nécessite pas d'inscription spécifique. En revanche, il est nécessaire d'avoir au moins un
abonné inscrit à un des services en ligne de l'Urssaf."*

The subscriber's surname and first name — needed for the auth flow — are readable at
**Mon profil → Gérer mes coordonnées** on Urssaf en ligne (A2, A3). They are *not* freely
chosen: they must match the registration exactly. A2 raises this as a *point d'attention*,
which means it is a known support driver.

##### 2.3 Service codes

| Code | Meaning | Who sets it |
|------|---------|-------------|
| **25** | Déclarant / tiers déclarant | A3 |
| **98** | Concentrateur | A3 |

These are the DSN service codes reused unchanged (the DSN guide gives 25 = DSN Régime
Général, 98 = Concentrateur DSN). **The number is the same; the entitlement is not.** Being
entitled to service 25 for the DSN grants nothing for the DPAE — the distinguishing element
is the authentication *endpoint*, `/authentifier_dpae` (§2.4), not the code.

There is no published DPAE analogue of the DSN test-environment codes (97 / 94). Consistent
with there being **no separate test environment** at all — P6 §6.1.

##### 2.4 Authentication request contract

A1 gives the endpoint. It gives no request body, because it defers to the DSN guide
(reference document [2]) for the machine-mode interface. The shape below is therefore
**DSN-guide-derived and marked as such**; the endpoint and the DPAE-specific path are from
A1/A3.

| Element | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Endpoint | `https://mon.urssaf.fr/authentifier_dpae` | **A1 §"Table d'adressage des services"**, corroborated by A3 |
| Method | `POST` | DSN guide §3.1 |
| Port | default (443) — **the 8443 rule does not apply to authentication** | A1, explicit |
| `Content-Type` | `application/xml` | DSN guide §3.1.1 |
| Compression | **Not applicable.** The DSN guide exempts the authentication services from the gzip obligation. | DSN guide §7.4 |
| Body | `<identifiants>` with `siret`, `nom`, `prenom`, `motdepasse`, `service` | DSN guide §3.1.1 |
| Response | HTTP 200, `Content-Type: application/octet-stream`, body **is** the base64 token; `Expires` header carries expiry | DSN guide §3.1.3 |

```http
POST /authentifier_dpae HTTP/1.1
Host: mon.urssaf.fr
User-Agent: Client-DSN (<logiciel>/<version>; Mercans)
Content-Type: application/xml
Content-Length: ...

<identifiants>
   <siret>12345678901234</siret>
   <nom>NOM-ABONNE</nom>
   <prenom>Prenom-Abonne</prenom>
   <motdepasse>...</motdepasse>
   <service>98</service>
</identifiants>
```

> **Note the endpoint shape.** DSN authentication is `POST /authentifier/1.0/` — a versioned
> resource. DPAE authentication is `POST /authentifier_dpae` — **unversioned, and a
> different path idiom**. Do not construct it by pattern from the DSN. It is given
> literally by A1 and A3 and must be used literally.

###### The four credential elements

A2 names them precisely: *Siret, nom, prénom et mot de passe* of the Urssaf-en-ligne
subscriber. Two design consequences:

1. **The password travels in clear inside the request body.** TLS is the only protection.
   The DSN guide requires it to be encrypted at rest in the payroll software; treat that as
   binding here. There is no OAuth2, no client-credentials grant, no API key, no mTLS.
2. **Surname and first name are part of the credential.** A subscriber changing their name
   on the portal breaks authentication. This is not hypothetical — A2 flags it as a
   *point d'attention* and tells declarants where to look the values up.

##### 2.5 Token handling

| Property | DPAE | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Transport | `Authorization` HTTP header on every business call | A3, DSN guide §2.3.1 |
| Scheme | `Authorization: DSNLogin jeton=<token>` (declarant) / `DSNLogin concentrateur=<token>` plus a base64 `declarant=<siret>;<nom>;<prenom>` element (concentrateur) | DSN guide §2.3.1, §2.4 |
| **Lifetime** | **Unlimited.** A3, verbatim: *"Celui-ci est toujours valide et n'a pas de durée d'utilisation."* | **A3** |
| Renewal | Not required by expiry | A3 |
| On HTTP 401 | Re-authenticate and replay the call once | DSN guide §2.3.1 |

> **This is a real divergence from the DSN, in our favour — and a trap.** The DSN guide
> describes a session-limited token with an `Expires` header; A3 states the DPAE token never
> expires. Design for **both**: cache the token indefinitely (do not re-authenticate per
> call — that would send the password on every request), but keep the 401-triggered
> re-authentication path live and tested. An unlimited token is a policy statement, not a
> guarantee, and it is also a **long-lived secret** that must be stored as one.

The concentrateur token is obtained with the **concentrateur's** SIRET; the declarant on
whose behalf a call is made is then identified per call by SIRET/nom/prénom (A3, DSN guide
§2.4). One token, many declarants.

##### 2.6 Certificates and TLS

| Item | Status |
|------|--------|
| Transport | HTTPS on every service. Not optional. |
| Client certificate | **Not required.** No artefact mentions mTLS. |
| Server CA chain for `mon.urssaf.fr`, `depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr`, `consultation.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr`, `telechargement.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` | **UNVERIFIED — not published.** The DSN guide's CA annex enumerates authorities for `*.net-entreprises.fr` and partner hosts; it says nothing about URSSAF's DPAE hosts. |
| Minimum TLS version | **UNVERIFIED.** The DSN guide records TLS 1.0/1.1 withdrawal for net-entreprises services; no DPAE statement exists. |

**Do not resolve this by probing the endpoints.** Establish it during enrolment
correspondence with `api-dpae@acoss.fr`, or at the first authorised connectivity test on the
live service once access is granted (P6 §6.3). Assume TLS 1.2+ and a publicly-trusted CA;
verify before go-live. Explicitly **do not** disable strict TLS verification as a
workaround — the DSN guide offers that as a convenience for its own hosts, and it is the
wrong trade here where the request body carries a portal password.

##### 2.7 Support channels

| Need | Channel | Source |
|------|---------|--------|
| Concentrateur registration; API access questions | **`api-dpae@acoss.fr`** | A3, data.gouv.fr |
| Urssaf en ligne account, subscriber identity, password | The employer's own Urssaf, via Urssaf en ligne | A2 |
| BIS (retour 20) content — unidentified employees | **Carsat, Cnav or CGSS** | A2, explicit |
| Business questions on the DPAE obligation | urssaf.fr DPAE pages | A11 |

Note the third row. A2 routes BIS support **away from URSSAF** to the pension/identity side.
A support runbook that sends every DPAE question to `api-dpae@acoss.fr` will stall on
identity anomalies, which are the most common non-trivial return (P5 §5.4).

##### 2.8 Access checklist

| # | Item | Owner | Blocking? |
|---|------|-------|-----------|
| 1 | Decide declarant vs concentrateur (§2.1) — **recommend concentrateur** | Solution design | **Yes — blocks everything** |
| 2 | Register a Mercans abonné on an Urssaf en ligne online service | Ops | Yes |
| 3 | Record that abonné's exact `nom` / `prénom` from *Mon profil → Gérer mes coordonnées* | Ops | Yes |
| 4 | Email `api-dpae@acoss.fr`, subject `Inscription Concentrateur`, with SIRET + nom + prénom + email | Ops | **Yes — no published SLA** |
| 5 | In the same email, ask the two questions the documentation does not answer: (a) the TLS/CA requirement for the DPAE hosts, (b) confirmation of the step-1 registration reading (§2.2) | Ops | No — but cheap, and closes both open items |
| 6 | Store the password in the secret manager; store the token as a **long-lived** secret, not a cache entry | Platform | Yes |
| 7 | Confirm the port-8443 rule is implemented for deposit / consultation / download and **not** for authentication | Build | Yes |
| 8 | Set the `User-Agent` to `Client-DSN (<logiciel>/<version>; Mercans)` — **mandatory for concentrateurs** | Build | Yes |

Items 1–4 are the critical path. Item 4 is the only one whose duration we cannot estimate
from published material.


### URSSAF-DPAE / `P3-transport-interface-contract.md`

#### P3 — Transport & Interface Contract
**FR · URSSAF-DPAE**

Status: **BUILD-READY** · 2 unverified items
Source basis: A1 §"Adresse des services" and §2.1; A3 call scenario; DSN API guide v6.8 for
every element A1 delegates to it

---

##### 3.0 How to read this phase

A1 is a **delta document**. It says so:

> *"Le mode API DPAE est entièrement compatible avec le mode API disponible pour la DSN. …
> Les modifications par rapport au document édité par le GIP-MDS pour la DSN ne portent pas
> sur les interfaces du mode machine mais sur les particularités d'adressage et d'éléments
> échangés."*

So the contract below has two provenances, and every row is tagged:

| Tag | Meaning |
|-----|---------|
| **[DPAE]** | Stated in A1, A2 or A3 — an URSSAF statement about the DPAE |
| **[DSN→]** | Not stated for the DPAE; taken from the DSN API guide under A1's blanket delegation |
| **[UNVERIFIED]** | Neither source speaks. Not a claim of absence. |

**[DSN→] rows carry version risk.** A1 v1.1 is a 2015–2017 document reissued in 2021; the
DSN guide is at v6.8 (2026). Features the DSN has gained since — `/authentifier/2.0/`,
`/deposer-dsn/2.0/` with JSON responses, the "jeton V2" vocabulary, TPT and PJ-IJ services —
**must not be assumed present here**. §3.2 marks the version boundary explicitly.

##### 3.1 Service inventory

Four services. That is the whole API.

| # | Service | Method | Resource | Direction | Tag |
|---|---------|--------|----------|-----------|-----|
| 1 | Authentication | `POST` | `/authentifier_dpae` | — | **[DPAE]** A1, A3 |
| 2 | Deposit a DPAE flow | `POST` | `/deposer-dsn/1.0/` | outbound | **[DPAE]** A1 §2.1, A3 |
| 3 | List returns for a flow | `GET` | `/lister-retours-flux/1.0/{idFlux}` | inbound | **[DPAE]** A3 |
| 4 | Download one return | `GET` | `/telecharger-retour/1.0/{idFlux}/{idRetour}` | inbound | **[DPAE]** A3 |

> **The deposit resource is literally `/deposer-dsn/`.** A1 anticipates the disbelief and
> answers it in a call-out box: *"Le nom de la ressource est bien /deposer-dsn, même dans le
> contexte dpae."* Do not "correct" it to `/deposer-dpae/`. There is no such resource.

###### Services the DSN has that the DPAE does not

| DSN service | DPAE status |
|---|---|
| `/lister-retours-declarant/1.0/{from}[/{to}]` — search by declarant and time range | **Documented as a capability, not as an endpoint.** A3 answers "yes, returns are consultable per declarant, and as in DSN one can search by declarant and period" but gives **no URL**. Treat the endpoint as **[UNVERIFIED]**; build on the per-flow path (#3) and confirm at enrolment. |
| `/consulter-depots/…` — deposit consultation | Not mentioned in any DPAE artefact. **[UNVERIFIED]** |
| `/deposer-dsn/2.0/`, `/authentifier/2.0/` (JSON) | Not mentioned. **Assume absent.** Build for the XML 1.0 responses. |
| Fiches de paramétrage OC, BPIJ, PJ-IJ, TPT | Not applicable — DSN-only flows. |

**Design consequence.** Because per-declarant search is unconfirmed, the integration must
**retain `idFlux` durably**, not treat it as a transient. It is the only proven handle on a
deposit's returns. See P5 §5.2 and P7 §7.6.

##### 3.2 Endpoint addressing

Base-URL table, verbatim from A1's *Table d'adressage des services*:

| Service | Base URL | Port (declarant) | Port (**concentrateur**) |
|---------|----------|------------------|--------------------------|
| Authentication | `https://mon.urssaf.fr/authentifier_dpae` | 443 | **443** — the 8443 rule explicitly does **not** apply |
| Deposit | `https://depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` | 443 | **8443** |
| Returns — list | `https://consultation.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` | 443 | **8443** |
| Returns — download | `https://telechargement.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` | 443 | **8443** |

A1's own call-out: *"Les concentrateurs doivent préciser le port 8443 dans l'url des services
de dépôt, consultation et téléchargement (pas pour le service d'authentification)."*

Full URLs, from A3's worked scenario:

```
1. POST   https://mon.urssaf.fr/authentifier_dpae
2. POST   https://depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr/deposer-dsn/1.0/
3. GET    https://consultation.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr/lister-retours-flux/1.0/{idFlux}
4. GET    https://telechargement.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr/telecharger-retour/1.0/{idFlux}/{idRetour}
```

As a concentrateur, rows 2–4 become `https://<host>:8443/...`; row 1 does not.

> **Wrong port is a `403`, not a connection error.** The DSN guide's status matrix assigns
> 403 to *"Le concentrateur tente d'accéder à l'API par un port différent de 8443"* — a
> live, well-formed HTTPS response that looks like an entitlement problem. If a concentrateur
> integration gets 403 on deposit while authentication succeeds, **check the port before
> escalating to `api-dpae@acoss.fr`.** [DSN→], but the port rule itself is [DPAE].

###### Environments

**There is one environment: production.** No `.test`, no `EDITEUR`, no sandbox host is
published for the DPAE. Testing is performed against the production endpoints with a test
indicator inside the payload — see P4 §4.6 and P6 §6.1. This is a property of the channel,
not a gap in acquisition: A3 answers the question *"Comment réaliser un dépôt de test ?"*
directly and describes production.

###### Hosts NOT to build against

| Host | Why it appears | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `www.net-entreprises.fr` / *NetDPAE* | The portal alternative for manual DPAE (A2) | Not the API. Fallback only — P6 §6.6 |
| `www.due.urssaf.fr`, `www2.due.urssaf.fr` | Legacy DUE portal and the static documentation tree the format specs live on | Documentation only |
| `embauche@due.fr` | The **closed** email transmission channel (A5, A10) | Ended 1 July 2022. Must not be designed for. |
| CFT flow | The **closed** file-transfer channel (A4) | Ended end-2022. Must not be designed for. |
| MSA deposit point | Régime agricole DPAE | Out of scope (P1 §1.4) |

##### 3.3 Request headers

| Header | Value | Applies to | Tag |
|--------|-------|------------|-----|
| `Authorization` | `DSNLogin jeton=<token>` (declarant) · `DSNLogin concentrateur=<token>` + base64 `declarant=<siret>;<nom>;<prenom>` (concentrateur) | 2, 3, 4 | **[DPAE]** A3 ("Toutes les URL … doivent porter un jeton en entête") + **[DSN→]** for the scheme |
| `Content-Type` | `application/xml` on auth; `text/plain` on deposit | 1 / 2 | **[DPAE]** A1 §2.1.1 |
| `Content-Encoding` | `gzip` | 2 | **[DPAE]** A1 §2.1.1 shows it in the request example |
| `Accept-Encoding` | must contain `gzip`, or be absent | 2, 3, 4 | **[DSN→]** §7.4 |
| `User-Agent` | `Client-DSN (<logiciel>/<version>; Mercans)` — **mandatory for concentrateurs** | all | **[DSN→]** §8.1 |
| `Content-Length` | per RFC | 1, 2 | **[DPAE]** A1 example |
| `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` | optional on requests, at the client's initiative | 2 | **[DSN→]** §7.3 |

**Compression is obligatory, not optional**, on every business call that has a body. [DSN→]
§7.4: *"L'activation de la compression par le client HTTP est obligatoire pour tous les
services métier sur toutes les requêtes qui ont un corps http … et sur toutes les réponses."*
The authentication services are exempt. A1's deposit example carries
`Content-Encoding: gzip`, which corroborates that the rule holds here.

Getting this wrong produces `415`, `406` or `400` — see §3.5.

##### 3.4 Response headers that carry control logic

| Header | Meaning | Tag |
|--------|---------|-----|
| `Expires` | On authentication: token expiry. **A3 states the DPAE token never expires** — so if this header appears, the DPAE statement and the DSN mechanism disagree; trust the header and re-authenticate. | [DPAE] + [DSN→] |
| `Expires` (on search) | Earliest permitted time for the next poll. Only present on HTTP 200. | **[DSN→]** §8.3.1 |
| `Accept-Ranges: minutes=0-N` | Maximum width of a time-range search, in minutes | **[DSN→]** §8.3.2 |
| `Content-Encoding: gzip` | Response body is compressed | **[DSN→]** §7.4 |
| `Content-Type` | Format of a downloaded return; the *version* of that format is inside the document | **[DSN→]** §3.5.2 |
| `Cache-Control: no-cache` | Set on all GET responses to prevent caching of nominative data | **[DSN→]** §2.2 |
| `WWW-Authenticate` | Disambiguates 401 — see §3.6 | **[DSN→]** §9.1.6 |

Two of these are **control-flow inputs, not diagnostics**: `Expires` on the list service and
`Accept-Ranges`. A poller that ignores them will earn `429` (§3.5).
