**Group 4 is the payload.** One occurrence per declared datum: `IND` names the datum, and exactly
one of `MOA`/`PCD`/`DTM`/`RFF`/`FTX`/`QTY`/`FII` carries its value.

##### 4.2 The `IND` index code — the addressing scheme

Everything in this channel is addressed by a **25-character code** in `IND`, composite `C546`,
data element **5030** (`an25`). Volume IV §4.2.5.5:

```
        F F F F F F F F F F   M M   I I I I   D D D D   C C   S S S
        └──── 1..10 ────┘    11-12  13..16   17..20   21-22  23..25
         Code formulaire     Version  Form     Data     Zone  Segment
         (from the printed    (not    repeat   repeat   code   type
          form number,       checked) index    index
          space-padded)
```

| Part | Rule |
|------|------|
| `FFFFFFFFFF` | Form code, derived from the printed form number, **left-aligned and space-padded to 10** |
| `MM` | Form version — **not used and not checked** by EDI-Paiement; kept for cross-teleprocedure consistency; numeric. In practice `00` |
| `IIII` | Form repetition index — `0000`, or `0001`..`9999` |
| `DDDD` | Datum repetition index within the form — `0000`, or `0001`..`9999` |
| `CC` | **Zone code** — the two-letter code of the field on the form (`AA`, `AB`, `GB`, …) |
| `SSS` | Expected segment: `RFF`, `MOA`, `FTX`, `DTM`, `QTY`, `PCD` |

> **The padding is load-bearing.** Volume III states it is **forbidden to compress the code by
> removing the blanks** — *"il est interdit de compresser en supprimant par exemple les blancs …
> la zone code formulaire doit ainsi être codée sous peine de rejet"*, rejected as *formulaire
> inconnu*. `2501` must be emitted as `2501` followed by six spaces.

**Worked example**, from the real signed functional group in A15
(`groupe_fonctionnel_a_signer.txt`):

```
SEQ++1'IND++2571      0000000000BAMOA'MOA+ZZZ:1000'
        └─ 2571 +6sp ─┘└MM┘└IIII┘└DDDD┘└CC┘└SSS┘
```

Applying the same construction to the TS forms:

| Datum | `IND` value (25 chars) | Value segment |
|---|---|---|
| 2501 line 01, tax due | `2501      0000000000AAMOA` | `MOA+ZZZ:<amount>` |
| 2501 line 03, net tax due | `2501      0000000000ACMOA` | `MOA+ZZZ:<amount>` |
| 2502 line 12, sector 1 rate | `2502      0000000000ALPCD` | `PCD+…` |
| 2502 line 20, balance payable | `2502      0000000000GAMOA` | `MOA+ZZZ:<amount>` |

The same `CCDDDD` / `FFFFFFFFFFIIII` notation reappears verbatim in every DGFiP error message
(P5 §5.6), so this code is also the **error-location key** — parse it, do not treat error text as
opaque.

##### 4.3 `P-IDENTIF` — mandatory, singular, and carries the money

*"Il est obligatoire, quel que soit par ailleurs le contenu du dépôt"* … *"Un seul formulaire
P-IDENTIF doit être déposé … Toute erreur sur ce point est sanctionnée par un rejet du dépôt"*
(Vol III-C §3.1.2.2).

Field inventory, **verbatim from `PAYE26D` (millésime 2026)**, decoded against the EDIFICAS
segment-type annexe:

| `CC` | Segment | Type code | Meaning | Format |
|------|---------|-----------|---------|--------|
| `AA` | `NAD` | `DT` | **Redevable identification — full NAD with SIREN** | SIREN + name + address, see below |
| `BB` | `DTM` | `CE` | Date of **cession / cessation** | `102` = `CCYYMMDD` |
| `CA` | `DTM` | `DP` | Period start date | `102` |
| `CB` | `DTM` | `FP` | Period end date | `102` |
| `KF` | `DTM` | `EC` | **Échéance (due date)** | **`610` = `CCYYMM`** |
| `KD` | `RFF` | `OF` | **Référence Obligation Fiscale (ROF)** | see §4.4 |
| `GA` `GB` `GC` | `FII` | `R1` `R2` `R3` | Bank details for télérèglements 1–3 | **BIC + IBAN** |
| `HA` `HB` `HC` | `MOA` | `M1` `M2` `M3` | Amount of télérèglements 1–3 | max **9 characters** |
| `KA` `KB` `KC` | `RFF` | `T1` `T2` `T3` | Payment reference for télérèglements 1–3 | see §4.6 |

`NAD` code `DT` structure (EDIFICAS annexe 1.1): `C082/3039` SIREN · `C080/3036` occ. 1–3 name /
qualité / legal form · `C059/3042` occ. 1–3 street / complement / locality · `3164` town ·
`3251` postcode · `3207` ISO-3166 country, **to be served only if not FR**.

###### Rules that bite

- **SIREN only — 9 numeric characters.** *"les dépôts TS millésime 2017 et des années postérieures
  seront obligatoirement identifiés avec le SIREN … la transmission d'un SIRET pour les dépôts
  EDI PAIEMENT générera un rejet syntaxique"* (Vol III-C §3.4.1). Take the first 9 characters of
  the SIRET; never send 14.
- **`CA`/`CB` are not used for TS.** *"Pour l'acquisition des paiements de la TS (solde et
  acomptes), aucune date de début et date de fin de période n'est transmise. Seule l'échéance est
  demandée, contrôlée et exploitée"* (Vol III-C §3.1.2.2 note). The fields exist in the form
  because `P-IDENTIF` is shared with other flux.
- **`BB` (cession/cessation) must be absent on a 2501.** *"La donnée Cession Cessation ne doit pas
  être présente pour un dépôt du bordereau d'acompte (2501)"*.
- **Only cession/cessation is modelled.** No receivership, liquidation or safeguard event is
  transmissible for TS.

##### 4.4 The ROF, and the software-identification references

**ROF — Référence Obligation Fiscale** (Vol III-C §3.1.2):

| Rule | Value |
|------|-------|
| Prefix | **`TS`** |
| Suffix | incrementing number **1..99999**, **no leading zeros between `TS` and the number** |
| Range | `TS1` … `TS99999` |
| Where | `P-IDENTIF` `KD`/`RFF`, type code `OF` |
| Where it comes back | **Returned in the `INFENT CR`** for wholly or partly accepted deposits |
| When to ask the SIE | Only to distinguish two deposits of the same category/regime by the same taxpayer for the same period — *"la consultation du SIE devra être limitée à ces cas"* |

So the ROF is **discovered from the return leg, not invented**. First-filing bootstrap for a new
client needs an explicit decision; record the ROF against the client on first `INFENT CR` and
reuse it. → P7 §7.3.

**Software identification** — mandatory in every message (Vol II §2.4.3.3.2), and visible in the
A15 sample at message level:

| `RFF` qualifier | Content | Sample (A15) |
|---|---|---|
| `AUM` | Editor name | `RFF+AUM:Nom éditeur logiciel` |
| `AUN` | Program name, version, revision | `RFF+AUN:Nom logiciel::0162:01` |
| `AUO` | **EDIFICAS conformity attestation reference** | `RFF+AUO:2006.01.0162` |

These are the `RFF` R×3 at message level in the segment table. They are not decorative — the
conformity attestation reference ties the message to P2 §2.5.

Group 1 (`NAD` ×4) carries the parties. From the A15 sample: `NAD+FR` accountant, **`NAD+MS`**
the emitting partenaire EDI, **`NAD+PO`** the ordering partenaire EDI (where subcontracting
applies, P2 §2.7), and `NAD+MR+++DGI_EDI_PAYE` the recipient.

##### 4.5 Form 2501-SD — *relevé de versement provisionnel*

`PAYE26F` classifies `2501` as: millésime `00`, type **`A`** (formulaire d'acompte), applicable
by **échéance** (`V`), document type **`TS`**.

**Millésime 2026 carries exactly three fields.** Verbatim from `PAYE26D`:

```
2501      00AAMOA        000000  999999
2501      00ABMOA  XX    000000  999999
2501      00ACMOA  YY   Z000000  999999
```

| Line | `CC` | Seg | Type | Label | NREF | EDI composite |
|------|------|-----|------|-------|------|---------------|
| 01 | `AA` | `MOA` | — | Montant de la taxe due au titre du mois ou du trimestre | `902412` | `AA:C516:5004:1` |
| 02 | `AB` | `MOA` | `XX` = excédent de versement | Excédent de versement résultant du report de l'année précédente à imputer | `902413` | `AB:C516:5004:1` |
| 03 | `AC` | `MOA` | `YY` = **montant à payer** | **Montant net** de la taxe due (= line 01 − line 02) | `902414` | `AC:C516:5004:1` |

> **Do not emit `AD`/`MOA` (NREF `909060`).** Line 03 *"Report du crédit d'impôt taxe sur les
> salaires antérieurement constaté à imputer"* was **suppressed** from millésime 2022, old line 04
> was renumbered to 03, and the CAPRO computation of `AC` dropped the `AD` term (2501 form spec,
> *Cartouche des modifs*, version N°1). `PAYE26D` **confirms the suppression persists into
> millésime 2026** — only `AA`, `AB`, `AC` exist. A build ported from a pre-2022 specification
> will emit a field DGFiP no longer knows.

###### Coherence controls on the 2501

| Control | Rule | EFI code | **EDI code** | Disposition |
|---------|------|----------|--------------|-------------|
| Format | `AA`, `AB`, `AC`: integer ≥ 0 or empty, **max 15 digits** (`9(15)`) | `FN15` | — | EFI only |
| Coherence | **`AB` ≤ `AA`** — the carried-forward excess may not exceed the tax due | `B172` | **`400`** | **Rejet TD** |
| Coherence | An entirely blank form is refused | `B147` | — | EFI only |

##### 4.6 Payment — télérèglement by SEPA B2B direct debit

Payment travels **inside the same deposit** as the form, in `P-IDENTIF`.

| Rule | Value | Source |
|------|-------|--------|
| Instrument | **SEPA interentreprises (B2B) direct debit**; DGFiP is creditor, Banque de France its bank | Vol II §2.1.4.2 |
| Cardinality | **Up to 3** télérèglements per deposit (`R1/M1/T1` … `R3/M3/T3`) | `PAYE26D` |
| Per-payment cap | **≤ €999,999,999** each — hence `MOA` max 9 characters | Vol II §2.1.4.2, EDIFICAS annexe 1.5 |
| Total cap | 3 × €999,999,999; beyond that, settle by successive deposits before the DLD | Vol II §2.1.4.2 |
| `FII` content | **BIC *and* IBAN**, SEPA zone. *"si l'une est absente, cela donne lieu à un rejet syntaxique du dépôt via un message CONTRL"* | Vol III-C §3.2.2 |
| Partial payment | Permitted — the amount paid may be less than the tax declared | Vol II §2.1.4.1 note 1 |
| Payment alone | **Forbidden** — *"un télérèglement ne peut jamais être émis sans télédéclaration associée"* | Vol II §2.1.4.1 note 3 |
| Form alone | Permitted — a deposit may be form-only | Vol IV §4.4.10 |

###### Payment reference construction (`KA`/`KB`/`KC`, type `T1`/`T2`/`T3`)

SEPA allows 140 characters; DGFiP **limits it to 31**, because 31 is the minimum length banks
guarantee to reproduce on the corporate account statement for reconciliation (Vol III-C §3.2.2):

```
   ROF          -   MMSSAA   -   Code formulaire principal
 ≤13 chars,     1      6      1          10 chars
 right-hand              =  31 characters maximum
 non-significant
 characters removed
```

where `MMSSAA` is the **month and year of the date limite de dépôt** and the principal form code
is `2501` or `2502`. The account statement additionally shows the **RUM**, carrying the creditor
identification (DGFiP) and the taxpayer's SIREN.

> Build this string deterministically and store it. It is the only key linking a DGFiP deposit to
> a line on the client's bank statement, and the client's finance function will ask. → P7 §7.4.

##### 4.7 Échéance — the only period datum TS transmits

| Rule | Value |
|------|-------|
| Format | **`CCYYMM`** (`DTM` format qualifier **`610`**) — year then month of the DLD |
| Why no day | *"Le jour n'est jamais transmis car il varie en fonction du QJLD ouvré (DLD théorique devenant DLD Réelle)"* |
| **2502** | DLD is **always 31 January** of the year following the taxed year *(but see P1 §1.8 — impots.gouv.fr states 15 January for annual filers; this is unresolved)* |
| **2501 quarterly** | **15 April / 15 July / 15 October** — against the March, June and September quarterly returns; **Q4 is settled by the balance** |
| **2501 monthly** | **15 February … 15 December**; **the 12th month is settled by the balance** |
| **January** | *"Le mois de janvier n'est donc jamais le sujet d'un acompte"* |

###### Accepted-deposit windows (Vol III-C §3.2.2)

| Form | Accepted when |
|------|---------------|
| **2502** | the taxed year must be **closed**; deposit date **after 31 December** of the taxed year; a complementary, corrective or late deposit is accepted **up to the theoretical DLD of the following year**, then rejected. On cession/cessation, up to **declared cession date + 12 months** |
| **2501** | deposit date **after the start** of the taxed year it relates to, **and on or before the DLD of the 2502** for that year |

Outside these windows the deposit is rejected as *dépôt hors du délai géré par la filière* or
*dépôt invalide, réalisé en dehors de la plage autorisée* (Vol III level-4 catalogue) — a **Rejet
RT**, which ends control processing.

##### 4.8 Form 2502-SD — *déclaration annuelle de liquidation et régularisation*

`PAYE26F` classifies `2502` as: millésime `00`, type **`S`** (solde), applicable by échéance
(`V`), document type **`TS`**. **`PAYE26D` carries exactly 76 fields for millésime 2026.**

###### 4.8.1 Part I — Liquidation grid (52 + 12 = 64 fields)

Five columns; four of them repeat the same thirteen line codes, and the fifth is the total.

| Column | Prefix |
|--------|--------|
| Secteur unique or 1er secteur | `A_` |
| 2ème secteur | `B_` |
| 3ème secteur | `C_` |
| Personnel commun | `D_` |
| **Total of all sectors** | `E_` |

| Line | 2nd letter | Segment | Designation |
|------|-----------|---------|-------------|
| 01 | `A` | `MOA` | Métropole — base, **taux normal** |
| 02 | `B` | `MOA` | Métropole — base, **1er taux majoré** |
| 03 | `C` | `MOA` | Métropole — base, **2e taux majoré** |
| 04 | `D` | `MOA` | DOM — base taux normal (Guadeloupe, Martinique, Réunion) |
| 05 | `E` | `MOA` | DOM — base taux normal (Guyane, Mayotte) |
| 06 | `F` | `MOA` | line 01 × normal rate |
| 07 | `G` | `MOA` | line 02 × 1st surcharge rate |
| 08 | `H` | `MOA` | line 03 × 2nd surcharge rate |
| 09 | `I` | `MOA` | line 04 × DOM rate 1 |
| 10 | `J` | `MOA` | line 05 × DOM rate 2 |
| 11 | `K` | `MOA` | **Totals** (06 + 07 + 08 + 09 + 10) |
| 12 | `L` | **`PCD`** | **Pourcentage d'imposition** — the only percentage field |
| 13 | `M` | `MOA` | **Total taxe brute** (= line 11 × line 12) |

> **`EL` does not exist.** The total column has no percentage: line 12 has no aggregate. That is
> why the `E_` column contributes **12** fields (`EA`–`EK`, `EM`) and not 13 — confirmed against
> `PAYE26D`, which lists `EK` then jumps to `EM`. `EM` carries the EDIFICAS type code **`BR`**
> (montant de la taxe brute). Any generator that mechanically emits `AA..AM` × 5 columns will
> produce an `EL` that DGFiP rejects as an unknown code.

###### 4.8.2 Part II — Relief measures (5 fields, total column only)

| Line | `CC` | Type | Designation |
|------|------|------|-------------|
| 14 | `FA` | **`FR`** | **Franchise** — if line 13 ≤ the franchise threshold, carry the line-13 total |
| 15 | `FB` | — | **Décote** — computed from the line-13 total within the décote band |
| 16 | `FC` | **`AB`** | **Abattement** for associations, professional unions and mutuals — CGI **art. 1679 A** |
| 17 | `FD` | — | **Taxe nette** before offset of instalments, excesses and any TS tax credit |
| 18 | `FE` | — | Instalments already paid for the period, or excesses carried forward |

###### 4.8.3 Part III — Payment or excess (7 fields)

| Line | `CC` | Type | Designation |
|------|------|------|-------------|
| 20 | `GA` | **`YY`** montant à payer | **Balance payable** (line 17 − 18 − 26 + 27), if > 0 |
| 21 | `GB` | **`XX`** excédent | **Excess paid** (line 18 − 17 + 26 − 27), if ≥ 0 |
| 22 | `GC` | **`WW`** | **Refund requested** |
| 22bis | `GD` | **`DI`** | Or **request to offset against a future échéance** |
| 23 | `GE` | **`RA`** | And/or **carry-forward to next year's 2501 instalment** |
| 26 | `GF` | — | If an initial 2502 was already filed for the period: amount already paid on the balance *(valid from `201601`)* |
| 27 | `GH` | — | If an initial 2502 was already filed: refund already requested *(valid from `201601`)* |

###### 4.8.4 The CITS block is gone — do not build it

The 2022 form specification still carries a **Part III — Crédit d'impôt TS**: line 31 `HE/MOA`
(`909079`), line 32 `HD/MOA` (`909058`), and a Part V line 36 `JD/MOA` (`910097`, CITS N-3 refund
request). Volume III-C §3.0 records their suppression, the renaming of Part IV to Part III, and
the removal of coherence control **`411`**.

**`PAYE26D` for millésime 2026 contains no `HD`, `HE` or `JD` row.** The EDIFICAS annexe still
defines the type codes `CI` (solde du CITS) and `CT` (imputation du crédit d'impôt TS) for form
2502, but no field uses them. Treat the CITS block as **removed**, and treat the surviving type
codes as vestigial rather than as evidence the fields exist.

###### 4.8.5 Coherence controls on the 2502

| **EDI code** | Rule | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `401` | The percentage amount is inconsistent | Rejet TD |
| `402` | **Franchise and décote are mutually exclusive** | Rejet TD |
| `403` | The abattement amount is wrong | Rejet TD |
| `404` | An abattement requires a total gross tax amount to be present | Rejet TD |
| `406` | **Refund requested and future-échéance offset are mutually exclusive** | Rejet TD |
| `407` | If an excess arises, at least one of refund / offset / carry-forward must be served | Rejet TD |
| `409` | **Refund + offset + carry-forward must sum exactly to the excess** | Rejet TD |
| `411` | TS tax-credit offsets may not exceed the net tax due — **suppressed with the CITS block** | — |

##### 4.9 Correction model — substitution, and it is destructive

There is no amendment operation. Correction is achieved by **depositing again**, and the meaning
of the second deposit depends entirely on whether the **date limite de substitution (DLS)** has
passed.

| Timing | Effect |
|--------|--------|
| **Before the DLS** | The **last** deposit is qualified *initial*; all earlier ones become *initiaux rectifiés*, are *"considérés comme n'ayant jamais été déposés"*, are not taken into account, and **disappear from the taxpayer's own portal view** after the DLS. Only the payment of the last deposit is sent to the Banque de France; **payments attached to earlier deposits are cancelled** |
| **Before the DLS, with a refund request** | **Exception**: a deposit containing a request for refund of an excess *is* taken into account and remains visible, even if later deposits arrive. Any later deposit is then qualified *rectificatif* |
| **After the DLS** | *"il n'est plus possible d'annuler et remplacer un dépôt et un paiement précédemment émis"*. Later deposits are *rectificatif*/*complémentaire* |
| **Any time** | If a replacement deposit before the DLS **omits the payment data, the previous payment is cancelled** |

> **Two traps.**
> 1. *"les informations de paiement du dépôt rectificatif effectué avant DLS se substituent à
>    celles du dépôt précédent"* — so where a declaration was rejected but its payment accepted,
>    the corrective deposit **must re-include the payment**, or the accepted payment is revoked.
>    Volume IV §4.1.2.5.3 flags this case explicitly.
> 2. Cancel-and-replace of a payment before the DLS is *"strictement réservée aux dépôts émis
>    dans la filière EDI"*. A client that files one period by portal and the next by EDI loses
>    that capability for the portal period.

Design consequence: the send path must be a **state machine keyed on (SIREN, ROF, échéance,
form)** that knows whether the DLS has passed and refuses to re-send after it without an explicit
operator decision. Blind retry is unsafe here. → P7 §7.3.

##### 4.10 Pre-flight gate

There is no sandbox and no tolerance for a technical rejection on the deadline (P3 §3.5).
Everything below is checkable **before** emission and each maps to a specific documented rejection.

**Suppression gates (P1 §1.3) — check first, they cancel the whole deposit:**

| # | Assertion |
|---|-----------|
| 1 | Client is recorded as **TS-liable for this year** (not VAT-liable on ≥ 90 % of turnover), by explicit configuration |
| 2 | Annual tax is **above the franchise floor** |
| 3 | The **filing rhythm** for this year derives from **last year's** tax, and this échéance belongs to that rhythm |
| 4 | The échéance is **not January** for a 2501 |

**Identification:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 5 | Redevable identified by **SIREN — exactly 9 numeric characters**, never a SIRET | syntactic rejection |
| 6 | Exactly **one** `P-IDENTIF` in the deposit | deposit rejection |
| 7 | ROF matches `^TS[1-9][0-9]{0,4}$` — prefix `TS`, no leading zero, 1..99999 | *Référence obligation fiscale déclarée inconnue* (alert 900) |
| 8 | `RFF+AUM`, `RFF+AUN`, `RFF+AUO` all served, `AUO` carrying the real EDIFICAS attestation reference | Vol II §2.4.3.3.2 |

**Structure:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 9 | Every `IND` value is **exactly 25 characters**, form code **space-padded to 10, not compressed** | *Formulaire inconnu ou invalide* — Rejet RT |
| 10 | Every `CC` code emitted exists in `PAYE26D` for this form and millésime | *Code inconditionné* — Rejet RT |
| 11 | The **segment matches** the `SSS` in the index (`PCD` for `AL`/`BL`/`CL`/`DL`, `MOA` elsewhere) | *Type de segment erroné* — Rejet RT |
| 12 | No duplicate `CCDDDD` within one `FFFFFFFFFFMMIIII` | *Code CCDDDD en double* — Rejet RT |
| 13 | **No `AD` on the 2501; no `HD`, `HE`, `JD` and no `EL` on the 2502** | *Code inconditionné* — Rejet RT |
| 14 | Form present and compatible with the `BGM` document type `TS` (`PAYE26F`) | *Formulaires incompatibles dans un même dépôt* |

**Values:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 15 | **All amounts positive or zero** — *"seuls les montants positifs ou à zéro sont acceptés"* | Vol III-C §3.2.3 |
| 16 | Amounts are **integers, ≤ 15 digits** | `FN15` |
| 17 | 2501: **`AB` ≤ `AA`** | **`400`** |
| 18 | The form is **not entirely blank** | `B147` |
| 19 | 2502: **franchise and décote not both served** | **`402`** |
| 20 | 2502: **refund and future-offset not both served** | **`406`** |
| 21 | 2502: if an excess is declared, **at least one** of refund / offset / carry-forward served | **`407`** |
| 22 | 2502: refund + offset + carry-forward **equals** the excess exactly | **`409`** |
| 23 | 2502: abattement present ⟹ gross tax total present | **`404`** |

**Payment:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 24 | Each `FII` carries **both BIC and IBAN** | **`CONTRL` syntactic rejection** |
| 25 | ≤ 3 télérèglements; each ≤ **€999,999,999** (≤ 9 characters) | Vol II §2.1.4.2 |
| 26 | No payment without a form | Vol II §2.1.4.1 note 3 |
| 27 | Payment reference ≤ **31 characters**, built to the §4.6 pattern | reconciliation failure downstream |

**Period and event:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 28 | Échéance is `CCYYMM` (`DTM` qualifier `610`); **no day component** | *Échéance invalide pour le formulaire déposé* |
| 29 | **No `CA`/`CB` period dates** on a TS deposit | — |
| 30 | Cession/cessation date **present only on a 2502**, never on a 2501 | *Date de cession/cessation absente ou erronée* |
| 31 | Deposit date falls inside the accepted window for the form (§4.7) | *Dépôt hors du délai géré par la filière* — Rejet RT |

**Mode:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 32 | `UNB/0035` and `UNG/0040` are **consistent** — both real (`—`/`DP1`) or both test (`1`/`DP3`) | interchange syntactic rejection |
| 33 | For a real filing, mode is **real** | a test file sent as `DP1` **is a real filing** |

**Securing:**

| # | Assertion | Failure |
|---|-----------|---------|
| 34 | The functional group is signed; certificate in `0560/USR/G2`, seal in `0560/USY/G3` | *"Tout envoi non sécurisé sera rejeté dans sa globalité"* |
| 35 | Signature is RSA-2048 over **SHA-256(GF) ‖ timestamp**, PKCS#1 | level-2 rejection of the whole interchange |

Assertions 9–13 and 15–23 are mechanically checkable against `PAYE26D` and the coherence sheets
already in `integration-sources/`. Build the gate as a data-driven validator reading `PAYE26D`
rather than as hand-written rules — DGFiP reissues that table every millésime (§4.11).

##### 4.11 Millésime management

The flux carries a **palier** (release) per year, and TS is on its own calendar:

- The **millésime 2022** palier went to production in **December 2021**, *"afin d'aligner le
  palier millésime TS sur celui des autres types de flux"* — so **the millésime label runs a year
  ahead of the deployment date**. The TS 2026 palier likewise dates from December 2025.
- The test flux version for that palier was `PED-DGI-IN-DP2201/TS22` (Vol III-C §3.1.3).
- Form specifications are distributed **separately, as zip files**, and multiple versions of the
  2502 coexist: one for the normal annual filing of year N−1 and one for **cession/cessation
  during year N** (Vol III-C §3.0.1, §3.2.3.5). Selecting the wrong one is a silent content error.
- **Thresholds move every millésime.** The abattement was raised from €21,044 to €21,086 for
  2021; industry notes record a further move for 2026. **This runbook is not the authority for
  any threshold** — the CCG is. What this runbook fixes is that a threshold change arrives as a
  **new `PAYE26D`/form-spec pair**, and that the validator must be re-pointed at it.

**Operational rule:** treat `PAYE26D`, `PAYE26F` and the form-specification zip as **versioned
configuration data, refreshed annually from EDIFICAS/impots.gouv.fr before the December palier**,
never as constants compiled into the emitter. → P7 §7.7.


### DGFIP-EDI / `P5-inbound-returns-contrl-infent-cr.md`

#### P5 — Inbound Returns: `INFENT RCS`, `CONTRL`, `INFENT CR`
**FR · DGFIP-EDI · DGFiP — taxe sur les salaires via the EDI channel**

Status: **BUILD-READY** · 1 unresolved (level-4 code numbering, §5.6)
Source basis: Volume IV §4.1.1, §4.4 (all four control levels, the three return GUMs, §4.4.10
consequences), Volume III 2021 level-4 catalogue, Volume III-C §3.5–3.6, Volume II §2.7.

> **This phase is where the filing actually happens.** Everything before it is a file transfer.

---

##### 5.1 The three return messages

| Message | Emitted by | When | Carries |
|---------|-----------|------|---------|
| **`INFENT RCS`** *(Réception Conformité Sécurisation)* | ESI Strasbourg | **Systematically, before any processing**, for network transmissions; also on a level-1 or level-2 failure | Receipt acknowledgement, or the cause of a readability/security rejection |
| **`CONTRL`** | ESI | **Only if at least one syntax error is detected** (level 3) | EDIFACT syntactic rejection detail |
| **`INFENT CR`** *(Compte Rendu de Traitement)* | ESI | After level-4 controls, **for every `INFENT DP` received** that reached that level | The **code bilan** — the verdict — plus the ROF and elementary error codes |

Each arrives wrapped in its own **`AUTACK`** and is **signed by DGFiP** (Vol II §2.7.2.3). All
three come back **on the same medium used to send** (Vol IV §4.1.1.2), into the partner's
withdrawal directory (P3 §3.4).

**`CONTRL` is emitted only on error.** Its absence is the success signal for level 3 —
so the parser must not wait for it. `INFENT RCS`, by contrast, is systematic, so **its** absence
is a genuine anomaly worth alerting on (P7 §7.5).

##### 5.2 The four control levels — and their blast radius

This is the single most important table in the runbook. The same deposit can fail at four
different scopes, and the correct recovery differs at each.

| Level | Control | Performed on | **Rejection scope** | Return |
|-------|---------|--------------|--------------------|--------|
| **1** | **Readability** of the received information | the medium | **Whole interchange** | `INFENT RCS` (in some cases) |
| **2** | **Securing** — authenticity and integrity, verifying the partner's electronic signature | the interchange | **Whole interchange** | `INFENT RCS` |
| **3** | **Syntax** — EDIFACT conformity against the message guides | interchange, functional group, message | **Whole interchange** if the error is in service segments or the `AUTACK` structure; **only the erroneous `INFENT DP` message(s)** if the error is inside a message | `CONTRL` |
| **4** | **Integrability** — can the data be integrated into DGFiP's systems | each `INFENT DP` | **Global or partial**, per code bilan | `INFENT CR` |

Sources: Vol II §2.6.3.1–2, Vol IV §4.4.10.1–4.

Two derived rules:

- **Levels 1–3 can destroy work that was itself valid.** One malformed message from one client
  can take down an interchange carrying fifty others. That is an argument for **smaller
  interchanges near a deadline**, not for batching everything. → P7 §7.2.
- *"Aucun délai supplémentaire n'est accordé pour procéder à la régularisation d'un dépôt ayant
  fait l'objet d'un rejet technique"* (Vol IV §4.1.2.3.1). A level-1 rejection on the deadline is
  a late filing.

###### Level-3 pre-filter on amounts

Volume IV §4.4.7.3.1 records two behaviours worth encoding:

- **Negative amounts are rejected at level 3** — they never reach the integrability controls.
- **Non-significant zero amounts are ignored** after level 3; a *significant* zero is treated as
  present.

This is the mechanism behind Volume III-C's *"seuls les montants positifs ou à zéro sont
acceptés"* (P4 §4.10 assertion 15).

##### 5.3 Retrieval sequence

```
1. connect (one session, n files — P3 §3.4)
2. list the withdrawal directory
3. for each file: download to durable storage FIRST, parse SECOND
4. verify the AUTACK signature  (both old and new schemes — P3 §3.7)
5. dispatch by message type: INFENT RCS | CONTRL | INFENT CR
6. correlate to the deposit; apply §5.4; persist the verdict
7. do not delete anything on the server
```

Four rules that are easy to get wrong:

1. **Download is destructive.** Files are removed on download, or after ~21 days
   (P3 §3.4). Persist before parsing; never re-fetch to re-parse.
2. **The withdrawal directory is shared across all EDI teleprocedures** (P3 §3.4) — TVA, TDFC
   and IS returns land beside TS ones. **Filter by content**, and never assume an unrecognised
   file is an error.
3. **Verify the signature before trusting the verdict.** DGFiP signs its returns precisely so
   that they can serve as proof; an unverified return is not proof of anything.
4. **A `CONTRL` may reference messages, not the whole file.** Correlate at message level, or a
   partial level-3 rejection will be mis-handled as a whole-interchange failure.

##### 5.4 The code bilan — the verdict, and the trap

At the end of level 4 a **code bilan** is assigned to each `INFENT DP` (Vol IV §4.4.10.4).
There are **seven** values: one acceptance and six rejections.

| Code bilan | Meaning (verbatim, Vol IV §4.4.9) | Declaration | Payment |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`VT`** | *Validité technique* — the message is **globally accepted** (declaration and payment, if present) | **accepted** | accepted |
| **Rejet `RT`** | *Rejet technique* — declaration **and** payment rejected; the declaration is **not identifiable**. Global, "et fin contrôles" | rejected | rejected |
| **Rejet `TD`** | The **declaration** part is rejected | rejected | accepted |
| **Rejet `TP`** | The **payment** part is rejected — **no** valid SEPA direct debit | accepted | rejected |
| **Rejet `TPp`** | **Partial** payment rejection — at least one SEPA debit invalid **and at least one valid** | accepted | partial |
| **Rejet `TD/TPp`** | Declaration rejected **and** payment partially rejected | rejected | partial |
| **Rejet `TD/TP`** | Declaration **and** payment both rejected | rejected | rejected |

And at **lot** level, in group 7 (`CCI`/`CAV`), code table **`TDG`** (Vol III-C §3.5.1):

| Value | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| **`AFL`** | Acceptation fonctionnelle lot |
| **`RFL`** | Rejet fonctionnel lot |

> ### The trap: `AFL DEC` is not `AFL TP`
>
> Volume II §2.7.2.3 states it directly: *"la partie déclarative et la partie paiement font
> l'objet de bilans distincts et doivent être analysés séparément"*. Volume IV's bilan matrix
> confirms it — the same message can carry `RFL` on the `DEC` lot and `AFL` on a `TP` lot.
>
> **`Rejet TD` is the dangerous one.** The money moves; nothing is declared. There is no
> deposit — Volume IV's own matrix marks the *code nature* of a `TD` outcome as **"Pas de
> dépôt"** — but the télérèglement has been accepted and forwarded to the Banque de France.
> A reconciler with a single boolean per deposit reports this as success, the client's bank
> statement agrees, and the missing return surfaces months later as a mise en demeure.
>
> **Model the outcome as a pair `(declaration_status, payment_status)`, never as one flag.**

Additionally (Vol IV §4.1.2.2): *"Si un message INFENT DP a donné lieu à un rejet TD ou TD/TPp,
avec tout ou partie du télérèglement valide, la prise en compte de la partie paiement ne vaut pas
« dépôt de déclaration »"*. The payment does not stand in for the filing.

###### Recovery matrix (Vol IV §4.4.10.4)

| Code bilan | Rejection scope | What must be re-emitted |
|---|---|---|
| `RT` (form only) | global technical | the **whole form**, as *initial* |
| `TD` (form only) | global | the **whole form**, as *initial* |
| `RT` (form + payment) | global technical | **form and payment**, as *initial* |
| `TD` (form + payment) | partial | **form and payment** if before the DLS; **form only** if after |
| `TP` | partial | form and payment (before DLS) / **payment only, marked *rectificative*** (after DLS) |
| `TPp` | partial | form and payment (before DLS) / the failed **`TLR n` only, *rectificative*** (after DLS) |
| `TD/TPp` | partial | form and payment (before DLS) / **form + `TLR n`, *initiale*** (after DLS) |
| `TD/TP` | global | **form and payment**, as *initial* |

Two conditions the matrix attaches (notes 2, 4, 5): re-emission presupposes the underlying
condition is now met — DGFiP's own example is a `TP` rejection because the taxpayer had not
subscribed to télérèglement, where *"la réémission ne doit être effectuée que si la souscription
TD/TP est valide au moment du dépôt rectificatif"* (P2 §2.2) — and a `TPn` lot verdict exists
only where that `TPn` was present in the message.

**The re-emission's *code nature* (initiale / rectificative) depends on whether the DLS has
passed.** The same failure needs a different message before and after that instant. This is why
P4 §4.9 requires a DLS-aware state machine rather than a retry counter.

##### 5.5 Reconciliation model

**Reconcile per `INFENT DP` message — that is, per (SIREN, ROF, échéance, form) — never per
file.** An interchange carries many deposits; each gets its own code bilan; nothing in the
verdict set is keyed to the file.

Minimum state per deposit:

| Field | Source |
|-------|--------|
| SIREN, ROF, échéance, form code | emitted payload (P4) |
| Interchange / functional group / message reference | `UNB`, `UNG`, `UNH` |
| Transfer timestamp | `226 Transfer complete` — **the filing date of record** (P3 §3.5) |
| `INFENT RCS` received? | §5.1 |
| `CONTRL` received? | absence = level-3 pass |
| **Code bilan** | `INFENT CR` |
| **`DEC` lot verdict** (`AFL`/`RFL`) | `INFENT CR`, table `TDG` |
| **`TP1..TP3` lot verdicts** | `INFENT CR`, table `TDG` |
| Elementary error codes + `CCDDDD`/`FFFFFFFFFFIIII` locations | `INFENT CR` group 7 |
| **ROF as returned by DGFiP** | `INFENT CR` — authoritative (P4 §4.4) |
| Payment reference emitted | P4 §4.6 — the bank-statement key |
| DLS for this échéance | derived |
| Deposit qualification (initial / initial rectifié / rectificatif) | derived from §4.9 |

> **Deposits before the DLS are provisional.** A deposit accepted with `VT` today can be demoted
> to *initial rectifié* tomorrow by a later deposit — *"considérés comme n'ayant jamais été
> déposés"*. The state store must therefore keep the **whole chain** per (SIREN, ROF, échéance),
> not just the latest verdict, and must not report "filed" as final until the DLS has passed.

##### 5.6 Error catalogue

###### TS business errors — Volume III-C §3.6, authoritative

| Code | Message | Disposition |
|------|---------|-------------|
| **`400`** | *Erreur zone CCDDDD du FFFFFFFFFFIIII* : the carried-forward excess must be ≤ the tax due | **Rejet TD** |
| **`401`** | the percentage amount is inconsistent | Rejet TD |
| **`402`** | franchise and décote are mutually exclusive | Rejet TD |
| **`403`** | the abattement amount is wrong | Rejet TD |
| **`404`** | the abattement requires a total gross tax amount to be present | Rejet TD |
| **`406`** | refund requested and future-échéance offset are mutually exclusive | Rejet TD |
| **`407`** | where an excess arises, at least one of refund / offset / carry-forward must be served | Rejet TD |
| **`409`** | refund + offset + carry-forward must equal the excess | Rejet TD |
| **`411`** | TS tax-credit offsets may not exceed the net tax due — **suppressed** with the CITS block | — |

Every one of these is **`Rejet TD`**: the declaration falls, the payment stands. They are exactly
the failure mode described in §5.4, and every one of them is **preventable in the pre-flight
gate** (P4 §4.10 assertions 17–23).

Volume III also records the code lifecycle: `409`–`412` created at the TS 2019 palier; `405`,
`408`, `410`, `412` suppressed at the same palier. **Do not treat the code list as stable across
millésimes** — re-read it at each palier (P7 §7.7).

###### Generic level-4 anomalies — Volume III, tableau 2

Reject dispositions are documented; the recurring one for identification and structure faults is
**"Rejet RT et fin contrôles"** — the deposit is unidentifiable and control processing stops.
Conditions include: invalid emitting partenaire EDI; invalid ordering partner; missing redevable
identification; **unrecognised SIREN**; missing period or échéance; **unknown or invalid form
`FFFFFFFFFFMMIIII`**; missing declarative form; missing or invalid principal form; **incompatible
forms in one deposit**; unconditioned code; **duplicate `CCDDDD`**; wrong segment type for a
code; wrong repetition index; **invalid échéance for the form deposited**; **deposit outside the
authorised window**; non-existent calendar date; missing or wrong cession/cessation date. Also,
not `RT`: *montant télérèglement absent ou invalide dans TPn* and *CB télérèglement absent ou
invalide dans TPn* — these are the payment-side faults that produce `TP`/`TPp`.

###### Alert codes (non-blocking) — Volume III

`900` *Référence obligation fiscale déclarée inconnue* is the one that matters for TS: it fires
on a ROF DGFiP does not recognise. **It is an alert, not a rejection** — so a deposit can succeed
while signalling that the ROF is wrong. Surface it; do not swallow it. Codes `901`–`906` concern
CVAE and RCM and are out of scope here.

> **Unresolved (Q5).** The numeric codes for the generic level-4 anomalies sit in table cells of
> a legacy `.doc` (A4) that flatten away from their labels under text extraction, so **the code
> numbers are not reproduced above** — only the labels and dispositions, which extracted
> reliably. The TS-specific codes `400`–`411` are unaffected: they come from A1 §3.6 in readable
> form. Resolve by opening A4 in a word processor, or by requesting the current Volume III from
> EDIFICAS in PDF. **This does not block the build** — the pre-flight gate is written against
> the *rules*, and the return parser must handle unknown codes gracefully in any case.

##### 5.7 Evidentiary value — the return is the proof

| Rule | Content | Source |
|------|---------|--------|
| Where the proof lives | *"Pour obtenir la preuve de l'accomplissement dans les délais des obligations déclaratives, le redevable se rapprochera de son partenaire EDI"* — **the partner holds the evidence, not the taxpayer** | Vol II §2.7.2.3 |
| What the report establishes | the **horodatage** of the send, and the acceptance or rejection level of the declarative and payment lots | Vol II §2.7.2.3 |
| DGFiP-side archival | **6 years**, in the original signed format, for every declaration technically exploitable (i.e. that passed levels 1–2) — including rejected ones | Vol II §2.7.3.1 |
| **Rejeu** | On dispute, DGFiP extracts the transmission from storage, confirms the declaration exists and the transmission was valid, and checks the content against what its systems hold. Can be initiated by DGFiP **or the taxpayer** | Vol II §2.7.3.2 |
| Rejeu limits | Does **not** apply where the claim is only about the declarant's own errors or omissions; and *"les réclamations concernant la date de dépôt ne nécessitent pas de mettre en œuvre la procédure de rejeu — la date de dépôt figurant sur les documents restitués au service fait foi"* | Vol II §2.7.3.2 |

**Retention design.** P2 §2.7 requires a partner to retain transmitted data only as long as
necessary; this section makes the *processing reports* the client's evidence. Resolve by
separating two stores:

| Store | Contents | Retention |
|-------|----------|-----------|
| **Evidence store** | `INFENT CR` (and `RCS`/`CONTRL`), their `AUTACK` signatures, the transfer timestamp, the code bilan and lot verdicts | Long — mirroring DGFiP's own 6 years, so a rejeu can be answered |
| **Transient store** | The emitted `INFENT DP` payload | Only as long as needed for transmission, confirmation, and any re-emission window |

Where a client leaves, or Mercans ceases to be a partenaire EDI, clients must be notified (P2
§2.7) — and the evidence store is what they will ask for. Design the export before go-live.

##### 5.8 Where the taxpayer can check independently

Accepted declarations and payments appear in the client's own **espace professionnel** on
impots.gouv.fr *"quelques jours après leur transmission"* (Vol II §2.6.4.1). Two uses:

1. **An independent cross-check** during pilot running (P6 §6.4) — the only view not derived
   from the return parser, and therefore the only one that can catch a parser bug.
2. **A trap after the DLS**: deposits demoted to *initial rectifié* **disappear** from that view
   (P4 §4.9). A client comparing the portal to Mercans' own history will find deposits missing
   that Mercans recorded as sent. Document this for client-facing staff — it is correct
   behaviour, not data loss.


### DGFIP-EDI / `P6-test-conformance-cutover.md`

#### P6 — Test, Conformance & Cutover
**FR · DGFIP-EDI · DGFiP — taxe sur les salaires via the EDI channel**

Status: **BUILD-READY**
Source basis: Volume IV §4.5 (test procedures), Volume II §2.4.3.3 (EDIFICAS attestation),
Volume III-C §3.1.3, **A17 `tables_population_fictive_pedi_v9.7`**, **A24 `PAYE26D`/`PAYE26F` +
the EDIFICAS autotest extract**, A15 (signing worked example).

---

##### 6.1 There is no test environment — and that is the good news

Test is **a pair of flags on the production channel**, not a separate host, port or directory
(P3 §3.8). The same accreditation, the same directories, the same processing chain.

| | `UNB` data **0035** | `UNG` data **0040** |
|---|---|---|
| **REAL** | not served | **`DP1`** |
| **TEST** | **`1`** | **`DP3`** = `INF_DP_ST_IT` (secured messages, **test** security, **test** integration) |

*"Ces 2 valeurs sont obligatoires. Si l'une des 2 est absente, alors l'interchange sera rejeté
syntaxiquement"* (Vol IV §4.5).

###### What test mode actually does — Volume IV §4.5.4

| | Syntax | Securing | Integrability | `CONTRL` | `INFENT CR` | **Stored / taxpayer notified** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **REEL** | yes | yes | yes | on error | yes | **yes** |
| **TEST** | yes | yes | yes | on error, `UNB/0035=1` | yes, `UNB/0035=1` | **no** |

So a test deposit runs **all four control levels** and returns a **real `INFENT CR` with a real
code bilan** — it is simply not stored and produces no taxpayer restitution. That is unusually
generous: unlike the DPAE channel, **the full validation chain is exercisable before go-live at
no risk**. The return interchange comes back with `CR3` in `UNG/0040` and `DP3` in `UNG/0044`.

Securing in test mode is generated using the partner's securing station **in test mode**; on
receipt, DGFiP verifies the signature **without archiving** (Vol IV §4.5.2).

> **The one asymmetry is dangerous.** Test is opt-in: a file that omits the flags is **real**.
> There is no "am I in production?" guard on the transport. Assertions 32–33 of the pre-flight
> gate (P4 §4.10) are the only defence, and they must fail closed.

##### 6.2 Gate 0 — before anything reaches DGFiP

| # | Gate | Evidence |
|---|------|----------|
| 1 | Habilitation granted, partner number issued | P2 §2.3 |
| 2 | Electronic accreditation collected and installed | P2 §2.4 |
| 3 | **EDIFICAS conformity attestation obtained**, reference available for `RFF+AUO` | P2 §2.5 |
| 4 | Connection parameters received; protocol fixed (one, both directions) | P2 §2.6, P3 §3.2 |
| 5 | Pre-flight validator (P4 §4.10, 35 assertions) implemented and **data-driven from `PAYE26D`** | P4 §4.10 |
| 6 | Signature path verified against the A15 worked example — **byte-identical** seal reproduced for the supplied sample | A15 |
| 7 | **Both** return-signature verifiers implemented (old and new schemes) | P3 §3.7 |
| 8 | Mode-flag guard fails closed | §6.1 |

**Gate 6 deserves emphasis.** A15 ships a complete key pair, a real accreditation, a real
functional group and the C source of the signer. Reproducing its seal exactly is a **zero-cost,
zero-risk, offline proof** that the securing implementation is correct — do it before the first
transmission, not after the first level-2 rejection.

##### 6.3 Gate 1 — EDIFICAS self-certification, offline

The conformity attestation (P2 §2.5) is obtained by self-test: EDIFICAS supplies a dossier, test
data and a self-evaluation tool; the candidate returns results; EDIFICAS issues the report and
the attestation. Two of the inputs are **already in hand and can be exercised now**:

| Artefact | Use |
|----------|-----|
| **`PAYE26D`** — data structure table, millésime 2026 | The authoritative list of valid `(form, millésime, CC, segment)` tuples with validity windows. Drive the P4 §4.10 structural assertions from it |
| **`PAYE26F`** — form table, millésime 2026 | Validates that the `BGM` document type matches the forms deposited: `2501` → type `A`, flux `TS`; `2502` → type `S`, flux `TS`; `P-IDENTIF` → type `R`, valid for all flux |
| **`Extrait des paramétrages de l'autotest — EDI-PAIEMENT 2026`** | The autotest parameter set itself |

Remember the attestation's declared scope: **EDIFACT syntactic conformity of the emitting module
only** — not the accounting application, not the content (Vol II §2.4.3.3 "IMPORTANT"). Passing
it proves the file is well-formed, **not** that the tax is right. Content correctness is proved
at Gate 3.

##### 6.4 Gate 2 — `TEST` transmissions against the fictitious population

DGFiP publishes a **fictitious test population** (A17, v9.7) — 200-odd SIRENs with per-flux
obligations. Sheet **`5 Info TS`** carries the TS cohort, and its composition maps exactly onto
the three statutory filing rhythms of P1 §1.2:

| Cohort | `Modalités Formulaires` | Count | Exercises |
|--------|------------------------|------:|-----------|
| Annual filer | `2502A` only | **63** | 2502-SD alone, no instalments |
| Monthly filer | `2502A` + **`2501M`** | **62** | 11 monthly 2501-SDs + closing 2502-SD |
| Quarterly filer | `2502A` + **`2501T`** | **62** | 3 quarterly 2501-SDs + closing 2502-SD |

Sheet **`1 Identification population`** supplies SIREN, NIC, denomination, legal form and
address for each — everything `P-IDENTIF`'s `NAD+DT` needs. The worked example in A15 uses
`07955542100019`, the first row of this table.

> Sheet **`7 CB Format BIC IBAN`** exists for bank details but is **not pre-populated** in v9.7.
> Payment-leg testing therefore needs BIC/IBAN values agreed with the ESI — raise it with the
> assistance service when connection parameters are issued. Everything except the payment leg
> can be tested without it.

**Also note the ±90-day rule.** Volume III-C §3.1.3: *"la date de dépôt concernant la population
fictive (population de test) est incrémentée de 90 jours, afin de valider les évolutions"* — the
fictitious population's deposit date is shifted forward by 90 days so a new palier can be tested
before it applies. **Test échéances must be computed on the shifted calendar**, or every test
deposit will be rejected as outside the authorised window (P4 §4.7).

###### Test case set

**Connectivity (C1–C6)** — protocol level, no business content:

| # | Case | Expected |
|---|------|----------|
| C1 | Connect and authenticate to the assigned platform | success |
| C2 | Deposit a file into the deposit directory | `226 Transfer complete` |
| C3 | Attempt to create a directory | **refused** (P3 §3.4) |
| C4 | Attempt to deposit a duplicate filename | **refused** |
| C5 | List and retrieve from the withdrawal directory in **one session, n files** | success |
| C6 | Confirm a retrieved file is removed server-side and the local copy survives | destructive-download behaviour understood (P3 §3.4) |

**Securing (S1–S3):**

| # | Case | Expected |
|---|------|----------|
| S1 | Signed interchange, valid accreditation | passes level 2 |
| S2 | **Unsigned** interchange | **whole interchange rejected** (Vol II §2.7.2.3) |
| S3 | Tampered functional group after signing | level-2 rejection, `INFENT RCS` with cause |

**Syntax (X1–X3):**

| # | Case | Expected |
|---|------|----------|
| X1 | Error in interchange/functional-group service segments | **whole interchange** rejected, `CONTRL` |
| X2 | Error inside **one** `INFENT DP` of several | **only that message** rejected; the others proceed |
| X3 | `FII` with IBAN but **no BIC** | **`CONTRL` syntactic rejection** (Vol III-C §3.2.2) |

**Business / integrability (T1–T14)** — all in `TEST` mode, all expecting a specific code bilan:

| # | Case | Cohort | Expected |
|---|------|--------|----------|
| T1 | Valid 2501-SD, monthly échéance, form only | `2501M` | **`VT`**, `AFL DEC` |
| T2 | Valid 2501-SD **with payment** | `2501M` | **`VT`**, `AFL DEC` + `AFL TP1` |
| T3 | Valid 2501-SD, quarterly échéance | `2501T` | **`VT`** |
| T4 | **2501-SD with a January échéance** | `2501M` | **rejected** — January is never an acompte (P1 §1.2) |
| T5 | 2501-SD with **`AB` > `AA`** | any | **`Rejet TD`, code `400`** |
| T6 | Entirely blank form | any | rejected (`B147` family) |
| T7 | Valid 2502-SD, annual | `2502A` | **`VT`** |
| T8 | 2502-SD with **franchise and décote both served** | `2502A` | **`Rejet TD`, code `402`** |
| T9 | 2502-SD with **refund and future-offset both served** | `2502A` | **`Rejet TD`, code `406`** |
| T10 | 2502-SD, excess declared, **no** refund/offset/carry-forward | `2502A` | **`Rejet TD`, code `407`** |
| T11 | 2502-SD, refund + offset + carry-forward ≠ excess | `2502A` | **`Rejet TD`, code `409`** |
| T12 | Deposit identified by **SIRET (14 chars)** instead of SIREN | any | **syntactic rejection** (Vol III-C §3.4.1) |
| T13 | **Two** `P-IDENTIF` in one deposit | any | **deposit rejected** (Vol III-C §3.1.2.2) |
| T14 | 2501-SD carrying a **cession/cessation date** | any | rejected — not permitted on a 2501 |

**The `Rejet TD` cases (T5, T8–T11) are the most valuable in the set**, because they are the ones
that in production accept the money and reject the return (P5 §5.4). Verify not just the code
bilan but that the reconciler records **`(declaration=rejected, payment=accepted)`** and raises
the right alert.

**Return-parsing (R1–R5):**

| # | Case | Expected |
|---|------|----------|
| R1 | `INFENT RCS` parsed and signature verified | receipt recorded |
| R2 | `CONTRL` **absent** on a clean deposit | interpreted as level-3 pass, not as a timeout |
| R3 | `INFENT CR` correlated to the right `INFENT DP` by (SIREN, ROF, échéance, form) | per-deposit reconciliation (P5 §5.5) |
| R4 | `INFENT CR` returning a **ROF** captured and stored as authoritative | P4 §4.4 |
| R5 | A return file for **another teleprocedure** (TVA/TDFC/IS) in the shared directory | ignored cleanly, not treated as an error (P3 §3.4) |

**Substitution (D1–D3)** — the highest-risk behaviour in the channel:

| # | Case | Expected |
|---|------|----------|
| D1 | Two deposits for the same (SIREN, ROF, échéance) **before the DLS** | second is *initial*; first becomes *initial rectifié* (P4 §4.9) |
| D2 | Second deposit **omitting payment data**, before the DLS | **previous payment cancelled** — verify the reconciler detects this |
| D3 | Deposit after the DLS | qualified *rectificatif*; no cancel-and-replace |

##### 6.5 Gate 3 — first real filing

Because there is no separate production environment, "cutover" is simply **the first interchange
sent with `DP1`**. Sequence it deliberately:

1. **Choose one pilot client**, TS-liable, on the **monthly** rhythm — it produces eleven
   observations a year rather than one, so defects surface fast.
2. Confirm the client's own prerequisites are complete: espace professionnel, **service "Payer"**
   adhesion, and the **signed SEPA B2B mandate lodged with its bank** (P2 §2.2). Without the last
   one the payment fails at the bank, invisibly to this channel.
3. **File well before the deadline.** There is no grace period and no tolerance for technical
   rejection (P3 §3.5). Target the échéance **minus at least 5 working days**, so that a full
   reject-correct-resend cycle plus the 2-working-day return SLA fits inside the window.
4. Run the deposit **form-only on the first cycle if the schedule allows**, then add the payment
   leg on the second. This separates declaration defects from payment defects, which is exactly
   the split the code bilan makes (P5 §5.4).
5. **Cross-check independently** in the client's espace professionnel a few days later
   (P5 §5.8) — the only view not produced by Mercans' own parser.
6. Only then extend to the quarterly and annual cohorts. The **annual 2502-SD is the last thing
   to go live**, because it is the filing with one attempt a year.

**The fallback is lawful and should be kept warm.** EFI portal filing is a fully equivalent
channel and mixed EDI/EFI filing is expressly permitted (Vol IV §4.1.2.1). If the first real
filing fails near a deadline, **file it on the portal** — this is not a compliance breach, and
it should be a documented, rehearsed runbook step rather than an improvisation. Note the one
consequence: cancel-and-replace before the DLS is *"strictement réservée aux dépôts émis dans la
filière EDI"* (P4 §4.9), so a period filed by portal loses that capability.

##### 6.6 Post-cutover watch

For the first three cycles, verify on every deposit:

| Check | Why |
|-------|-----|
| `INFENT RCS` received | systematic — its absence is a real anomaly (P5 §5.1) |
| Code bilan is **`VT`** | anything else needs the §5.4 recovery matrix |
| **`DEC` and `TP` lot verdicts recorded separately** | the `Rejet TD` trap (P5 §5.4) |
| ROF returned matches the ROF sent | P4 §4.4 |
| Alert **`900`** absent | unknown ROF, non-blocking but wrong |
| Payment reference appears on the client's bank statement | the SEPA leg completed (P4 §4.6) |
| Deposit visible in the client's espace professionnel | independent confirmation |
| Return latency within **2 working days** | the only published SLA (P3 §3.6) |

And once per year, before the December palier: re-pull `PAYE26D`, `PAYE26F`, the form
specification zip and Volume III-C, and re-run the T-series. Millésime changes are **not**
backward-compatible — the 2501 lost a field in 2022 and the 2502 lost its whole CITS block
(P4 §4.5, §4.8.4). → P7 §7.7.


### DGFIP-EDI / `P7-run-operate.md`

#### P7 — Run & Operate
**FR · DGFIP-EDI · DGFiP — taxe sur les salaires via the EDI channel**

Status: **BUILD-READY** · SLA and rate limits unpublished (§7.5)
Source basis: Volume II §2.5–2.7, Volume III-C §3.1.3, §3.2.2, Volume IV §4.1.2, §4.5,
A12/A13/A14 (transport operating rules), and P1–P6.

---

##### 7.1 The schedule is derived, not fixed

Unlike every other integration in the FR dossier, **this one has no cadence of its own**. What to
send, and whether to send at all, is derived per client per year.

```
for each client, each year:

  ── liability gate ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  Is the client TS-liable this year?               ← explicit config, P1 §1.3
     no  → SUPPRESS. File nothing. Not a nil return.
     yes ↓
  Is estimated annual tax above the franchise?
     no  → SUPPRESS.
     yes ↓
  ── rhythm derivation ───────────────────────────────────────────
  Look up LAST year's tax:
     < 4 000        → annual   : 2502-SD only
     4 000–10 000   → quarterly: 2501-SD ×3  (Apr/Jul/Oct) + 2502-SD
     > 10 000       → monthly  : 2501-SD ×11 (Feb–Dec)     + 2502-SD
  ── event override ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  Cession / cessation during the year?
     yes → 2502-SD within 60 days of the event; no further 2501-SD
```

Four properties of this schedule that must be built in, not bolted on:

1. **The rhythm is set by the *previous* year's tax.** It changes at a year boundary, and a
   client crossing €10,000 moves from three deposits a year to twelve. Recompute annually, in
   January, before the first échéance.
2. **January is never an instalment** (P1 §1.2). Eleven 2501-SDs, not twelve.
3. **The suppression decision is the most common correct outcome** and must be *recorded*, not
   silent. An audit trail saying "not liable, suppressed, decided by X on Y" is the deliverable
   for most clients.
4. **The annual 2502-SD is the closing act of a rhythm**, not a separate obligation: the December
   (or Q4) tax is settled by it, which is why there is no December instalment either.

###### Échéance calendar

| Form | Rhythm | Échéance (`CCYYMM`, P4 §4.7) |
|------|--------|------------------------------|
| 2501-SD | monthly | 15 February … 15 December |
| 2501-SD | quarterly | 15 April, 15 July, 15 October |
| 2502-SD | all | **31 January** following the taxed year *(15 January for annual filers per impots.gouv.fr — unresolved, P1 §1.8)* |
| 2502-SD | cession/cessation | event date + 60 days |

Send **at échéance minus at least 5 working days** (P6 §6.5): there is no grace period, no
tolerance for technical rejection, and the return SLA alone is 2 working days.

##### 7.2 Jobs

| Job | Trigger | Does |
|-----|---------|------|
| **J1 — Annual rhythm review** | January, before the first échéance | Recompute liability and rhythm per client from last year's tax; record suppressions with an owner |
| **J2 — Deposit build & send** | échéance − 5 working days | Select due clients; build `P-IDENTIF` + form (+ payment); run the 35-assertion pre-flight gate (P4 §4.10); sign; transmit; record the `226 Transfer complete` timestamp |
| **J3 — Return retrieval** | Daily | One session, n files (P3 §3.4); persist before parsing; verify `AUTACK`; dispatch by message type; update per-deposit state (P5 §5.5) |
| **J4 — Reconciliation & escalation** | Daily | Apply the §5.4 recovery matrix; flag `Rejet TD`-family outcomes; chase deposits with no `INFENT CR` after 2 working days |
| **J5 — DLS watch** | Per échéance | Mark deposits final once the DLS passes; block further automated sends for that (SIREN, ROF, échéance) |
| **J6 — Palier refresh** | Annually, before December | Re-pull `PAYE26D`/`PAYE26F`, the form-spec zip and Volume III-C; re-run the P6 T-series (§7.7) |

**Batch sizing.** A level-1, level-2 or service-segment level-3 fault rejects the **whole
interchange** (P5 §5.2) — one bad message can take down fifty good ones. Near a deadline, prefer
**several small interchanges** over one large one. Away from a deadline, batch freely: the
PassTrans limit is 4 GB and DGFiP explicitly asks partners not to open one session per file
(P3 §3.4).

##### 7.3 Deposit lifecycle

```
 BUILT ─→ GATED ─→ SIGNED ─→ SENT ─→ RECEIVED ─→ SYNTAX-OK ─→ ADJUDICATED
                                (226)     (RCS)   (no CONTRL)     (INFENT CR)
                                                                    │
                    ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
                    ▼                                               ▼
              code bilan VT                              code bilan ≠ VT
                    │                                               │
          ┌─────────┴──────────┐                         apply §5.4 matrix
          ▼                    ▼                         (initiale vs rectificative
   before DLS: PROVISIONAL   after DLS: FINAL             depends on the DLS)
          │
   a later deposit demotes this one to INITIAL-RECTIFIÉ
```

**Keep the chain, not the latest state.** Any deposit accepted before the DLS can be retroactively
annulled by a later one (P4 §4.9). "Filed" is only final once the DLS has passed.

**Retry rules:**

| Class | Retry | Notes |
|-------|-------|-------|
| Transfer failure / uncertainty | **Yes, automatically** — retransmit (Vol II §2.5.2) | Watch filename uniqueness: overwrite is forbidden |
| Level 1 / 2 rejection | Yes, after correction — **whole interchange** | |
| Level 3, service segments | Yes — **whole interchange** | |
| Level 3, one message | Yes — **that message only** | |
| Level 4 (`RT`, `TD`, `TP`, `TPp`, `TD/TPp`, `TD/TP`) | **Not automatically.** Content is wrong; the *code nature* of the resend depends on the DLS | Human decision |
| After the DLS | **Never automatically** | No cancel-and-replace exists |

> **Blind retry is unsafe on this channel.** Re-sending before the DLS is a *substitution* that
> silently annuls the previous deposit — and if the resend omits payment data, it **cancels an
> accepted payment** (P4 §4.9). Gate every resend on a DLS-aware state machine.

Source payload must be **persisted**, not just the emitted file: recovery requires rebuilding
and re-signing a whole interchange (P3 §3.9).

##### 7.4 Failure modes this channel will actually produce

Ordered by how badly they mislead.

| # | Failure | Signal | Why it misleads |
|---|---------|--------|-----------------|
| 1 | **`Rejet TD` — declaration rejected, payment accepted** | code bilan `TD`, `RFL DEC` + `AFL TP` | Money moved; nothing declared. Bank statement agrees; a single success flag reports success. Surfaces months later as a mise en demeure. **Alert loudly** |
| 2 | **SEPA B2B mandate missing at the client's bank** | *Nothing on this channel.* Code bilan `VT`; the debit is refused by the bank | Entirely outside DGFiP's control chain. Detect by reconciling the payment reference (P4 §4.6) against the client's bank statement |
| 3 | **Deposit silently demoted to *initial rectifié*** | Disappears from the client's espace professionnel after the DLS | Correct behaviour that looks like data loss. Keep the chain; brief client-facing staff (P5 §5.8) |
| 4 | **Resend omitting payment cancels an accepted payment** | No error at all | The most destructive single mistake available. Guard in the state machine (§7.3) |
| 5 | **Whole interchange rejected for one bad message** | `INFENT RCS`/`CONTRL`, no `INFENT CR` for anyone | Fifty clients fail because of one. Mitigate by batch sizing (§7.2) |
| 6 | **Test file emitted as real** | Code bilan `VT`, taxpayer notified | No transport-level guard exists. Assertions 32–33 must fail closed (P4 §4.10) |
| 7 | **Millésime drift** | *Formulaire inconnu*, *Code inconditionné* — Rejet RT | The 2501 lost a field in 2022; the 2502 lost its CITS block. Refresh annually (§7.7) |
| 8 | **Return files lost to platform retention** | Returns simply absent | Files vanish on download or after ~21 days. Only recovery is the DGFiP rejeu (P5 §5.7) |
| 9 | **Filed for a non-liable client** | Rejection, or worse, acceptance of a filing that was never owed | The liability gate is configuration, not computation (§7.1) |
| 10 | **Accreditation compromise or key loss** | Level-2 rejection of everything | Recovery is another **face-to-face appointment in France** (P2 §2.4). Rehearse it |

##### 7.5 Alerting

**Alert:**

| Condition | Severity |
|-----------|----------|
| Code bilan `TD`, `TD/TP` or `TD/TPp` — **declaration rejected** | **Critical** — statutory obligation unmet |
| Code bilan `RT` | Critical |
| Code bilan `TP` or `TPp` — payment rejected | High |
| No `INFENT CR` more than **2 working days** after `226 Transfer complete` | High — the only published SLA breached |
| No `INFENT RCS` after a network transmission | Medium — RCS is systematic, so absence is anomalous |
| `AUTACK` signature verification failure on an inbound return | **Critical** — do not act on an unverified verdict |
| Alert code **`900`** (unknown ROF) on an otherwise accepted deposit | Medium — non-blocking but wrong |
| Pre-flight gate failure at build time | High — but *before* transmission, so recoverable |
| Payment reference not matched on the client's bank statement within the expected window | High — the invisible SEPA failure (§7.4 #2) |
| Échéance approaching with a due deposit not yet `VT` | **Critical from échéance − 2 working days** |

**Do not alert:**

| Condition | Why |
|-----------|-----|
| **No `CONTRL` received** | `CONTRL` is emitted **only on error** (P5 §5.1). Its absence is the level-3 pass signal |
| Files in the withdrawal directory belonging to other teleprocedures | The directory is shared across TVA/TDFC/IS (P3 §3.4) |
| A client suppressed for non-liability producing no deposit | That is the correct outcome for most clients (§7.1) |
| A deposit demoted to *initial rectifié* by a later deposit before the DLS | Documented substitution behaviour (P4 §4.9) |
| A form-only deposit with no payment | Permitted (P4 §4.6) |
| No December or January 2501-SD | Neither exists (§7.1) |

**Logs are an obligation, not a convenience.** DGFiP requires partners to be able to supply
transfer logs and the associated commands on request, and states that without them incident
analysis may be impossible (A12 §3). Retain transfer logs with the same rigour as the evidence
store.

##### 7.6 Archival and retention

Two stores, for two different reasons (P5 §5.7):

| Store | Contents | Retention | Basis |
|-------|----------|-----------|-------|
| **Evidence** | `INFENT CR`, `INFENT RCS`, `CONTRL`, their `AUTACK` signatures, the `226` timestamp, code bilan, `DEC`/`TP` lot verdicts, ROF, payment reference | Long — mirror DGFiP's **6 years** so a rejeu can be answered | Vol II §2.7.3.1–2 |
| **Transient** | The emitted `INFENT DP` payload and source data | Only as long as needed for transmission, confirmation and the re-emission window | Vol II §2.4.4.4 — retain "only as long as necessary" |
| **Transfer logs** | Session logs and commands | Long enough to answer an ESI incident query | A12 §3 |

Also standing (P2 §2.7): CNIL/data-protection registration; confidentiality — data may not be
disclosed to anyone but another contracted partenaire EDI or DGFiP without **express taxpayer
authorisation**; clients must be notified of subcontracting, and on cessation of activity or loss
of accreditation. **Build the client evidence-export before go-live** — it is what a departing
client will ask for.

**Key custody.** The private key is a 2048-bit RSA key in a password-protected PEM (P2 §2.4).
There is no online revocation, no rotation schedule, and recovery from compromise means
regenerating the pair and **attending another appointment in France**. Hold it in an HSM or
equivalent, restrict access, and write the loss/compromise procedure down before it is needed —
noting that one accreditation covers **all** DGFiP teleprocedures the partner holds, so the blast
radius is the whole relationship, not just TS.

##### 7.7 Change management

| Watch | Where | Cadence |
|-------|-------|---------|
| **TS palier / millésime** | impots.gouv.fr *Cahiers des charges EDI* + **EDIFICAS** document library | **Annually, before December** — the TS palier ships in December for the *following* millésime label |
| `PAYE26D` / `PAYE26F` structure tables | EDIFICAS autotest extract | Same |
| Form specification zip (2501, 2502, P-IDENTIF) | impots.gouv.fr EDI-Paiement page | Same |
| Error-code list | Volume III-C §3.6 and Volume III tableau 2 | Same — codes are created and suppressed per palier (P5 §5.6) |
| Transport prerequisites | A12/A13 (PassTrans) — reissued 04/06/2026 | On change; algorithms tighten over time and *"vos transferts seront bloqués"* if stale |
| Securing scheme | A15/A16 packs — currently ship **both** old and new return verifiers | On change |
| Convention renewal | Tacit annual reconduction; notify the regional correspondent of any relocation | Annually |
| EDIFICAS attestation | Reference must stay valid in `RFF+AUO` | On each software release |

> **Millésime changes are not backward-compatible.** Concrete precedents from the sources in
> hand: the 2501 **lost** line 03 (`AD/MOA`, NREF `909060`) at millésime 2022 and renumbered line
> 04; the 2502 **lost its entire CITS block** (`HE`, `HD`, `JD`) and coherence control `411`; the
> abattement figure moved between millésimes. `PAYE26D` for 2026 confirms all of these
> removals. Treat the structure tables as **versioned configuration refreshed annually**, never
> as constants (P4 §4.11).

##### 7.8 Per-échéance runbook

1. **T−10 working days** — J1 output confirmed: liability, rhythm and échéance list for the
   period. Suppressions recorded with an owner.
2. **T−5 working days** — J2 builds deposits. Pre-flight gate must pass **all 35 assertions**; a
   failure here is cheap, a failure at DGFiP is not.
3. **T−5** — sign, transmit, record the `226 Transfer complete` timestamp as the filing date of
   record. Prefer several small interchanges to one large one.
4. **T−4** — expect `INFENT RCS`. Absence is anomalous; investigate before assuming success.
5. **T−3** — no `CONTRL` means level-3 passed. Do not wait for one.
6. **T−3 to T−2** — `INFENT CR` arrives. Record code bilan **and** the `DEC`/`TP` lot verdicts
   separately. Capture the ROF.
7. **If not `VT`** — apply the §5.4 recovery matrix. Determine whether the DLS has passed; that
   decides *initiale* vs *rectificative*, and whether payment data must be re-included.
   Re-send by **T−1** at the latest.
8. **If still failing at T−1** — file on the **espace professionnel (EFI)**. Lawful, expressly
   permitted, and better than a missed deadline. Record the channel switch.
9. **T+3 to T+5** — cross-check in the client's espace professionnel; confirm the payment
   reference on the client's bank statement.
10. **At the DLS** — J5 marks the deposit final and blocks further automated sends for that
    (SIREN, ROF, échéance).


### DGFIP-EDI / `INTERLINKAGES.md`

#### INTERLINKAGES
**FR · DGFIP-EDI ↔ NETENT-DSN, URSSAF-DPAE, and the FR dossier artefacts**

This file is the **only** place in the DGFIP-EDI runbook set where other authorities are
discussed. P1–P7 are scoped to `DGFIP-EDI` alone. Nothing here is a build instruction for
another authority — each has its own runbook.

---

##### 1. Authority map

| Authority | Channel | Filings | Runbook |
|-----------|---------|---------|---------|
| `NETENT-DSN` | REST API, net-entreprises.fr (GIP-MDS) | 10 | Separate — `../NETENT-DSN/` |
| `URSSAF-DPAE` | REST API, `depot.dpae-edi.urssaf.fr` | 1 — `FR-URSSAF-001` | Separate — `../URSSAF-DPAE/` |
| **`DGFIP-EDI`** | **EDIFACT batch via an accredited partenaire EDI** | 1 — `FR-DGFIP-003` | **This set** |

Three channels, three enrolments, three failure modes — and this one is **the odd one out in
every dimension**:

| | NETENT-DSN | URSSAF-DPAE | **DGFIP-EDI** |
|---|---|---|---|
| Paradigm | REST/JSON-ish over HTTPS | REST over HTTPS | **Batch EDIFACT file exchange** |
| Access | Charte / registration | Email registration | **Accreditation + a physical appointment in France** |
| Credential | Portal credentials → token | Portal credentials → token | **DGFiP-issued RSA-2048 accreditation on a USB key** |
| Interaction | Synchronous request/response | Synchronous request/response | **Deposit a file, poll a directory, wait up to 2 working days** |
| Applies to | Every client | Every client that hires | **Only the minority of clients not VAT-liable** |
| Carries money | Withholding remitted from the DSN | No | **Yes — SEPA B2B direct debit inside the payload** |

**Nothing technical is reusable between DGFIP-EDI and the other two.** No shared HTTP client, no
shared auth, no shared payload writer, no shared return parser. Plan it as a standalone build.

---

##### 2. DGFIP-EDI ↔ NETENT-DSN — same authority, different obligation

This is the interlinkage most likely to cause a scoping error, because **DGFiP appears on both
sides**.

| | Withholding at source (PAS) | **Taxe sur les salaires** |
|---|---|---|
| Filings | `FR-DGFIP-001` (outbound), `FR-DGFIP-002` (inbound rate) | **`FR-DGFIP-003`** |
| Legal character | Tax withheld from the *employee* and remitted | A tax **on the employer**, based on payroll |
| Channel | **Inside the monthly DSN**, via net-entreprises.fr | **EDI-Paiement**, direct to DGFiP |
| Runbook | `../NETENT-DSN/` | This set |
| Collection | Direct debit initiated from the DSN | **SEPA B2B initiated from this payload** (P4 §4.6) |
| Population | Every employee | Only TS-liable employers (P1 §1.3) |

The landscape entry states it correctly: *"DGFiP's withholding-at-source obligation travels
inside the DSN and is covered by NETENT-DSN"*. **Two DGFiP obligations, two channels, zero shared
code.** A build that consolidates "DGFiP integration" into one component is wrong at the first
design review.

One genuine, non-technical link: **the DSN is the source of payroll fact** for the amounts that
end up on the 2502-SD grid. That is a data-lineage relationship handled in the Data Dictionary,
not a runtime dependency between the two integrations. Neither channel calls the other.

##### 3. DGFIP-EDI ↔ URSSAF-DPAE

**No functional relationship.** The DPAE is a pre-hire declaration to a social-security collector;
the TS is a tax return to the fiscal administration. Different bodies, different law, different
lifecycle, no shared identifier beyond the employer's SIREN.

One useful contrast for design reviews — the two channels fail in **opposite** ways:

| | URSSAF-DPAE | DGFIP-EDI |
|---|---|---|
| Deadline recoverable? | **No.** A DPAE filed after the employee starts is a breach nothing repairs | **Partly.** Before the DLS, a later deposit substitutes for the earlier one (P4 §4.9) |
| Correction model | **None** — a registered DPAE cannot be amended | **Destructive substitution** — the later deposit annuls the earlier |
| Test environment | None; test is a flag on production (`C_VERSION = TST`) | None; test is a flag on production (`UNB/0035=1` + `UNG/0040=DP3`) — but **all four control levels still run**, so it is materially more useful (P6 §6.1) |
| The dangerous success | HTTP 200 with no `R_DOSSIER` | **Code bilan `Rejet TD`** — payment accepted, declaration rejected (P5 §5.4) |

Both channels share one architectural lesson, and it is worth stating once for the whole FR
dossier: **the transport-level success signal is never the filing.** On the DPAE it is the AR
carrying `R_DOSSIER`; here it is the `INFENT CR` carrying code bilan `VT`.

##### 4. Shared enrolment? — no

| | Grants access to DGFIP-EDI? |
|---|---|
| net-entreprises.fr registration (DSN) | **No** |
| URSSAF-en-ligne subscriber account | **No** |
| Urssaf concentrateur registration | **No** |
| An `impots.gouv.fr` espace professionnel | **No** for the EDI channel — but **required of the client** for the payment leg (P2 §2.2) |

Conversely, the DGFiP **partenaire EDI habilitation is itself broad**: one habilitation covers
EDI-TDFC, EDI-Requête, EDI-TVA **and** EDI-PAIEMENT together, and one accreditation secures all
of them (P2 §2.3, §2.4). So the accreditation obtained for TS would also open EDI-TVA and
EDI-TDFC if Mercans ever needed them — and, symmetrically, a compromise of that one key affects
all of them. **EDI-IR is excluded** and needs a separate convention.

##### 5. Where this runbook stops and the rest of the dossier begins

| Question | Owner | Not this runbook because |
|----------|-------|--------------------------|
| Is this client TS-liable? | **CCG** / client configuration | A legal determination about the client's VAT position, not an integration behaviour. P7 §7.1 consumes it as a gate |
| What are the TS rates, bands, franchise, décote and abattement figures? | **CCG** | P4 quotes thresholds only to *identify a field*, never as authority for the figure (P4 preamble, §4.11) |
| Which wage types enter the TS base? | **WTC** | The 2502-SD grid's sector columns and rate bands consume the base; they do not define it |
| Where does each 2502-SD line's value come from in the payroll data? | **Data Dictionary** | P4 §4.8 fixes *where the value goes*, not where it comes from |
| Does the TS appear on the payslip? | **Payslip spec** | It is an employer tax, not an employee deduction |
| What is the filing's identity in the dossier? | **`scope_manifest.json`** — `FR-DGFIP-003` | P1 §1.7 records two refinements to that record (format and direction) |

###### Corrections this runbook raises against dossier artefacts

Three, all evidenced, none of them scope changes:

| Artefact | Record | Correction | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scope_manifest.json` | `FR-DGFIP-003` format `XML` | **UN/EDIFACT** (`INFENT DP`, D00B). No XML representation exists on this channel | Vol III-C §3.1.0, Vol IV §4.1.1 |
| `scope_manifest.json` | `FR-DGFIP-003` direction `outbound` | **Both.** Three inbound message types carry the only proof the filing exists | Vol IV §4.1.1, Vol II §2.7.2.3 |
| `integration-landscape.json` | *"EDI-TDFC: transmission of the annual liquidation return 2502-SD"* | **Both 2501-SD and 2502-SD ride EDI-Paiement**, not EDI-TDFC. The only documents admissible in a TS interchange are `P-IDENTIF`, `2501`, `2502` | Vol III-C §3.2.1 |

The landscape's substantive judgements — `integration: true`, the accredited-partner monopoly,
the conditional-filing constraint, the schedule-driven rhythm — are **confirmed**. Its open
question **FR-SI-Q5** ("a 2026 edition must be confirmed before any field-level work") is now
**closed**: the millésime-2026 palier exists, and its structure tables are in
`integration-sources/dgfip-edi/` (P4 §4.5, §4.8).

##### 6. Sequencing across the three FR integrations

| Order | Rationale |
|-------|-----------|
| **1. NETENT-DSN** | Every client, every month; largest payload; and it carries the PAS obligation |
| **2. URSSAF-DPAE** | Every client that hires; reuses roughly the DSN's transport half; hardest deadline |
| **3. DGFIP-EDI** | **Smallest population, largest access lead time** |

**But start DGFIP-EDI's *enrolment* first, on day one, regardless of build order.** Its critical
path is not code — it is a habilitation dossier, a decision by a regional DRFiP correspondent, a
face-to-face appointment in France, and an EDIFICAS conformity attestation (P2 §2.3–2.5). None of
that can be compressed by engineering, none of it overlaps with the other two authorities, and
none of it has a published SLA. Everything else about this integration can be built against the
specification while it runs. → `effort-estimates.md` §1.


### DGFIP-EDI / `effort-estimates.md`

#### Effort Estimates
**FR · DGFIP-EDI · DGFiP — taxe sur les salaires (2501-SD / 2502-SD) via the EDI channel**

Basis: the specification set actually in hand (source manifest A1–A24). Estimates assume
**Model A — Mercans becomes a partenaire EDI** (P2 §2.1), which is the only model with an
integration to build. Model B and the EFI fallback are costed in §4.

**Unit:** person-days of a developer fluent in integration work but **new to EDIFACT and new to
DGFiP**. They exclude payroll-engine work — sourcing the correct *amounts* for the forms is CCG /
WTC / Data-Dictionary scope, not integration scope (`INTERLINKAGES.md` §5).

**No reuse credit is available.** Unlike URSSAF-DPAE after NETENT-DSN, this integration shares
nothing with the other two FR authorities: different paradigm, different transport, different
credential, different payload family (`INTERLINKAGES.md` §1). A single figure is therefore given
throughout.

> **Confidence.** Build effort is estimated with **high** confidence — the interface is fully
> specified, the specification is in hand, the payload is confirmed **millésime-2026-current**
